:::

### [Requirements for Holders of Magazine Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797075 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities of licence holder

**[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a magazine
licence must ensure that the requirements of sections 147 to 160 are met
and that the people referred to in section 161 are aware of their
obligations under that section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acceptable distance requirement

- **[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every magazine must be located at an
  acceptable distance from surrounding structures, infrastructure and
  places where people are likely to be present.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria --- acceptable distance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a vendor magazine licence and a user
  magazine licence, acceptable distance is determined by the Minister on
  the basis of risk of harm to people or property, taking into account
  the quantity and type of explosives to be stored in the magazine, the
  strength, proximity and use of surrounding structures and
  infrastructure and the number of people likely to be in the vicinity
  of the magazine at any one time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criterion --- user magazine zone
  licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a user magazine zone licence,
  acceptable distance is the minimum distance in metres to be maintained
  between each magazine at the site and each vulnerable place shown on
  the site plan, as set out in the National Standard of Canada Standard
  CAN/BNQ 2910--510/2015 entitled Explosives --- Quantity Distances as
  amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 38, 40
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400366){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400366.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-147-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 147"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Structural requirements

**[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** Every magazine must be
constructed and maintained so that it is well-ventilated and resistant
to theft, weather and fire. A magazine for the storage of explosives
that are classified as hazard category PE 1 must also be
bullet-resistant, unless the magazine licence specifies otherwise.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400370){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400370.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-148-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 148"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized storage

- **[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  explosive may be stored in a magazine only if the licence authorizes
  the storage of that explosive in that magazine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other materials and equipment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Materials and equipment may be brought into or stored
  in a magazine only if they are required for operations, including
  handling explosives, in the magazine and they do not increase the
  likelihood of an ignition.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400374){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400374.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-149-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 149"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stacking

- **[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Packages and containers of
  explosives must be stacked so that they will not fall over, collapse
  or be deformed, torn or crushed. They must not be stacked higher than
  the stacking line for the magazine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited use of packages

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Packages and containers of explosives must not be
  used as supports for conveyors or ramps.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Air circulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} There must be enough space between the stacks of
  explosives, the walls, the ceiling and the ventilation openings to
  permit air circulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opening packages

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Packages or containers that are made from wood or
  have metal fasteners or strapping must not be opened in a magazine.
  Other packages or containers may be opened in a magazine for
  inspection or to remove explosives or to add an explosive of the same
  product name, but not more than two packages or containers may be
  opened at a time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opened packages

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any package or container of explosives that has been
  opened outside a magazine must be dry, clean and free of grit and
  other contamination before it is taken into the magazine.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400378){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400378.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 38](#1451470-1452427){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451470-1452427.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-150-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 150"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fire prevention

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Precautions must be taken that
  minimize the likelihood of fire in or near a magazine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No smoking

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Smoking must be prohibited in a magazine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Thunderstorms

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the approach of a thunderstorm, all people in a
  magazine must be immediately moved to a safe place and until the storm
  passes must not be permitted to return.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited activities

**[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** The following
activities must not be carried out inside a magazine unless the magazine
licence authorizes them to be carried out there:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} packing or repacking explosives;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} adding a detonator to, or inserting a detonator in,
  an explosive;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} assembling explosive components;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} uncoiling the leg wires of, or removing the shunt
  from, an electric detonator or an electric initiator;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} opening a package or container of explosives to
  expose an explosive substance; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} stripping, cutting or slitting the wrapping of an
  explosive article to expose an explosive substance.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400384){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400384.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 39(F)](#1451477-1452428){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451477-1452428.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 152"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlocked magazine

- **[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  magazine must be attended in person when it is unlocked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Key control plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A key control plan that includes the following
  requirements must be put in place for each magazine:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} every key to the magazine must be numbered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person may have possession of a key to the
    magazine only if they are named in the plan;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number of people named in the plan must not
    exceed the number necessary for the operation of the magazine;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} each key must be kept in a locked and secure
    location when it is not in the possession of a person named in the
    plan; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an industrial explosive or a type D
    explosive, the lock on the magazine must be of a type for which keys
    can be obtained only from the lock's manufacturer or a certified
    locksmith designated by the manufacturer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of circumstances

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The plan must be updated to reflect any change in
  circumstances that could adversely affect the security of the magazine
  site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lost or stolen key

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a key is lost or stolen, the lock must be
  immediately replaced.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 14
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400388){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400388.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 153"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fire safety plan

- **[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  copy of the fire safety plan included in the licence application must
  be sent to the local fire department and made available to employees.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of circumstances

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The plan must be updated to reflect any change in
  circumstances that could adversely affect the safety of the site. A
  copy of the updated plan must be sent to the local fire department as
  soon as the circumstances permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Site security plan

- **[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  site security plan is included in the licence application, it must be
  implemented.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of circumstances

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The plan must be updated to reflect any change in
  circumstances that could adversely affect the security of the magazine
  site. A copy of the updated plan must be sent to the Chief Inspector
  of Explosives as soon as the circumstances permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of plan

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A copy of the most recent version of the plan must be
  made available to the people who are responsible for implementing it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage record

**[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** A record for each
magazine must be kept for two years after the date on which it is made.
The record must include

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} each type of explosive that is stored;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of each type of explosive that is
  stored; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the dates on which each explosive was placed in and
  removed from the magazine.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400392){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400392.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-156-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 156"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintenance of magazine

- **[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  magazine must be kept clean, dry and organized. Any spill, leakage or
  other contamination must be cleaned up immediately.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Combustible material

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A magazine must be kept free of grit, combustible or
  abrasive material, any fire-producing, spark-producing or
  flame-producing device and any substance that might spontaneously
  combust.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lighting, electrical fixtures and
  wiring

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The lighting (including portable lighting),
  electrical fixtures and wiring systems that are used in a magazine
  must not increase the likelihood of an ignition. All portable lighting
  must be impact-resistant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Repairs to magazine

- **[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before any repair work that could
  increase the likelihood of an ignition begins in or on a magazine, the
  explosives in the magazine must be either

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} put into another magazine; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} taken to a location where the presence of the
    explosives will not increase the likelihood of harm to people or
    property, the explosives are protected from weather and the repair
    work will not cause an ignition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attendance of explosives

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any explosives that are not put into another magazine
  must be attended in person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Putting explosives back

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Explosives must not be returned to a magazine until
  the repairs to the magazine no longer increase the likelihood of an
  ignition.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 15
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400397){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400397.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 158"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interior sign

**[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159 .sectionLabel}** A sign that indicates
the type of explosives, and the maximum quantity of each type, that may
be stored in the magazine, as specified in the licence, must be posted
inside every magazine in a clearly visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400401){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400401.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-159-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 159"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deteriorated explosives

- **[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  explosives in a magazine must be checked regularly for signs of
  deterioration and to ensure that the manufacturer's expiry date has
  not passed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marking

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every deteriorated, expired or misfired explosive
  must be clearly marked "Deteriorated/[Détérioré]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}" or "Expired/[Périmé]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or
  "Misfired/[Raté]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of deteriorated, expired or
  misfired explosives

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Explosives that have deteriorated, expired or
  misfired must be safely destroyed as soon as the circumstances permit.
  However, explosives that have deteriorated to the extent that they are
  unstable or in a very dangerous condition must be destroyed
  immediately in a manner that does not increase the likelihood of an
  accidental ignition during or after the destruction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization required

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not authorize a person to destroy
  deteriorated, expired or misfired explosives. The destruction must be
  authorized by these Regulations or otherwise under the [Explosives
  Act](/eng/acts/E-17).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage until destruction --- normal
  hazard

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A deteriorated, expired or misfired explosive may be
  stored in a magazine with other compatible explosives if the storage
  would not increase the likelihood of an ignition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage until destruction --- more than
  normal hazard

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A deteriorated, expired or misfired explosive must be
  stored in a magazine that does not contain other explosives if storing
  it with other explosives could increase the likelihood of an ignition.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400407){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400407.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 160"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person in possession of key

**[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** A person who is in
possession of a key that is subject to a key control plan referred to in
subsection 153(2) must not duplicate the key. After using it, the person
must return the key to a locked location to which access is controlled.

### [Requirements for Holders of User Magazine Zone Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797188 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change of site

- **[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  the storage of explosives that is authorized by a user magazine zone
  licence is moved to a new site, within 24 hours after the move the
  holder of the licence must complete, sign and send a notice of change
  of site, in the form provided by the Department of Natural Resources,
  to the Minister, to the police force in the locality of the previous
  site and to the police force in the locality of the new site. The
  notice must be dated and include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone and cellphone number
    and email address of both the licence holder and a contact person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder's licence number and its expiry date;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and cellphone number of
    the person who is responsible for the new site;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the storage of explosives began
    at the new site;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the geographic coordinates of the previous and new
    sites;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the directions by road to the new site;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the safety and security features of the new site
    (for example, signs, alarm systems, barriers, fencing and berms);
    and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a list of the magazines to be used at the new site
    that sets out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a magazine that has been moved,
      its number, letter or distinctive name as shown on the previous
      site plan,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its tag number, if any,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable magazine type number, as set out
      in the National Standard of Canada Standard CAN/BNQ 2910--500/2015
      entitled Explosives --- Magazines for Industrial Explosives, as
      amended from time to time, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} each type of explosive, and the quantity of each
      type, to be stored.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Site plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must include a site plan that shows

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the location of each magazine and each vulnerable
    place at the site as well as the location of each vulnerable place
    outside the site that is exposed to the hazards (for example, debris
    or blast effect) that could result from ignition of the explosives
    to be stored;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the distance in metres between each magazine at the
    site, between each magazine and each vulnerable place at the site as
    well as between each magazine at the site and each vulnerable place
    outside the site that is exposed to the hazards (for example, debris
    or blast effect) that could result from ignition of the explosives
    to be stored at the site; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the minimum distance in metres to be maintained
    between each magazine at the site and each vulnerable place shown on
    the site plan, as set out in the National Standard of Canada
    Standard CAN/BNQ 2910--510/2015 entitled Explosives --- Quantity
    Distances, as amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 16, 41, 42(E), 43(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400411){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400411.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 40](#1451479-1452430){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451479-1452430.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-162-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 162"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of licence and notice

**[[163]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-163 .sectionLabel}** The holder must ensure
that a copy of the user magazine zone licence and of the notice are
posted in each magazine.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 17(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-163-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 163"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Provisions of General Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-797215 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[164]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164 .sectionLabel}** This Part sets out
certain terms and conditions that apply to holders of the documents
(licences, permits and certificates) issued by the Minister under
section 7 of the [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17) and the grounds upon
which the Minister may refuse to issue them. It also sets out the
procedures for changing or renewing those documents and provides for
their suspension and cancellation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 41](#1451481-1452433){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451481-1452433.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-164-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 164"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Refusal to Issue]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1452434 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal of Minister

- **[[164.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by written notice
  giving reasons, refuse to issue a licence, permit or certificate if
  the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that issuing the
  licence, permit or certificate would constitute a risk to the safety
  or security of persons or property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant may, within 30 days after the day on
  which they receive a notice of refusal from the Minister, send the
  Minister a written request for a review of the refusal on the grounds
  that the information on which the refusal was based is incorrect,
  accompanied by any additional documents or any other information in
  writing that is necessary to support the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision --- request for review

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} After reviewing the request under subsection (2), the
  Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} issue the licence, permit or certificate, if the
    information on which the refusal was based is incorrect; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} give the applicant written notice of the refusal,
    and the reasons for the refusal, if the information on which the
    refusal was based is correct.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 41](#1451481-1452445){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451481-1452445.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Terms and Conditions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797220 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities

- **[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, permit or certificate may carry out any activity
  involving an explosive that is authorized by the document and must do
  so in the manner specified in it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility of holder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must ensure that their employees and other
  workers carry out the activities authorized by the document in the
  manner specified in it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400415){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400415.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-165-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 165"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of licence, permit or
certificate

**[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence,
permit or certificate must present their document for review at the
request of a peace officer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fire

- **[[167]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, permit or certificate must immediately inform the
  local fire department of any fire that involves an explosive under
  their control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incidents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence, permit or certificate must
  inform an inspector as soon as the circumstances permit of any of the
  following incidents that involves an explosive under their control:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the theft, attempted theft or loss of an explosive;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a fire, spill or accidental explosion;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an injury or death; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any accidental property damage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder must provide the Chief Inspector of
  Explosives with a detailed follow-up report about the incident as soon
  as the circumstances permit. The report must include the likely cause
  of the incident and the steps that the holder will take to prevent
  such an incident from happening again.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400419){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400419.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-167-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 167"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of explosives

**[[168]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence,
permit or certificate who has not applied to renew the document or
obtain a new document must ensure that on or before the expiry date of
their document the explosives under their control for which a licence,
permit or certificate is required

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} are destroyed in a manner that does not increase the
  likelihood of an accidental ignition during or after the destruction;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} are returned to the person from whom they were
  bought; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} are delivered to a holder of a factory licence that
  authorizes storage of those explosives.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 16
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400422){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400422.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-168-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 168"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decommissioning plan --- factory

- **[[169]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-169
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before decommissioning a factory, a
  holder of a division 1 factory licence must send a written
  decommissioning plan to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decommissioning plan --- magazine

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before decommissioning a workplace or magazine, a
  holder of a division 2 factory licence, a magazine licence or a
  manufacturing certificate must send a written decomissioning plan to
  the Minister if the workplace or magazine contains any explosive
  residue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of plan

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A decommissioning plan must include a description of
  the safety measures that the holder will take to minimize the
  likelihood of harm to people or property during and after the
  decommissioning.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional safety measures

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may require the holder to implement
  additional safety measures that are necessary to minimize the
  likelihood of harm to people or property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility of holder

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The holder must implement the decommissioning plan
  and ensure that the factory, workplace or magazine is decommissioned
  in a safe manner and that any possibility of harm to people or
  property is eliminated after the factory or magazine is
  decommissioned. They must inform the Minister when the decommissioning
  is complete.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 38, 44(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400426){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400426.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-169-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 169"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[170]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2024-77, s. 42\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-170-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 170"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of activity

**[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171 .sectionLabel}** A licence holder who
intends to suspend an activity for which their licence was issued must,
no later than 14 days before the date on which the suspension is to
begin, give the Minister written notice of the date of suspension and
the anticipated date, if any, for resuming the activity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-171-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 171"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Amendment and Renewal]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797277 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment or renewal with amendment

- **[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for an amendment of a licence, permit or certificate, or for
  a renewal with amendment, must complete, sign and send to the Chief
  Inspector of Explosives the application form provided by the
  Department of Natural Resources. The application must state whether
  the amendment requested is to a licence, a permit or a certificate and
  must include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and email
    address of both the applicant and a contact person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of the applicant's licence, permit or
    certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amendment requested; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all the information that differs from that provided
    in the previous application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal without amendment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a renewal without amendment of a
  licence, permit or certificate must complete, sign and send to the
  Chief Inspector of Explosives the application form provided by the
  Department of Natural Resources. The application must include the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and email
    address of both the applicant and a contact person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of the applicant's licence, permit or
    certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), a person who applies
  for the renewal of a manufacturing certificate must comply with
  section 109.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An applicant must pay the applicable fees set out in
  Part 19.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 43](#1451484-1452450){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451484-1452450.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-172-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 172"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Suspension and Cancellation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797293 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

- **[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Chief Inspector of Explosives may suspend a licence, permit or
  certificate, in whole or in part, if the holder fails to comply with
  the [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17), these Regulations or any term or
  condition of the document. The suspension continues until the measures
  required to bring the holder into compliance are taken.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Inspector may cancel a licence, permit or
  certificate if the holder

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fails more than one time to comply with the
    [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17), these Regulations or any term or
    condition of the document; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} jeopardizes the safety of the public or the
    holder's employees by failing to follow the practices of the
    explosives industry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before suspending or cancelling a licence, permit or
  certificate, the Chief Inspector must provide the holder with written
  notice of the reasons for the suspension or cancellation and its
  effective date and give them an opportunity to provide reasons why the
  licence, permit or certificate should not be suspended or cancelled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review by Minister

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder may request that the Minister review the
  Chief Inspector's decision to suspend or cancel. The request must be
  made in writing within 15 days after the Chief Inspector has given the
  holder notice of the decision. The Minister must confirm, revoke or
  amend the decision.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400433){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400433.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-173-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 173"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation by Minister

- **[[173.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by written notice
  giving reasons, cancel a licence, permit or certificate if the
  Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that the holder of the
  licence, permit or certificate no longer meets the conditions of the
  approval letter issued by the Minister under section 183 or equivalent
  document referred to in Part 8 or if the Minister has reasonable
  grounds to believe that the activities subject to the licence, permit
  or certificate would constitute a risk to the safety or security of
  persons or property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence, permit or certificate may,
  within 30 days after the day on which they receive a notice of
  cancellation from the Minister, send the Minister a written request
  for a review of the cancellation on the grounds that the information
  on which the cancellation was based is incorrect, accompanied by any
  additional documents or any other information in writing that is
  necessary to support the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision --- request for review

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} After reviewing the request under subsection (2), the
  Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} reissue the licence, permit or certificate, if the
    information on which the cancellation was based is incorrect; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} give the holder written notice of the cancellation,
    and the reasons for the cancellation, if the information on which
    the cancellation was based is correct.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 44](#1451489-1452461){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451489-1452461.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Screening]{.HTitleText1} {#h-797307 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174 .sectionLabel}** This Part sets out the
screening requirements for people who have access to high hazard
explosives. Division 1 sets out the requirements that must be met by
applicants for licences, permits or certificates if they intend to
manufacture, store, import or export high hazard explosives or transport
them in transit. Division 2 sets out the duties of licence, permit and
certificate holders to control access to high hazard explosives. It also
sets out the requirements for obtaining letters of approval.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 502
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400436){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400436.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-174-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 174"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[approval letter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[approval letter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an approval letter
      issued by the Minister under section 183. ([lettre
      d'approbation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a certificate that
      authorizes the manufacture or storage of a high hazard
      explosive. ([certificat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[equivalent document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[equivalent document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a security
      clearance, issued by a competent authority, that has not expired
      or been suspended or withdrawn, including

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a [permis général]{.otherLang lang="fr"} issued
        under Quebec's An Act respecting explosives, as amended from
        time to time;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a FAST card (free and secure trade card) issued
        by the Canada Border Services Agency;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a NEXUS card issued by the Canada Border
        Services Agency;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a Firearms Possession and Acquisition Licence
        issued under the [Firearms Act](/eng/acts/F-11.6);

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a security clearance issued by Public Services
        and Procurement Canada to individuals working for or on behalf
        of entities that are registered in its Contract Security
        Program; or

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an Employee Possessor Clearance issued by the
        U.S. Bureau of Alcohol, Tobacco, Firearms and Explosives if the
        employee holds a valid Canadian work permit. ([document
        équivalent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[high hazard explosive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[high hazard explosive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} refers to

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} military explosives or law enforcement
        explosives (type D);

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} high explosives (type E); or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} initiation systems (type I). ([explosif à
        risque élevé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes
      the storage of a high hazard
      explosive. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a permit that authorizes the
      importation, exportation or in transit transportation of a high
      hazard explosive. ([permis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person is considered to have access to a high
  hazard explosive if it is possible for them to come into contact, even
  momentary contact, with such an explosive.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 503
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
- SOR/2018-231, s. 18

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1401070.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 45](#1451491-1452466){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451491-1452466.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Application for Licence, Permit or Certificate]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797333 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2013-211, s. 507

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval letter or equivalent document

- **[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual who applies for a licence, permit or certificate or for the
  renewal of one must include with their application proof that they
  have an approval letter or equivalent document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of employees

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every applicant for a licence, permit or certificate,
  or for the renewal of one, must also include a list of their employees
  who are required by this Part to have an approval letter and must
  indicate whether the employee has applied for the letter and whether
  it has been received.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 507(E)
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 19, 45(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-176-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 176"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of document

**[[177]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-177 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must not
issue or renew the licence, permit or certificate unless the applicant
has an approval letter or equivalent document.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, ss. 507(E), 508(F)
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 20, 44(F)
- SOR/2018-231, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-177-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 177"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Approval Letters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797347 .Subheading}

#### [Requirements for Holders of a Licence, Permit or Certificate]{.HTitleText3} {#h-797348 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2013-211, s. 507

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval letter required

- **[[178]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-178 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, permit or certificate must ensure that an
  approval letter or equivalent document is held by every employee,
  director or contractor who, in carrying out their functions for the
  holder,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has access to a high hazard explosive;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} permits others to have access to a high hazard
    explosive; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} controls, directly or indirectly, a person who has
    access to a high hazard explosive or permits others to have access
    to such an explosive.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control over others

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence, permit or certificate must
  ensure that a person who does not have an approval letter or an
  equivalent document does not occupy a position in which they control,
  directly or indirectly, a person who, in carrying out their functions
  for the holder, has access to a high hazard explosive.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 507
- SOR/2016-75, s. 45(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-178-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 178"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access prevented

- **[[179]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-179 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, permit or certificate must ensure that a person
  who does not have an approval letter or an equivalent document does
  not have access to a high hazard explosive that is being manufactured,
  stored, sold, imported, exported or transported by the holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- supervised person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a person
  who does not hold an equivalent document, has applied for an approval
  letter and is still waiting for a response if, when they have access
  to a high hazard explosive, they are at all times under the direct
  supervision of another person who has an approval letter or equivalent
  document.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 504
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400439){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400439.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 46](#1451496-1452469){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451496-1452469.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-179-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 179"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Visitors

**[[180]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
or certificate must ensure that a visitor to their factory, magazine
site, satellite site or workplace who does not have an approval letter
and who could have access to a high hazard explosive is at all times
under the direct supervision of a person who has an approval letter or
an equivalent document.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 505
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-180-20150201.html "Link to previous version of section 180"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- peace officers, etc.

**[[181]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181 .sectionLabel}** Subsection 179(1) and
section 180 do not apply in respect of the following people when they
are acting in the course of their duties:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a peace officer;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an employee of the federal government; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an inspector appointed under the [Explosives
  Act](/eng/acts/E-17).

#### [Application for Approval Letter]{.HTitleText3} {#h-797377 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[182]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-182 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person may apply for an approval letter by completing, signing and
  sending to the Chief Inspector of Explosives the application form
  provided by the Department of Natural Resources. The application must
  include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, address, telephone number and
    email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's date of birth;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant is employed by or is a director of
    a holder of a licence, permit or certificate, the holder's name,
    address, telephone number and email address and, if applicable, the
    name of the applicant's supervisor; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant resides outside of Canada, a
    letter from the applicant's employer in Canada confirming the work
    to be done and supporting the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criminal record check

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must include the original or a
  certified copy of a criminal record check carried out on the applicant
  within one year before the date on which the application is received
  by the Minister, or if the applicant resides outside of Canada, a
  certified police certificate from their country of origin dated not
  more than one year before the date on which the application is
  received by the Minister or a valid Canadian work permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Inspector of Explosives may request that a
  person who has submitted an application for an approval letter provide
  any additional information or any document that is necessary to enable
  the Minister to determine whether to issue the approval letter.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 507
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 47](#1451498-1452476){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451498-1452476.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-182-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 182"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of letter

- **[[183]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-183 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the applicant's criminal record check does not reveal any of the
  circumstances set out in subsection (2), the Minister must issue a
  dated approval letter to the applicant and send a copy of the letter
  to any holder of a licence, permit or certificate mentioned in the
  application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must refuse to issue an approval letter
  and must give the applicant written notice of the refusal, and the
  reasons for the refusal, if the applicant's criminal record check
  reveals any of the following circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant is subject to a court order
    prohibiting them from possessing any explosives;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant has, within the five years before the
    date on which the application was received by the Minister, been
    convicted of any of the following offences in Canada or equivalent
    offences committed outside of Canada:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an indictable offence under the [Explosives
      Act](/eng/acts/E-17),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indictable offence under Quebec's An Act
      respecting Explosives, as amended from time to time,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an offence under any of the following
      provisions of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46):

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} section 80 (breach of duty),

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} section 81 (using explosives),

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} section 82 (possession of explosives without
        lawful excuse),

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} subsection 235(1) (first and second degree
        murder),

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} subsection 239(1) (attempted murder),

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} subsection 431.2(2) (explosive or other lethal
        device),

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} section 436.1 (possession of incendiary
        material), or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the applicant has, within the five years before
      the date on which the application was received, been convicted
      more than once of either of the following offences or has been
      convicted at least once of each of them:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an indictable offence in the commission of
        which violence against another person was used, threatened or
        attempted, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an offence under section 264 of the [Criminal
        Code](/eng/acts/C-46) (criminal harassment).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other grounds of refusal

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must refuse to issue an approval
  letter and must give the applicant written notice giving reasons of
  the refusal if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant has made a false or misleading
    statement in the application; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
    issuing the approval letter for the licence, permit or certificate
    mentioned in the application would constitute a risk to the safety
    or security of persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant may, within 30 days after the day on
  which they receive a notice of refusal from the Minister, send the
  Minister a written request for a review of the refusal on the grounds
  that the information on which the refusal was based is incorrect,
  accompanied by any additional documents or any other information in
  writing that is necessary to support the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition on review

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} After reviewing the new information or documents, the
  Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} issue the approval letter if the information on
    which the refusal was based is incorrect; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} give the applicant written notice of the refusal,
    and the reasons for the refusal, and send a copy of the notice to
    any licence, permit or certificate holder mentioned in the
    application, if the information on which the refusal was based is
    correct.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review not requested

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant does not request a review, the
  Minister must, at the end of the period mentioned in subsection (3),
  send a copy of the notice of refusal to any licence, permit or
  certificate holder mentioned in the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 507
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 38, 44(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400442){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400442.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 48](#1451503-1452485){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451503-1452485.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-183-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 183"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

- **[[183.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-183.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may by written notice
  with reasons cancel the approval letter referred to in section 182 if
  one of the circumstances referred to in paragraph 183(2)(a) or (b)
  applies to the holder of the letter or on either grounds referred to
  in subsection 183(2.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Inspector of Explosives may request that a
  person who has submitted an application for an approval letter provide
  any additional information or any document that is necessary to enable
  the Minister to determine whether to cancel the approval letter.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 49](#1451510-1452492){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451510-1452492.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

**[[184]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-184 .sectionLabel}** An approval letter
remains valid for five years after the date on which it is issued.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of letter

- **[[185]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-185 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who has been issued an approval letter may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} obtain a copy of the letter by sending a request to
    the Chief Inspector of Explosives that sets out the person's name,
    address, telephone number and email address and the date of the
    letter; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have a copy of the letter sent to a holder of a
    licence, permit or certificate by sending a request to the Chief
    Inspector of Explosives that sets out the person's name, address,
    telephone number and email address, the date of the letter and the
    holder's name, address and email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence, permit or certificate who
  wishes to verify that a director or employee of the holder, or a
  person seeking employment from the holder, has an approval letter must
  complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector of Explosives the
  application form provided by the Department of Natural Resources. The
  application must include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and email
    address of both the holder and a contact person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and date of birth of the person whose
    approval letter is to be verified; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that person's written consent, witnessed and signed
    by a witness.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 507
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 50](#1451512-1452494){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451512-1452494.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-185-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 185"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 9]{.HLabel1}[Transporting Explosives]{.HTitleText1} {#h-797432 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[186]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186 .sectionLabel}** This Part sets out the
requirements for transporting explosives, including in transit
transportation and the loading and unloading of explosives, that must be
met by shippers, carriers and drivers. When certain explosives are to be
shipped, the requirements of section 190 apply. In all other cases, the
requirements of sections 191 to 201 (dealing with transportation by
vehicle) and sections 202 to 203.1 (dealing with transportation by other
means) apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 20
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400445){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400445.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 51](#1451514-1452497){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451514-1452497.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-186-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 186"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[187]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who transports
    explosives or who provides the service of transporting
    explosives. ([transporteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[shipper]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[shipper]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who arranges for a
    carrier, prepares the explosives for transport and delivers them to
    the carrier. ([expéditeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400449){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400449.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-187-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 187"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Explosive quantity

**[[188]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188 .sectionLabel}** A reference to the mass
of an explosive in this Part is a reference to its net quantity (the
mass of the explosive excluding the mass of any packaging or container
and, in the case of an explosive article, also excluding any component
that is not an explosive substance), except in section 190 where it is a
reference to its gross quantity (the mass of the explosive plus the mass
of any packaging or container).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400453){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400453.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-188-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 188"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Driver who is not a carrier

**[[189]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189 .sectionLabel}** A driver is not subject
to the requirements for carriers that are set out in this Part if the
driver is an employee, or an agent or mandatary, of the carrier.

### [Exemption of Certain Explosives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797445 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2018-231, s. 21

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of explosives

- **[[190]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-190 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  carrier or driver is not subject to the requirements of sections 191
  to 203.1 if they transport

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no more than 12 kg of an explosive with the number
    UN 0027, BLACK POWDER (GUNPOWDER) or UN 0028, BLACK POWDER
    (GUNPOWDER) COMPRESSED;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no more than 150 kg of an explosive with the number

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} UN 0161, POWDER, SMOKELESS,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0186, ROCKET MOTORS,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0191, SIGNAL DEVICES, HAND,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} UN 0197, SIGNALS, SMOKE,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} UN 0276, CARTRIDGES, POWER DEVICE,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} UN 0312, CARTRIDGES, SIGNAL,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0336, FIREWORKS,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0351, ARTICLES, EXPLOSIVE, N.O.S.,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} UN 0403, FLARES, AERIAL,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} UN 0431, ARTICLES, PYROTECHNIC,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} UN 0453, ROCKETS, LINE THROWING,

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0499, PROPELLANT, SOLID,

    - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0501, PROPELLANT, SOLID,

    - [(xiv)]{.lawlabel} UN 0503, AIR BAG INFLATORS, AIR BAG MODULES,
      SEAT-BELT PRETENSIONERS,

    - [(xv)]{.lawlabel} UN 0505, SIGNALS, DISTRESS,

    - [(xvi)]{.lawlabel} UN 0509, POWDER, SMOKELESS; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any quantity of an explosive with the number

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} UN 0012, CARTRIDGES, SMALL ARMS,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0014, CARTRIDGES, SMALL ARMS, BLANK or
      CARTRIDGES FOR TOOLS, BLANK,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0044, PRIMERS, CAP,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} UN 0055, CASES, CARTRIDGE, EMPTY, WITH PRIMER,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} UN 0105, FUSE, SAFETY,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} UN 0131, LIGHTERS, FUSE,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0173, RELEASE DEVICES, EXPLOSIVE,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0323, CARTRIDGES, POWER DEVICE;

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} UN 0337, FIREWORKS,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} UN 0373, SIGNAL DEVICES, HAND,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} UN 0404, FLARES, AERIAL,

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0405, CARTRIDGES, SIGNAL,

    - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0432, ARTICLES, PYROTECHNIC,

    - [(xiv)]{.lawlabel} UN 0454, IGNITERS,

    - [(xv)]{.lawlabel} UN 0506, SIGNALS, DISTRESS, or

    - [(xvi)]{.lawlabel} UN 0507, SIGNALS, SMOKE.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the carrier and driver must
  ensure that the explosives are transported in packaging or a container
  that is designed, constructed, filled, closed, secured and maintained
  so that under normal conditions of transport the likelihood of an
  ignition is minimized.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 21
- SOR/2018-231, s. 22
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 52(E)](#1451516-1452500){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451516-1452500.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-190-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 190"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Transporting Explosives in a Vehicle]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797490 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vehicle requirements

- **[[191]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-191 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  carrier of explosives by vehicle must ensure that the portion of the
  vehicle that contains the explosives

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is either an intermodal container or is fully
    enclosed and fire resistant and is constructed of or is lined with a
    non-sparking material or does not increase the likelihood of an
    ignition; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is kept locked except when the explosives are being
    loaded or unloaded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vehicle kept locked

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The driver of the vehicle must also ensure that the
  portion of the vehicle that contains the explosives is kept locked
  except when the explosives are being loaded or unloaded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Towed vehicle

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A carrier of explosives must not transport explosives
  in a towed vehicle unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the explosives are in a semi-trailer attached to a
    truck tractor or in a fifth-wheel trailer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the explosives are in a trailer that is part of a
    road train travelling over ice roads and the Minister has determined
    that precautions minimizing the likelihood of an ignition have been
    taken;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister or a police officer directs that the
    vehicle be towed because of an emergency or a breakdown;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it is for the purposes of returning the vehicle to
    the road and the following requirements are met:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} there is no evidence of theft, attempted theft or
      loss of an explosive,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} there is no evidence of a fire, spill or
      accidental explosion,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} there is no evidence of an injury or death,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} there is no evidence of accidental property or
      vehicle damage,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} there is no evidence of a release or anticipated
      release of explosives, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} after the vehicle has been towed to the road,
      the driver conducts an inspection to verify that the vehicle
      continues to meet the safety and roadworthiness requirements of
      this Part and to confirm that the explosives remain undamaged; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the explosives are classified as UN 3375 and are in
    a [[road vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 1.4
    of the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286), and no more than two
    drawn road vehicles are used in a road train.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transportation of explosives at a mine site or
    quarry; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transportation of fireworks with UN number UN
    0333, UN 0334, UN 0335 or UN 0336 in a towed vehicle if the driver
    of the tow vehicle holds a fireworks operator certificate (display
    assistant), fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor) or
    fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor with endorsement)
    and ensures that the quantity of fireworks transported in the towed
    vehicle does not exceed 750 kg and precautions have been taken to
    minimize the sway of both vehicles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Oversized load

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an explosive article or equipment that is
  contaminated with an explosive substance is too large to be contained
  in a fully enclosed portion of a vehicle or an intermodal container,
  the article or equipment may be transported on a flatbed if the
  carrier obtains a permit to do so issued by the Minister under
  paragraph 7(1)(b) of the [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17). The carrier
  and the driver must ensure that the article or equipment is secured to
  the flatbed and, if feasible, covered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for permit

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A carrier who applies for a permit to use a flatbed
  to transport an explosive article or equipment that is contaminated
  with an explosive substance must complete, sign and send to the Chief
  Inspector of Explosives the application form provided by the
  Department of Natural Resources. The application must include the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, address, telephone number and
    email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number and vehicle identification
    number of the flatbed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number of the division 1 factory licence or
    magazine licence that authorizes the manufacture or storage of the
    explosive article or the explosive that contaminated the equipment,
    along with the address of the factory or magazine from which or to
    which the article or equipment is to be transported;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the explosive article or
    contaminated equipment; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the method to be used to cover the
    article or equipment and to secure it to the flatbed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1), (2) and (4) do not apply to jet
  perforating guns if they\

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are transported in a motor vehicle or fifth-wheel
    trailer that is equipped with racks, carrying cases or devices that
    are designed and constructed to ensure that the jet perforating guns
    remain securely held in place during transport;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are protected from damage during transport;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} do not extend beyond the body of the motor vehicle
    or the bed of the fifth-wheel trailer; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} are transported in a manner that protects them from
    theft.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement --- material

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a part of a portion of the vehicle that will
  contain explosives could come into contact with the explosives or
  their packaging during transport and if that part consists of a
  material that could increase the likelihood of an ignition if contact
  were to occur, the carrier must ensure that the part is covered with
  material that will prevent the contact from occurring.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 53\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fire extinguishers

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that the vehicle that
  contains explosives is equipped with two fire extinguishers that have
  a rating of at least 4-A :40-B:C and are easily accessible.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 53\]]{.Repealed}

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 53\]]{.Repealed}

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 53\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dangerous goods marks

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} The carrier and the driver must ensure that any
  dangerous goods marks that are required by the [Transportation of
  Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) to be
  displayed when the vehicle is transporting explosives on a public
  highway are also displayed when the vehicle contains explosives and is
  not on a public highway.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detonators

- **[[192]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-192 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  detonators are to be transported in a vehicle that contains other
  explosives, the shipper and the carrier must ensure that no more than
  20 000 detonators will be transported with the other explosives. They
  must also ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle that contains no more than
    2 000 kg of explosives, the detonators are stowed apart from the
    other explosives so that an explosion of one or more detonators will
    not ignite any of the other explosives; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle that contains more than 2
    000 kg of explosives, the detonators are stowed in a container in or
    compartment of the vehicle that is fully enclosed, cannot be
    accessed from the portion of the vehicle that contains the other
    explosives and will prevent the detonators from exploding during a
    fire for at least one hour.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Damaged or deteriorated explosives

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The shipper and the carrier must not transport
  damaged or deteriorated explosives unless the Minister determines that
  the explosives must be transported to another location for the purpose
  of storing them, making them fit for use, remaking or repairing them
  or destroying them and that precautions that minimize the likelihood
  of an ignition have been taken.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Items transported with explosives

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The shipper and the carrier must ensure that things
  other than explosives are not transported with explosives unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle that contains no more than
    2 000 kg of explosives, the things are stowed, or separated from,
    the explosives so as to minimize the likelihood of an ignition; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle that contains more than 2
    000 kg of explosives, the vehicle has been authorized to transport
    the things by a permit issued by the Minister under paragraph
    7(1)(b) of the [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17) and the permit is in
    the vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permit to transport non-explosive items

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A shipper or a carrier who applies for a permit to
  transport things other than explosives in a vehicle that will contain
  explosives must complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector of
  Explosives the application form provided by the Department of Natural
  Resources. The application must include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, address, telephone number and
    email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a list of the non-explosive items to be transported
    with the explosives in the vehicle; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the precautions that will be taken to eliminate any
    possibility of an ignition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit for road trains on ice roads

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a road train travelling over ice
  roads, the carrier must ensure that no more than 20 000 kg of
  explosives are transported in any trailer forming part of the train.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sound mechanical condition

- **[[193]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-193 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  carrier must ensure that the following requirements are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle to be used to transport explosives is
    in sound mechanical condition and capable of safely transporting
    explosives, meaning that any dangers including those related to
    gasoline engines, heaters and lights, refrigeration and climate
    control systems and hot components of the exhaust system have been
    addressed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle is fuelled, the oil level and tire
    pressure are checked and all required servicing of the vehicle is
    carried out before the loading of explosives begins.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Combustible material

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The carrier and the driver must ensure that the
  portion of the vehicle containing explosives is free of grit,
  combustible or abrasive material, any fire-producing, spark-producing
  or flame-producing device and any substance that might ignite
  spontaneously.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The driver must ensure that the vehicle, while
  transporting explosives, is inspected daily to verify that the
  following requirements are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the fire extinguishers are filled and in good
    working order;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the electric wiring is completely insulated and
    firmly secured;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the fuel tank and fuel lines have no leaks;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the chassis, engine, pan and bottom of the body are
    clean and free from excess oil and grease;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the brakes and steering apparatus are in good
    condition; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the tires are not worn smooth or visibly defective.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correcting deficiencies

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The driver must ensure that any deficiencies found on
  the vehicle during an inspection are corrected before it is used to
  transport explosives.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loading and unloading

- **[[194]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-194 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  carrier must ensure that, while explosives are being loaded into or
  unloaded from a vehicle, precautions that minimize the possibility of
  an ignition are taken. The carrier must also ensure that only
  activities that are necessary for loading or unloading the explosives
  are carried out in the immediate vicinity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that precautions are taken
  that will prevent unauthorized people from having access to the
  explosives during loading or unloading and that will prevent any
  person in the immediate vicinity from doing anything that could
  increase the likelihood of an ignition.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 56\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Engine turned off and brakes set

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} During the loading or unloading of explosives, the
  driver must ensure that the engine is turned off and the brakes are
  set. However, the engine may be left running if it is needed to
  operate a power take-off or if cold and wind conditions could make
  restarting the engine difficult.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No stopping

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that once the loading or
  unloading begins, it continues without stopping until it is completed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Throwing or dropping explosives

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that packages or containers
  of explosives are not thrown or dropped during the loading or
  unloading of explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Securing explosives

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that the explosives are
  stowed and secured in a manner that eliminates any possibility of an
  ignition, including ignition by another item or substance that is also
  being transported.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400470){.wb-lbx

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation --- shipper

- **[[195]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-195
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before delivering the explosives to
  the carrier, the shipper must obtain confirmation from the intended
  recipient that the explosives will be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used on the same day they are delivered and
    attended in person until they are used;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} stored in a factory magazine, licensed magazine,
    storage unit or dwelling in accordance with these Regulations; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shipped immediately to the next recipient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation --- carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A carrier must not load explosives into a vehicle
  unless they have obtained confirmation from the intended recipient
  that they will be able to receive them at the time of delivery.

::: HistoricalNote

  role="button"}

Version](section-195-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 195"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Age of driver

- **[[196]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is less than 21 years old must not drive a vehicle that is
  transporting more than 2 000 kg of explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No smoking

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person must not smoke while they are in a vehicle
  that contains explosives, or while they are attending one.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Performance-diminishing substance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who is under the influence of alcohol or
  another performance-diminishing substance must not be in or attend a
  vehicle that contains explosives. However, a person who has taken a
  prescription drug may be in or attend a vehicle containing explosives
  if they have medical proof that they need the drug and that it will
  not impede their ability to function safely.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Performance-diminishing substance ---
  possession

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A driver and any person assisting the driver must not
  carry alcohol or another performance-diminishing substance for their
  personal use while transporting explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No stopping en route

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The driver of a vehicle transporting explosives must
  not stop en route unnecessarily. If a stop is necessary, the driver
  must stop for no longer than required under the circumstances and must
  park the vehicle away from any place where there is an open flame,
  match or any other thing that could increase the likelihood of an
  ignition, and, in order to ensure that the risk of harm to people and
  property is as low as reasonably practicable, the driver must park the
  vehicle away from:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any dwelling;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any place where flammable substances, including but
    not limited to gasoline pumps, propane tanks or above-ground storage
    tanks for flammable liquid or flammable gas, are stored; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any area where people are likely to gather.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (5), a vehicle transporting no
  more than 25 kg of high explosives (type E) and no more than 100
  detonators (type I), may be parked if the following requirements are
  met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no other item or substance that could increase the
    likelihood of an ignition is in the vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle is parked in a place where there is no
    open flame, match or any other thing that could increase the
    likelihood of an ignition; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle is parked at least 30 m from any
    dwelling, railway line or public highway and from any place where
    flammable substances, including gasoline pumps, propane tanks or
    above-ground storage tanks for a flammable liquid or flammable gas,
    are stored.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Repairs

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a mechanical breakdown of a vehicle
  transporting explosives, the driver must ensure that repairs to the
  vehicle are done at the location of the breakdown only if doing the
  repair work will not increase the likelihood of an ignition and that
  the work is done by a person who understands the hazards to which they
  could be exposed and who is competent to carry out their duties in a
  manner that is safe, lawful and ensures the security of the vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Route

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The driver of a vehicle transporting explosives must
  follow dangerous goods routes. If there are no dangerous goods routes,
  the driver must, if possible, avoid routes that pass through densely
  populated areas.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintaining separation

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A driver of a vehicle transporting more than 2 000 kg
  of explosives must maintain a distance of at least 300 m between their
  vehicle and any other vehicle that is transporting more than 2 000 kg
  of explosives.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 57\]]{.Repealed}
