[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obtaining assistance

**[[197]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-197 .sectionLabel}** The carrier must ensure
that the driver of a vehicle transporting more than 2 000 kg of
explosives is able to rely on assistance from one or more of the
following people:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an assistant who is accompanying the driver;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who is in another vehicle that is not
  transporting explosives following the vehicle transporting explosives
  and with whom the driver is in constant communication;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person who is monitoring a two-way radio or
  equivalent communication system.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400484){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400484.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-197-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 197"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tracking and communication system

- **[[198]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-198 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  vehicle --- other than a vehicle in which a manufacturing operation
  may be carried out and that remains on a mine site or quarry while
  transporting explosives referred to in subsection (2) --- is used to
  transport 1 000 or more detonators or at least 2 000 kg of explosives
  referred to in subsection (2), the carrier must ensure that it is
  equipped with a tracking and communication system.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Listed explosives

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tracking and communication system is required if
  the vehicle is transporting explosives that are classified under the
  [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Division 1.1, 1.2 or 1.3;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Division 1.4 with one of the following UN numbers:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} UN 0104, CORD DETONATING, MILD EFFECT or FUSE
      DETONATING, MILD EFFECT,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0237, CHARGES, SHAPED, FLEXIBLE, LINEAR,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0255, DETONATORS, ELECTRIC,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} UN 0267, DETONATORS, NON-ELECTRIC,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} UN 0289, CORD, DETONATING,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} UN 0361, DETONATOR ASSEMBLIES, NON-ELECTRIC,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0365, DETONATORS FOR AMMUNITION,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0366, DETONATORS FOR AMMUNITION,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} UN 0440, CHARGES, SHAPED,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} UN 0441, CHARGES, SHAPED,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} UN 0445, CHARGES, EXPLOSIVE, COMMERCIAL,

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0456, DETONATORS, ELECTRIC,

    - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} UN 0500, DETONATOR ASSEMBLIES, NON-ELECTRIC;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Division 1.5 or 1.6; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} Division 5.1, for explosives numbered UN 3375
    AMMONIUM NITRATE EMULSION, GEL, OR SUSPENSION.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for system

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tracking and communication system must allow the
  person who is monitoring the system to locate the vehicle at all times
  and must allow the driver and the person to communicate with one
  another.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Monitoring system

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that a person is monitoring
  the tracking and communication system at all times while the
  explosives are being transported and will notify the police in case of
  an emergency.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 25
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400488){.wb-lbx

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vehicle attended

- **[[199]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-199 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  carrier and the driver of a vehicle that contains explosives must
  ensure that it is attended in person when it is not at a factory or
  satellite site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), up to 25 kg of high
  explosives (type E) and up to 100 detonators (type I) may be left
  unattended in a vehicle if the requirements set out in subsections
  196(5) or (5.1) have been met and if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the explosives have been removed from a factory
    magazine or licensed magazine for a specific purpose set out in
    their authorization;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the explosives are stored in a storage unit that
    has been serviced at a factory or licensed magazine and is bolted or
    welded to the vehicle or, if the explosives are jet perforating
    guns, the guns are securely locked to the vehicle; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a device or system is in place to ensure that the
    vehicle is immobilized and that the driver is alerted by an alarm if
    an attempt is made to steal the explosives, tamper with the storage
    unit or tamper with or steal the vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Leftover explosives

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When the specific purpose for which the explosives
  were removed has been carried out or abandoned, the carrier and driver
  must ensure that any explosives remaining in the vehicle are stored in
  a factory or licensed magazine as soon as the circumstances permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic monitoring

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A vehicle containing explosives numbered UN 0332 or
  UN 3375 that are transported in bulk may be attended using electronic
  means if the following requirements are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle, including the portion of the vehicle
    containing explosives, is locked;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle, including the portion of the vehicle
    containing explosives, is parked in a secure location and is under
    video surveillance;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle has a device or system to ensure that
    the vehicle is immobilized and that the driver and carrier are
    alerted by an alarm if an attempt is made to steal the explosives or
    tamper with or steal the vehicle; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an emergency, the driver or carrier
    contacts the appropriate authorities as soon as feasible.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400493){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400493.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 59](#1451549-1452571){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451549-1452571.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-199-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 199"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[200]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-200 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2024-77, s. 60\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-200-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 200"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accidents and incidents

- **[[201]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-201 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  driver of a vehicle that contains explosives must, as soon as the
  circumstances permit,\

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notify the shipper and the carrier if the vehicle
    is delayed for any reason, including road conditions or mechanical
    issues; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the police, the shipper and the carrier if
    the vehicle is involved in a road accident or incident that results
    in

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the theft, attempted theft or loss of an
      explosive,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a fire, release, anticipated release or
      accidental explosion,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an injury or death, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any accidental property or vehicle damage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the event of an accident or incident referred to
  in paragraph (1)(b), the carrier must, as soon as the circumstances
  permit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} report the accident or incident to an inspector;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that any damaged explosives are transported
    to any location that the Minister designates;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} ensure that any undamaged explosives are
    transported to their destination or to a safe and secure location;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an accident or incident referred to
    in subparagraph (1)(b)(i), (ii) or (iii), provide the Chief
    Inspector of Explosives with a written report on the accident or
    incident that sets out its likely cause and the steps that the
    carrier will take to prevent a recurrence of an accident or incident
    of that nature.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
- SOR/2018-231, s. 26
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1399974){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1399974.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 60](#1451554-1452588){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451554-1452588.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-201-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 201"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Transporting Explosives in a Means of Transport Other Than a Vehicle]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797676 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loading and unloading

- **[[202]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  carrier must ensure that, while explosives are being loaded into or
  unloaded from a means of transport other than a vehicle, precautions
  that minimize the likelihood of an ignition are taken. The carrier
  must also ensure that only activities that are necessary for loading
  or unloading the explosives are carried out in the immediate vicinity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that precautions that prevent
  unauthorized people from having access to the explosives during
  loading or unloading are taken, as well as precautions that prevent
  any person in the immediate vicinity from doing anything that might
  increase the likelihood of an ignition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Throwing or dropping explosives

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that packages or containers
  of explosives are not thrown or dropped during the loading or
  unloading of the explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Securing explosives

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The carrier must ensure that the explosives are
  stowed and secured in a manner that eliminates any possibility of an
  ignition, including ignition by another item or substance that is also
  being transported.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400504){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400504.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 202"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation --- shipper

- **[[203]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-203
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before delivering the explosives to
  the carrier, the shipper must obtain confirmation from the intended
  recipient of the explosives that the explosives will be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used on the same day they are delivered and
    attended until they are used;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} stored in a factory magazine, licensed magazine,
    storage unit or dwelling in accordance with these Regulations; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shipped immediately to the next recipient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation --- carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A carrier must not load explosives into a means of
  transport other than a vehicle unless they have obtained confirmation
  from the intended recipient that they will be able to receive them at
  the time of delivery.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400510){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400510.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-203-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 203"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transportation by vessel

- **[[203.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-203.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no person
  shall, at a wharf or port facility, load onto or unload from a vessel
  packaged goods that contain 25 kg or more of explosives unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of explosives does not exceed 20 000
    kg and the explosives are loaded or unloaded by driving a single
    vehicle directly aboard a roll-on, roll-off cargo vessel immediately
    before departing, or driving it ashore immediately after arrival, on
    a last-on, first-off basis; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a quantified risk assessment that meets the
    requirements of subsection (5) has been conducted on the wharf or
    port facility where explosives of the types in question will be
    loaded or unloaded and an inspector has determined that the quantity
    of explosives to be loaded or unloaded does not exceed the maximum
    quantity permitted for each type of explosive and hazard category in
    the assessment report and the safety measures set out in the report
    are complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional safety measures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An inspector who makes the determination referred to
  in paragraph (1)(b) must impose any additional safety measures that
  are necessary for the safe loading and unloading of the explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum waiting time

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If explosives are loaded or unloaded under paragraph
  (1)(a), the maximum waiting time of the vehicle dockside is 30 minutes
  and during that time the vehicle must be parked as far as possible
  from all areas used by the general public or for the handling or
  storage of other cargo.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (3) do not apply to explosives
  included in Class 1.4S of the International Maritime Dangerous Goods
  Code, published by the International Maritime Organization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantified risk assessment ---
  requirements

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The quantified risk assessment must meet the
  following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its methodology must be approved by the Chief
    Inspector of Explosives;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be conducted by a person who is qualified
    to carry out quantified risk assessments of activities involving
    explosives;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must establish safety measures, including
    quantity limits, for loading and unloading the types of explosives
    in question, taking into account the hazard categories of the
    explosives;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the safety measures must ensure that the risk of
    harm to people and property resulting from loading or unloading the
    explosives does not increase the risk to people who are not involved
    in loading or unloading the explosives or the risk to property; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the safety measures must be set out in a report
    that has been approved by an inspector.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of methodology --- criteria

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Inspector of Explosives must approve the
  methodology to be used in a quantified risk assessment if he or she
  determines that the methodology is capable of accurately calculating
  the risks to people and property resulting from the loading and
  unloading of explosives at the wharf or port facility to be assessed,
  taking into account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the types of explosives that will be loaded or
    unloaded and the quantity of each type;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the hazard categories of the explosives;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the structure of the vessel onto which the
    explosives will be loaded or from which they will be unloaded;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the distance between the explosives and people
    likely to be in the vicinity;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the characteristics, proximity and use of the
    surrounding structures and infrastructure; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the activities that are likely to be carried out in
    the vicinity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of report --- criteria

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An inspector to whom a quantified risk assessment
  report has been submitted must approve the report if he or she
  determines that the safety measures in it meet the requirements
  referred to in paragraphs (5)(c) and (d).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 27
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400514){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400514.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-203.1-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 203.1"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 10]{.HLabel1}[Military Explosives and Law Enforcement Explosives]{.HTitleText1} {#h-797724 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[204]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-204 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of military explosives (type D) and
law enforcement explosives (type D) and sets out the rules for sellers
and users.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[205]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[law enforcement agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[law enforcement agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a police force,
    the Correctional Service of Canada or the Canada Border Services
    Agency. ([organisme d'application de la loi]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes the
    storage of the military explosives or law enforcement explosives to
    be sold or acquired. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, or a law enforcement
    agency, that acquires military explosives or law enforcement
    explosives for use. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 205"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797734 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition for sale

**[[206]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-206 .sectionLabel}** A seller may acquire,
store and sell military explosives or law enforcement explosives if they
hold a licence. A seller who acquires military explosives or law
enforcement explosives must comply with this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

- **[[207]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-207 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must store their military explosives and law enforcement
  explosives in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No display for sale

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not display military explosives or law
  enforcement explosives for sale.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- licensed buyer

- **[[208]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-208 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller may sell military explosives or law enforcement explosives only
  to a buyer who holds a licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more military explosives or
  law enforcement explosives to a buyer than the buyer is authorized by
  their licence to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- certain entities

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a seller may sell the
  following explosives to the following buyers even if the buyer is
  unlicensed:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} military explosives and law enforcement explosives
    to the Department of National Defence and to any armed forces
    cooperating with the Canadian Forces; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} military explosives, hazard category PE 3 or PE 4,
    and law enforcement explosives, hazard category PE 3 or PE 4, to a
    police force operating in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[209]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of every sale of military explosives or law enforcement
explosives in a secure location for two years after the date of the
sale. The record must include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the individual who bought the
  explosives and, if applicable, the name of the entity for which the
  explosives were acquired;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licensed buyer, the licence number
  and expiry date;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the product name of each explosive sold and the name
  of the person who obtained its authorization;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of explosives sold under each product
  name; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400517){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400517.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 209"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Rules for Users]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797760 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition --- licensed user

- **[[210]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-210 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user may acquire and store military explosives or law enforcement
  explosives if they hold a licence. A user who acquires military
  explosives or law enforcement explosives must comply with subsection
  (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user must store their military explosives and law
  enforcement explosives in the magazine specified in their licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition --- exception

- **[[211]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-211
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 210(1), a law
  enforcement agency may acquire and store military explosives, hazard
  category PE 3 or PE 4, or law enforcement explosives, hazard category
  PE 3 or PE 4, even if the agency is unlicensed. A law enforcement
  agency that acquires such explosives must comply with subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The explosives must be stored away from flammable
  substances and sources of ignition, in a manner that prevents them
  from being stolen and ensures that access to them is limited to people
  authorized by the law enforcement agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 24
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-211-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 211"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 11]{.HLabel1}[Industrial Explosives]{.HTitleText1} {#h-797773 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[212]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-212 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of explosives that are used for
industrial purposes and sets out the rules for sellers and users.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[213]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-213 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[industrial explosive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[industrial explosive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the following
    types of explosive:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} E.1 --- blasting explosives;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} E.2 --- perforating explosives;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} E.3 --- special-application explosives;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} I --- initiation systems; and

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} P.1 --- black powder and hazard category PE 1
      black powder substitutes, when they are used in mining, quarrying,
      construction or avalanche control. ([explosif
      industriel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that is issued by
    the Minister and authorizes storage of the type of industrial
    explosive to be sold or acquired. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[manufacturing certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manufacturing certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    certificate that authorizes the manufacture and storage of the type
    of industrial explosive to be acquired or manufactured. ([certificat
    de fabrication]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires industrial
    explosives for use or conveyance. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 25, 38
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400521){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400521.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-213-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 213"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797789 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition for sale

**[[214]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-214 .sectionLabel}** A seller may acquire,
store and sell industrial explosives if they hold a licence. A seller
who acquires industrial explosives must comply with this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

**[[215]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-215 .sectionLabel}** A seller must store
their industrial explosives in the magazine specified in their licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No display for sale

**[[216]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-216 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not
display industrial explosives for sale.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- authorized buyer

- **[[217]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-217 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller may sell industrial explosives only to a buyer who holds a
  licence or a manufacturing certificate or who is authorized by a
  competent provincial or territorial authority to use, keep or store
  industrial explosives at a mine site or quarry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more industrial explosives to
  a buyer than the buyer is authorized by their licence, certificate or
  provincial or territorial authorization to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply to explosives in bulk
  that are loaded into prepared boreholes.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 61](#1451556-1452593){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451556-1452593.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-217-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 217"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on packaging

- **[[218]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-218 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must mark the number of the buyer's licence, manufacturing
  certificate or provincial or territorial authorization in a clear and
  permanent manner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the outer packaging or container of the
    industrial explosives if the outer packaging or container is sealed;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the inner packaging of each industrial explosive
    or on each reel of detonating cord if the outer packaging or
    container is not sealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the outer packaging or containers of industrial
    explosives if the number is contained in a barcode or matrix code
    that is printed on the packaging or container;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} containers holding industrial explosives in bulk;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} intermediate bulk containers holding industrial
    explosives in bulk; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} plastic bags that meet the requirements of UN
    packaging codes 5H1, 5H2, 5H3 or 5H4.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 28
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400524){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400524.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 62](#1451561-1452600){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451561-1452600.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-218-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 218"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[219]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-219 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of each sale of industrial explosives for two years after the
date of the sale. The record must include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of the buyer's licence, certificate or
  authorization;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the type, product name and dimensions of each
  explosive sold and name of the person who obtained its authorization;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of explosives sold under each product
  name; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400527){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400527.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 63(F)](#1451568-1452601){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451568-1452601.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-219-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 219"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reuse of packaging

- **[[220]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-220 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must ensure that any packaging or container that has been used
  for industrial explosives is not reused unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is in good condition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of a container holding
    industrial explosives in bulk, it contains no explosive residue;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is reused for the same type of explosive that it
    previously contained; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all information on the packaging or container
    remains accurate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging --- nitroglycerine-based
  explosive

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must ensure that any packaging or container
  that has been used for a nitroglycerine-based explosive or any other
  explosive that is manufactured from a liquid explosive is destroyed
  (for example, by breaking the packaging or container) as soon as the
  circumstances permit after the packaging or container is emptied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging --- in poor condition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A seller must ensure that any packaging or container
  that is not in good condition when it is emptied of explosives is
  destroyed (for example, by breaking the packaging or container) as
  soon as the circumstances permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply to containers holding
  explosives in bulk.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400532){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400532.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 64](#1451570-1452605){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451570-1452605.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-220-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 220"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Rules for Users]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797836 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[221]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-221 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire and
store industrial explosives if they hold a licence or a manufacturing
certificate or are authorized by a competent provincial or territorial
authority to use, keep or store such explosives at a mine site or
quarry. A user who acquires industrial explosives must comply with this
Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 65](#1451575-1452609){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451575-1452609.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-221-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 221"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on packaging

- **[[221.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-221.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A user who holds a magazine licence
  and imports industrial explosives must mark the number of the buyer's
  licence, manufacturing certificate or provincial or territorial
  authorization in a clear and permanent manner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the outer packaging or container of the
    explosives if the outer packaging or container is sealed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the inner packaging of each explosive or each
    reel of detonating cord if the outer packaging or container is not
    sealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the outer packaging or containers of explosives if
    the number is contained in a barcode or matrix code that is printed
    on the packaging or container;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} containers holding explosives in bulk;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} intermediate bulk containers holding explosives in
    bulk; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} plastic bags that meet the requirements of UN
    packaging codes 5H1, 5H2, 5H3 or 5H4.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 65](#1451575-1452622){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451575-1452622.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on packaging --- acquisition

- **[[222]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-222 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who acquires industrial explosives that are in sealed outer
  packaging or a sealed container must, after opening the packaging or
  container, mark the number of their licence, manufacturing certificate
  or provincial or territorial authorization in a clear and permanent
  manner on the inner packaging of each explosive or on each reel of
  detonating cord.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on packaging ---
  importation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who imports industrial explosives must mark
  the number of their licence, manufacturing certificate or provincial
  or territorial authorization in a clear and permanent manner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the outer packaging or container of the
    explosives if the outer packaging or container is sealed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the inner packaging of each explosive or on each
    reel of detonating cord if the outer packaging or container is not
    sealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the outer packaging or containers of industrial
    explosives if the number of the licence is contained in a barcode or
    matrix code that is printed on the packaging or container;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} containers holding industrial explosives in bulk;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} intermediate bulk containers holding industrial
    explosives in bulk; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} plastic bags that meet the requirements of UN
    packaging codes 5H1, 5H2, 5H3 or 5H4.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 65](#1451575-1452637){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451575-1452637.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-222-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 222"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

- **[[223]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-223 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their industrial explosives in the
  magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Explosives to be attended

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who holds a licence or a manufacturing
  certificate must ensure that their industrial explosives are attended
  when they are at a site of use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to industrial
  explosives that are stored on offshore platforms for use in offshore
  oil or gas wells.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400537){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400537.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-223-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 223"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reuse of packaging

- **[[224]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-224 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user must ensure that any packaging or container that has been used
  for industrial explosives is not reused unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is in good condition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of a container holding
    industrial explosives in bulk, it contains no explosive residue;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is reused for the same type of explosive that it
    previously contained; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all information on the packaging or container
    remains accurate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging --- nitroglycerine-based
  explosive

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user must ensure that any packaging or container
  that has been used for a nitroglycerine-based explosive or any other
  explosive that is manufactured from a liquid explosive is destroyed so
  that it cannot be reused (for example, by breaking the packaging or
  container) as soon as the circumstances permit after the packaging or
  container is emptied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging not in good condition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A user must ensure that any packaging or container
  that is not in good condition when it is emptied of explosives is
  destroyed so that it cannot be reused (for example, by breaking the
  packaging or container) as soon as the circumstances permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- subsections (1) and (2)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the outer packaging or containers of industrial
    explosives if the number is contained in a barcode or matrix code
    that is printed on the packaging or container;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} containers holding industrial explosives in bulk;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} intermediate bulk containers holding industrial
    explosives in bulk; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} plastic bags that meet the requirements of UN
    packaging codes 5H1, 5H2, 5H3 or 5H4.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- subsection (3)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply to intermediate bulk
  containers holding industrial explosives in bulk or containers holding
  industrial explosives in bulk.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 66](#1451577-1452648){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451577-1452648.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-224-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 224"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 12]{.HLabel1}[Blank Cartridges for Tools]{.HTitleText1} {#h-797861 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2016-75, s. 26

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[225]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-225 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of blank cartridges for tools (type
C.2). Division 1 sets out the rules for sellers, while Division 2 sets
out the rules for users.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-225-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 225"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[226]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-226 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who sells blank
      cartridges for tools to other distributors or to retailers,
      whether or not they sell to
      users. ([distributeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes
      the storage of blank cartridges for
      tools. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than a
      distributor, who sells blank cartridges for
      tools. ([détaillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributor or a
      retailer. ([vendeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires blank
      cartridges for tools for use. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, blank cartridges for
  tools are stored in a sales establishment, including a dwelling, if
  they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inside the sales establishment, whether or not they
    are in a storage unit or displayed for sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} outside the sales establishment in a storage unit
    that is used in operating the establishment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a licensed magazine that is either inside or
    outside the establishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 37
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400540){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400540.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-226-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 226"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797883 .Subheading}

#### [Acquisition for Sale]{.HTitleText3} {#h-797884 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributor and retailer

**[[227]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-227 .sectionLabel}** A distributor or
retailer may acquire, store and sell blank cartridges for tools. A
distributor or retailer who acquires blank cartridges for tools must
comply with this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 37
- SOR/2018-231, s. 29
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 67](#1451582-1452651){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451582-1452651.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-227-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 227"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Storage]{.HTitleText3} {#h-797889 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Seller

**[[228]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-228 .sectionLabel}** A seller must store
their blank cartridges for tools in a sales establishment and must
ensure that the requirements of sections 229 to 231 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 37
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 68](#1451584-1452654){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451584-1452654.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-228-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 228"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No display for sale

- **[[229]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-229
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Blank cartridges for tools must not
  be displayed for sale in a dwelling.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display for sale

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Blank cartridges for tools that are displayed for
  sale in a sales establishment other than a dwelling must be kept
  behind a sales counter or locked up (for example, in a cabinet).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Only people authorized by the retailer may have
  access to the area behind a sales counter.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-229-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 229"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[230]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-230 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2018-231, s. 30\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-230-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 230"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[231]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-231 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  blank cartridges for tools are stored in a dwelling, they must be
  stored away from flammable substances and sources of ignition, in a
  manner that protects them from theft and ensures that access to them
  is limited to people authorized by the retailer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When blank cartridges for tools are stored in a
  storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if propellant powder, percussion caps, small arms
    cartridges, unmixed reactive targets or black powder cartouches are
    stored with blank cartridges for tools in the storage unit, they
    must be stored separately from one another (for example, on
    different shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 69\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 37, 46(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400544){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400544.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 69](#1451586-1452665){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451586-1452665.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-231-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 231"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Sale]{.HTitleText3} {#h-797925 .Subheading}

**[[232]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2024-77, s. 70\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-232-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 232"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

**[[233]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233 .sectionLabel}** A retailer may sell
blank cartridges for tools only to users.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-233-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 233"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Rules for Users]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797937 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[234]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-234 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire and
store blank cartridges for tools. A user who acquires or stores blank
cartridges for tools must comply with this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 37
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 71](#1451589-1452670){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451589-1452670.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-234-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 234"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

**[[235]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-235 .sectionLabel}** A user must store their
blank cartridges for tools in a dwelling or a storage unit and must
ensure that the storage requirements of section 237 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 37
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400548){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400548.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 71](#1451589-1452673){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451589-1452673.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-235-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 235"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[236]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-236 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2018-231, s. 31\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-236-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 236"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[237]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  blank cartridges for tools are stored in a dwelling, they must be
  stored away from flammable substances and sources of ignition, in a
  manner that protects them from theft and ensures that access to them
  is limited to people authorized by the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When blank cartridges for tools are stored in a
  storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if propellant powder, percussion caps, small arms
    cartridges, unmixed reactive targets or black powder cartouches are
    stored with blank cartridges for tools in the storage unit, they
    must be stored separately from one another (for example, on
    different shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 72\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 37, 46(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400552){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}
