## [PART 13]{.HLabel1}[Special Purpose Explosives]{.HTitleText1} {#h-797968 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[238]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-238 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of special purpose explosives.
Division 1 sets out the rules for sellers and users of low-hazard
special purpose explosives (type S.1: for example, highway flares,
personal distress flares, bird scaring cartridges and fire extinguisher
cartridges). Division 2 sets out the rules for sellers and users of
high-hazard special purpose explosives (type S.2: for example, reactive
targets, explosive bolts and cable cutters). Division 3 deals with the
destruction of expired marine flares (type S.1 or S.2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-238-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 238"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[239]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-239 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who sells
      special purpose explosives to other distributors or to retailers,
      whether or not they sell to
      users. ([distributeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than a
      distributor, who sells special purpose
      explosives. ([détaillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributor or a
      retailer. ([vendeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires special
      purpose explosives for use. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, special purpose
  explosives are stored in a sales establishment if the explosives are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inside the sales establishment, whether in a
    storage unit or displayed for sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} outside the sales establishment in a storage unit
    that is used in operating the establishment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a licensed magazine that is either inside or
    outside the establishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400556){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400556.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-239-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 239"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Explosive quantity

**[[240]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-240 .sectionLabel}** A reference to the mass
of a special purpose explosive in this Part is a reference to its gross
mass (the mass of the explosives plus the mass of any packaging or
container).

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Low-hazard Special Purpose Explosives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-797987 .Subheading}

Definition of [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[241]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes the
storage of low-hazard special purpose explosives.

#### [Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-797990 .Subheading}

##### [Acquisition for Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-797991 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributor

- **[[242]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-242 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  distributor may acquire, store and sell low-hazard special purpose
  explosives if they hold a licence. A distributor who acquires
  low-hazard special purpose explosives must comply with this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer may acquire, store and sell low-hazard
  special purpose explosives, whether or not they hold a licence. A
  retailer who acquires low-hazard special purpose explosives must
  comply with this Division.

##### [Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-797997 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licensed seller

- **[[243]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-243 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller who holds a licence must store their low-hazard special purpose
  explosives in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlicensed retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer who does not hold a licence must store
  their low-hazard special purpose explosives in a sales establishment
  other than a dwelling and ensure that the requirements of sections 244
  to 246 are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display for sale

- **[[244]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Only
  highway flares, marine flares and personal distress flares may be
  displayed for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No more than 1 000 kg of flares may be displayed for
  sale at any one time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Flares that are displayed for sale must be kept
  behind a sales counter or locked up (for example, in a cabinet) unless
  the flares are in consumer packs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Only people authorized by the retailer may have
  access to the area behind a sales counter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lots

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Flares that are displayed for sale must be separated
  into lots of 25 kg or less. Each lot must be separated from the other
  lots by a fire break. The lots must be kept away from flammable
  substances and sources of ignition.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

- **[[245]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-245 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  more than 1 000 kg of low-hazard special purpose explosives may be
  stored in a sales establishment at any one time, including those that
  are displayed for sale. If the sales establishment is located in a
  building that contains a dwelling, no more than 100 kg may be stored
  in the establishment at any one time, including explosives that are
  displayed for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Low-hazard special purpose explosives that are not
  displayed for sale must be stored in a storage unit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400561){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400561.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-245-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 245"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

**[[246]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-246 .sectionLabel}** When low-hazard special
purpose explosives are stored in a storage unit,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located in a dry place, away
  from flammable substances and sources of ignition;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
  to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
  weather;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
  impede exit in case of fire;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
  must be kept unobstructed;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be constructed
  from a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} nothing other than special purpose explosives may be
  stored in the storage unit;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
  unlocked;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be kept clean, dry, organized
  and free of grit;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} any spill, leakage or other contamination in the
  storage unit must be cleaned up immediately;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of fire in
  or near the storage unit must be taken; and

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
  Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at least
  10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a symbol, at
  least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a clearly
  visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400566){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400566.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-246-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 246"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798032 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No sale from dwelling

**[[247]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not sell
low-hazard special purpose explosives from a dwelling.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[248]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more low-hazard special purpose explosives to a
  licensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by their licence to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed buyer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more low-hazard special
  purpose explosives to an unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized
  by this Division to store.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

**[[249]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-249 .sectionLabel}** A retailer may sell
low-hazard special purpose explosives only to a user.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[250]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-250 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of every sale of more than 100 kg of low-hazard special purpose
explosives. The record must be kept for two years after the date of the
sale and must include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licensed buyer, the licence number
  and expiry date;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the product name of each explosive sold and the name
  of the person who obtained its authorization;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of explosives sold under each product
  name; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400569){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400569.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-250-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 250"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Rules for Users]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798051 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[251]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire and
store low-hazard special purpose explosives, whether or not they hold a
licence. A user who acquires low-hazard special purpose explosives must
comply with this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

- **[[252]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their low-hazard special purpose
  explosives in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- unlicensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who does not hold a licence must store their
  low-hazard special purpose explosives in a dwelling or a storage unit
  and ensure that the requirements of sections 253 and 254 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400574){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400574.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-252-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 252"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

**[[253]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-253 .sectionLabel}** No more than 1 000 kg
of low-hazard special purpose explosives may be stored at any one time,
of which no more than 40 kg may be stored in a dwelling.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[254]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-254 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  low-hazard special purpose explosives are stored in a dwelling, they
  must be stored away from flammable substances and sources of ignition,
  in a manner that protects them from theft and ensures that access to
  them is limited to people authorized by the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When low-hazard special purpose explosives are stored
  in a storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located in a dry place,
    away from flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} nothing other than special purpose explosives may
    be stored in the storage unit;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be kept clean, dry, organized
    and free of grit;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any spill, leakage or other contamination in the
    storage unit must be cleaned up immediately;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of fire in
    or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400579){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400579.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-254-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 254"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[High-hazard Special Purpose Explosives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798077 .Subheading}

Definition of [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[255]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-255 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes the
storage of high-hazard special purpose explosives.

#### [Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798080 .Subheading}

##### [Acquisition for Sale and Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798081 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition for sale

**[[256]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-256 .sectionLabel}** A seller may acquire,
store and sell high-hazard special purpose explosives if they hold a
licence. A seller who acquires high-hazard special purpose explosives
must comply with this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

**[[257]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-257 .sectionLabel}** A seller must store
their high-hazard special purpose explosives in the magazine specified
in their licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display for sale

**[[258]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-258 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not
display high-hazard special purpose explosives for sale.

##### [Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798088 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[259]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-259 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more high-hazard special purpose explosives to a
  licensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by their licence to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed buyer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more high-hazard special
  purpose explosives to an unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized
  by this Division to store.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

**[[260]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-260 .sectionLabel}** A retailer may sell
high-hazard special purpose explosives only to a user.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[261]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-261 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of every sale of high-hazard special purpose explosives for two
years after the date of the sale. The record must include the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licensed buyer, the licence number
  and expiry date;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the product name of each explosive sold and the name
  of the person who obtained its authorization;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of explosives sold under each product
  name;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the number of the buyer's Firearms Possession and
  Acquisition Licence issued under the [Firearms Act](/eng/acts/F-11.6).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400582){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400582.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 73](#1451593-1452689){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451593-1452689.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-261-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 261"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Rules for Users]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798105 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[262]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire and
store high-hazard special purpose explosives, whether or not they hold a
licence. A user who acquires high-hazard special purpose explosives must
comply with this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition --- reactive targets

**[[262.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262.1 .sectionLabel}** A user who acquires
a reactive target must hold a Firearms Possession and Acquisition
Licence issued under the Firearms Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 74](#1451595-1452693){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451595-1452693.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Selling --- reactive targets

**[[262.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262.2 .sectionLabel}** A seller may sell
reactive targets only to a user who holds a Firearms Possession and
Acquisition Licence issued under the Firearms Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 74](#1451595-1452697){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451595-1452697.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

- **[[263]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-263 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their high-hazard special purpose
  explosives in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- unlicensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who does not hold a licence must store their
  high-hazard special purpose explosives in a storage unit and ensure
  that the requirements of sections 264 and 265 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400587){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400587.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-263-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 263"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

- **[[264]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-264 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  more than 20 kg of high-hazard special purpose explosives other than
  reactive targets may be stored at any one time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reactive targets

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No more than 6 kg of reactive targets may be stored
  at any one time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 75](#1451597-1452704){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451597-1452704.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-264-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 264"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

**[[265]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-265 .sectionLabel}** When high-hazard
special purpose explosives are stored in a storage unit,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from flammable
  substances and sources of ignition;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
  and dry;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
  to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
  weather;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
  unlocked;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
  impede exit in case of fire;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
  must be kept unobstructed;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from a
  non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} if propellant powder, percussion caps, blank
  cartridges for tools, small arms cartridges, unmixed reactive targets
  or black powder cartouches are stored together in the storage unit,
  they must be stored separately from one another (for example, on
  different shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} unless the special purpose explosives consist
  exclusively of unmixed reactive targets, nothing other than special
  purpose explosives may be stored in the storage unit;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
  ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
  Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at least
  10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a symbol, at
  least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a clearly
  visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400592){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400592.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 76](#1451599-1452716){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451599-1452716.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-265-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 265"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Marine Flares]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798128 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposal plan

- **[[266]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-266 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  distributor who sells marine flares, whether low-hazard or
  high-hazard, must implement the marine flare disposal plan included in
  their licence application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of flares

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A distributor must accept the return of any marine
  flares sold by them that have expired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The distributor must store any expired marine flares
  that are returned to them in the magazine specified in their licence
  and destroy them in a manner that does not increase the likelihood of
  an accidental ignition during or after the destruction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If any expired marine flares are returned during a
  calendar year, the distributor must keep for two years after the date
  of the return, a record that sets out the number of each type, whether
  type S.1 or S.2, that were returned and the number of each type that
  were destroyed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 77](#1451601-1452720){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451601-1452720.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-266-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 266"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 14]{.HLabel1}[Small Arms Cartridges, Propellant Powder and Percussion Caps]{.HTitleText1} {#h-798138 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[267]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-267 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of small arms cartridges and the
manufacture of small arms cartridges and black powder cartouches.
Division 1 sets out the rules for sellers and users of commercially
manufactured small arms cartridges (type C.1). It also sets out rules
for storing small arms cartridges that are manufactured under Division
2. Division 2 sets out the rules for sellers and users of propellant
powder (type P) and percussion caps and primers (type C.3) and for
manufacturers of small arms cartridges and black powder cartouches.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400596){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400596.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 78(E)](#1451603-1452723){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451603-1452723.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-267-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 267"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[268]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-268 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[black powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[black powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an explosive classified
      as type P.1. ([poudre noire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[propellant powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[propellant powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means black powder and
      smokeless powder. ([poudre propulsive]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[small arms cartridge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[small arms cartridge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cartridge
      that is designed to be used in small arms, has a calibre of no
      more than 19.1 mm (.75 calibre), is fitted with centre or rim fire
      priming and contains a propelling charge, with or without a solid
      projectile. It includes a shotgun shell of any gauge. ([cartouche
      pour armes de petit calibre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[smokeless powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[smokeless powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an explosive
      classified as type P.2. ([poudre sans fumée]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, small arms cartridges,
  propellant powder and percussion caps are stored in a sales
  establishment, including a dwelling, if they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inside the sales establishment, whether or not they
    are in a storage unit or displayed for sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} outside the sales establishment in a storage unit
    that is used in operating the establishment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a licensed magazine that is either inside or
    outside the establishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400600){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400600.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-268-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 268"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of cartridges, powder or
cartouches

**[[269]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-269 .sectionLabel}** A reference in this
Part to the mass of a small arms cartridge, propellant powder or a black
powder cartouche is a reference to its net quantity (the mass of the
explosive excluding the mass of any packaging, container, shell casing
or projectile).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400604){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400604.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-269-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 269"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Small Arms Cartridges]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798155 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[270]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-270 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Division.

[[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who sells small
    arms cartridges to other distributors or to retailers, whether or
    not they sell to users. ([distributeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes the
    storage of small arms cartridges. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than a
    distributor, who sells small arms
    cartridges. ([détaillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributor or a
    retailer. ([vendeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires small arms
    cartridges for use. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 145](#1451822-1453183){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451822-1453183.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-270-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 270"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798163 .Subheading}

##### [Acquisition for Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798164 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributor

- **[[271]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-271 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  distributor may acquire, store and sell small arms cartridges if they
  hold a licence. A distributor who acquires small arms cartridges must
  comply with this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer may acquire, store and sell small arms
  cartridges, whether or not they hold a licence. A retailer who
  acquires small arms cartridges must comply with this Division.

##### [Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798170 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licensed seller

- **[[272]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-272 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller who holds a licence must store their small arms cartridges in
  the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlicensed retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer who does not hold a licence must store
  their small arms cartridges in a sales establishment and must ensure
  that the requirements of sections 273 to 275 are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attendance

- **[[273]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-273 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  a sales establishment is unlocked, small arms cartridges that are
  displayed for sale must be attended, kept behind a sales counter or
  locked up (for example, in a cabinet).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Only people authorized by the retailer may have
  access to the area behind a sales counter.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400608){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400608.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-273-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 273"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

- **[[274]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-274 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  more than 225 kg of small arms cartridges may be stored in a sales
  establishment at any one time, including cartridges that are displayed
  for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Small arms cartridges that are not displayed for sale
  must be stored in a dwelling or a storage unit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400613){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400613.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 145](#1451822-1453185){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451822-1453185.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-274-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 274"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[275]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-275 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  small arms cartridges are stored in a dwelling, they must be stored
  away from flammable substances and sources of ignition. People not
  authorized by the retailer must not be given unlimited access to the
  cartridges.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When small arms cartridges are stored in a storage
  unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if propellant powder, percussion caps, blank
    cartridges for tools, unmixed reactive targets or black powder
    cartouches are stored with small arms cartridges in the storage
    unit, they must be stored separately from one another (for example,
    on different shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 79\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 79\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote

:::

##### [Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798203 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[276]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-276 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more small arms cartridges to a licensed buyer
  than the buyer is authorized by their licence to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed buyer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more small arms cartridges to
  an unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by this Division to
  store.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

**[[277]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-277 .sectionLabel}** A retailer may sell
small arms cartridges only to a user.

#### [Rules for Users]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798211 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[278]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-278 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire and
store small arms cartridges, whether or not they hold a licence. A user
who acquires small arms cartridges must comply with this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

- **[[279]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-279 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their small arms cartridges in the
  magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- unlicensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who does not hold a licence must store their
  small arms cartridges, including any small arms cartridges
  manufactured by the user under Division 2, in a dwelling or a storage
  unit and ensure that the requirements of sections 280 and 281 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400623){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400623.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-279-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 279"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

**[[280]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-280 .sectionLabel}** No more than 225 kg of
small arms cartridges may be stored at any one time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 145](#1451822-1453188){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451822-1453188.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-280-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 280"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[281]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-281 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  small arms cartridges are stored in a dwelling, they must be stored
  away from flammable substances and sources of ignition. People not
  authorized by the user must not be given unlimited access to the
  cartridges.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When small arms cartridges are stored in a storage
  unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if propellant powder, percussion caps, blank
    cartridges for tools, unmixed reactive targets or black powder
    cartouches are stored with small arms cartridges in the storage
    unit, they must be stored separately from one another (for example,
    on different shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition or a fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 80\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 80\]]{.Repealed}

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Propellant Powder and Percussion Caps and the Manufacture of Small Arms Cartridges and Black Powder Cartouches]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798238 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[282]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-282 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Division.

  [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who sells
      propellant powder or percussion caps to other distributors or to
      retailers, whether or not they sell to
      users. ([distributeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence issued under the
      [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17) that authorizes the storage of
      the explosive, whether propellant powder, percussion caps or black
      powder cartouches, that are to be sold, acquired or
      manufactured. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than a
      distributor, who sells propellant powder or percussion
      caps. ([détaillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributor or a
      retailer. ([vendeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires
      propellant powder or percussion caps for
      use. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Propellant powder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in this Division to a mass of propellant
  powder does not include propellant powder that is in a small arms
  cartridge, percussion cap or primer.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400632){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400632.html"
  role="button"}

  role="button"}

#### [Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798249 .Subheading}

##### [Acquisition for Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798250 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributor

- **[[283]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-283 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  distributor may acquire, store and sell propellant powder and
  percussion caps if they hold a licence. A distributor who acquires
  propellant powder or percussion caps must comply with this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer may acquire, store and sell propellant
  powder and percussion caps, whether or not they hold a licence. A
  retailer who acquires propellant powder or percussion caps must comply
  with this Division.

##### [Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798256 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licensed seller

- **[[284]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-284 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller who holds a licence must store their propellant powder and
  percussion caps in the magazine specified in their licence and ensure
  that the requirements of section 286 are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not store propellant powder and
  percussion caps in the same magazine.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 82](#1451611-1452755){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451611-1452755.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-284-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 284"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlicensed retailer

**[[285]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-285 .sectionLabel}** A retailer who does not
hold a licence must store their propellant powder and percussion caps in
a sales establishment and must ensure that the requirements of sections
286 to 288 are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display for sale --- propellant powder

- **[[286]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-286 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  more than 12 kg of propellant powder, of which no more than 500 g may
  be black powder, may be displayed for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Size of container

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Propellant powder that is displayed for sale must be
  in a container that holds no more than 500 g.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display for sale --- percussion caps

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No more than 10 000 percussion caps may be displayed
  for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Original packaging

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Percussion caps that are displayed for sale must be
  in their original packaging.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Propellant powder and percussion caps that are
  displayed for sale must be kept behind a counter or locked up (for
  example, in a cabinet).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Only people authorized by the retailer may have
  access to the area behind a sales counter.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Original packaging

**[[286.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-286.1 .sectionLabel}** A seller may sell
percussion caps only if they are in their original packaging.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 83](#1451613-1452761){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451613-1452761.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of storage

- **[[287]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-287
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Propellant powder and percussion
  caps that are not displayed for sale must be stored in a dwelling or a
  storage unit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Original packaging

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Percussion caps must be stored in their original
  packaging.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Original container

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Black powder and smokeless powder must be stored in
  their original containers. No more than 500 g of black powder may be
  stored in a container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detached dwellings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The maximum quantity of propellant powder that may be
  stored at any one time in a detached dwelling, or in a storage unit
  attached to a detached dwelling, is 25 kg of which no more than 10 kg
  may be black powder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other dwellings --- smokeless powder

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum quantity of smokeless powder that may be
  stored at any one time in a dwelling other than a detached dwelling,
  or in a storage unit attached to a dwelling other than a detached
  dwelling, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 20 kg, if all the smokeless powder is in containers
    that hold no more than 1 kg; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 5 kg, if any of the smokeless powder is in a
    container that holds more than 1 kg.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other dwellings --- black powder

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The maximum quantity of black powder that may be
  stored at any one time in a dwelling other than a detached dwelling,
  or in a storage unit attached to a dwelling other than a detached
  dwelling, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 1 kg, with no more than 500 g of black powder per
    container, if the black powder is in original containers; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 3 kg less any quantity that is in containers, if
    the black powder is in small arms cartridges or black powder
    cartouches.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detached storage unit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The maximum quantity of black powder and smokeless
  powder that may be stored at any one time in storage units that are
  not attached to a dwelling, whether in a single unit or in several, is
  25 kg and 75 kg, respectively.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400636){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400636.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 84](#1451615-1452767){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451615-1452767.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 145](#1451822-1453192){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451822-1453192.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-287-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 287"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[288]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-288 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  propellant powder or percussion caps are stored in a dwelling, they
  must be stored away from flammable substances and sources of ignition.
  People not authorized by the retailer must not be given unlimited
  access to the propellant powder or percussion caps.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When propellant powder or percussion caps are stored
  in a storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if small arms cartridges, blank cartridges for
    tools, unmixed reactive targets or black powder cartouches are
    stored with propellant powder or percussion caps in the storage
    unit, they must be stored separately from one another (for example,
    on different shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 85\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 85\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400641){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400641.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 85](#1451622-1452778){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451622-1452778.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-288-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 288"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer of powder

**[[289]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-289 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not
transfer propellant powder from one container to another for the purpose
of sale unless they hold a licence that authorizes them to do so.

##### [Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798313 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of Chief Inspector

**[[290]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-290 .sectionLabel}** A retailer who does not
hold a licence must, before beginning to sell propellant powder, send
the Chief Inspector of Explosives a written notice that sets out their
name, address, telephone number and email address and the date on which
they will begin to sell. If such a retailer stops selling propellant
powder, they must send the Chief Inspector a written notice to that
effect as soon as the circumstances permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 86](#1451624-1452785){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451624-1452785.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-290-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 290"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[291]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-291 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2024-77, s. 86\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-291-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 291"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[292]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-292 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more propellant powder or percussion caps to a
  licensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by their licence to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed
  retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more propellant powder to an
  unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by this Division to
  store.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

**[[293]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-293 .sectionLabel}** A retailer may sell
propellant powder or percussion caps only to a user.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification

- **[[294]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-294
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before selling propellant powder to
  a buyer, the seller must require the buyer to provide their valid
  fireworks operator certificate --- pyrotechnician or Firearms
  Possession and Acquisition Licence issued under the [Firearms
  Act](/eng/acts/F-11.6) --- or, in the case of buyer buying on behalf
  of a public service agency, the public agency identification number
  assigned under paragraph 7(1)(a) of the [Public Agents Firearms
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-98-203) --- as well as to establish
  their identity by providing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a piece of identification, issued by the Government
    of Canada or a provincial, municipal or foreign government, that
    bears a photograph of the buyer; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} two pieces of identification, each of which sets
    out the buyer's name, at least one of which is issued by the
    Government of Canada or a provincial, municipal or foreign
    government and at least one of which sets out the buyer's address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification of identity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the buyer provides a piece of identification that
  bears a photograph, the seller must ensure, before selling the
  propellant powder, that the photograph is that of the buyer.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 87](#1451626-1452793){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451626-1452793.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-294-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 294"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[295]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-295 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of each sale of propellant powder for two years after the date of
the sale. The record must include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and if applicable, the number of the
  licence issued to them under the [Firearms Act](/eng/acts/F-11.6);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licensed buyer, the licence number
  and expiry date;

- [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} the number and expiry date of the buyer's fireworks
  operator certificate --- pyrotechnician, if applicable;

- [(b.2)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the name of the public service
  agency, the name of the buyer buying on behalf of the public service
  agency and the number of the buyer's public agency identification
  number assigned under the [Public Agents Firearms
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-98-203);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the type and product name of the powder sold, the
  size of the container in which it was sold and the name of the person
  who obtained its authorization;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of powder sold under each product name;
  and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)

  role="button"}
