#### [Rules for Users]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798341 .Subheading}

##### [Acquisition]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798342 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[296]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-296 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire and
store propellant powder and percussion caps, whether or not they hold a
licence. A user may manufacture small arms cartridges and black powder
cartouches for their own personal use and may store them, whether or not
they hold a licence. A user who acquires propellant powder or percussion
caps or manufactures small arms cartridges or black powder cartouches
must comply with this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400648){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400648.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 145](#1451822-1453194){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451822-1453194.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-296-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 296"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798345 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licensed user

- **[[297]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-297 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their propellant powder and black
  powder cartouches in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user must not store propellant powder and
  percussion caps in the same magazine.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 89](#1451633-1452795){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451633-1452795.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-297-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 297"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlicensed user

**[[298]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-298 .sectionLabel}** A user who does not
hold a licence must store their propellant powder, percussion caps and
black powder cartouches in a dwelling or a storage unit and ensure that
the requirements of sections 299 to 304 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400652){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400652.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 145](#1451822-1453196){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451822-1453196.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-298-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 298"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Percussion caps

- **[[299]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-299
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Percussion caps must be stored in
  their original packaging.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Smokeless powder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Smokeless powder must be stored in its original
  container or in small arms cartridges.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Black powder

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Black powder must be stored in its original container
  with no more than 500 g of black powder per container, in small arms
  cartridges or in black powder cartouches.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400656){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400656.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 90](#1451635-1452798){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451635-1452798.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 145](#1451822-1453198){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451822-1453198.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-299-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 299"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

**[[300]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-300 .sectionLabel}** The maximum quantity of
propellant powder that may be stored by a user at any one time under
sections 301 to 303 is reduced by the quantity of any propellant powder
that the user is storing under section 375 and any quantity that they
are storing under section 389.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detached dwellings

**[[301]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-301 .sectionLabel}** The maximum quantity of
propellant powder that may be stored at any one time in a detached
dwelling, or in a storage unit attached to a detached dwelling, is 25 kg
of which no more than 10 kg may be black powder.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400660){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400660.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-301-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 301"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other dwellings --- smokeless powder

- **[[302]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-302 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  maximum quantity of smokeless powder that may be stored at any one
  time in a dwelling other than a detached dwelling, or in a storage
  unit attached to a dwelling other than a detached dwelling, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 20 kg, if all the smokeless powder is in containers
    that hold no more than 1 kg; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 5 kg, if any of the smokeless powder is in a
    container that holds more than 1 kg.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other dwellings --- black powder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum quantity of black powder that may be
  stored at any one time in a dwelling other than a detached dwelling,
  or in a storage unit attached to a dwelling other than a detached
  dwelling, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 1 kg, if the black powder is in containers; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 3 kg less any quantity that is in containers, if
    the black powder is in small arms cartridges or black powder
    cartouches.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detached storage unit

**[[303]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-303 .sectionLabel}** The maximum quantity of
black powder or smokeless powder that may be stored at any one time in
storage units that are not attached to a dwelling, whether in a single
unit or in several, is 25 kg and 75 kg, respectively.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400664){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400664.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 91](#1451637-1452801){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451637-1452801.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-303-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 303"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[304]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  propellant powder, percussion caps or black powder cartouches are
  stored in a dwelling, they must be stored away from flammable
  substances and sources of ignition. People not authorized by the user
  must not be given unlimited access to the propellant powder,
  percussion caps or black powder cartouches.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When propellant powder, percussion caps or black
  powder cartouches are stored in a storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if small arms cartridges, blank cartridges for
    tools or unmixed reactive targets are stored with propellant powder,
    percussion caps or black powder cartouches in the storage unit, they
    must be stored separately from one another (for example, on
    different shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of
    ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 92\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 92\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 32(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400668){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400668.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 92](#1451639-1452812){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451639-1452812.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-304-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 304"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Manufacture]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798394 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Age

- **[[305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-305 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who manufactures small arms cartridges or black powder
  cartouches must be at least 18 years old or under the supervision of a
  person who is at least 18 years old.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who manufactures small arms cartridges or
  black powder cartouches must ensure that the following requirements
  are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the place where the manufacturing is carried out
    must have a means of escape that will permit all people in the place
    to leave it quickly and easily in an emergency;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition must be taken;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all containers of explosives must be labelled to
    identify their contents and must be kept closed when not in use;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} no more than 2 kg of smokeless powder may be within
    1 m of the loading area;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} no more than 500 g of black powder may be within 1
    m of the loading area;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the small arms cartridges must not include an
    incendiary or similar military component or device; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} no more than 150 percussion caps may be kept in the
    loader mechanism of the reloading equipment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification of explosives

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of transporting small arms
  cartridges or black powder cartouches manufactured under this
  Division, the small arms cartridges are classified as UN 0012 and the
  black powder cartouches are classified as UN 0014.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400672){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400672.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-305-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 305"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 15]{.HLabel1}[Model and High-Power Rocket Motors]{.HTitleText1} {#h-798409 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-306 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of rocket motors, reloading kits and
igniters. Division 1 sets out the rules for sellers and users of model
rocket motors (type R.1), model rocket motor reloading kits (type R.1)
and igniters for model rocket motors (type R.3). Division 2 sets out the
rules for sellers and users of high-power rocket motors (type R.2),
high-power rocket motor reloading kits (type R.2) and igniters for
high-power rocket motors (type R.3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[307]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-307 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who sells
      rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters to other distributors or
      to retailers, whether or not they sell to
      users. ([distributeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes
      storage of the type of rocket motor, reloading kit or igniter to
      be sold or acquired. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[high-power rocket motor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[high-power rocket motor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      recreational rocket motor with an impulse that is produced by
      combustion of a solid propellent and exceeds 160 newton-seconds
      but does not exceed 40 960 newton-seconds. ([moteur de fusée haute
      puissance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[model rocket motor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[model rocket motor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a recreational
      rocket motor with an impulse that is produced by combustion of a
      solid propellent and does not exceed 160 newton-seconds. ([moteur
      de fusée miniature]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reloading kit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reloading kit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a package that
      contains a solid propellant and other components that are designed
      to be used in a reloadable rocket motor. ([trousse de
      rechargement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than a
      distributor, who sells rocket motors, reloading kits or
      igniters. ([détaillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributor or a
      retailer. ([vendeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires rocket
      motors, reloading kits or igniters for
      use. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, rocket motors,
  reloading kits and igniters are stored in a sales establishment,
  including a dwelling, if they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inside the sales establishment, whether or not they
    are in a storage unit or displayed for sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} outside the sales establishment in a storage unit
    that is used in operating the establishment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a licensed magazine that is either inside or
    outside the establishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400676){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400676.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 93(F)](#1451641-1452815){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451641-1452815.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-307-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 307"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of motors and kits

**[[308]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-308 .sectionLabel}** A reference to the mass
of a rocket motor or reloading kit in this Part is a reference to its
gross mass (the mass of the motor or kit plus the mass of any packaging
or container).

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Model Rocket Motors]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798430 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Motor rockets, kits and igniters

**[[309]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-309 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
unless otherwise indicated, a reference to a rocket motor, a reloading
kit or an igniter is a reference to a model rocket motor, a reloading
kit for a model rocket motor or an igniter for a model rocket motor.

#### [Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798433 .Subheading}

##### [Acquisition for Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798434 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributor

- **[[310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-310 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  distributor may acquire, store and sell rocket motors, reloading kits
  and igniters if they hold a licence. A distributor who acquires rocket
  motors, reloading kits or igniters must comply with this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer may acquire, store and sell rocket motors,
  reloading kits and igniters, whether or not they hold a licence. A
  retailer who acquires rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters must
  comply with this Division.

##### [Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798440 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licensed seller

- **[[311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-311 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller who holds a licence must store their rocket motors, reloading
  kits and igniters in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlicensed retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer who does not hold a licence must store
  their rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters in a sales
  establishment and must ensure that the requirements of sections 312 to
  315 are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display for sale prohibited

- **[[312]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Rocket motors, reloading kits and
  igniters must not be displayed for sale in a dwelling.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a sales establishment that is not a
  dwelling, no more than 25 kg of rocket motors and reloading kits
  (combined quantity) and no more than 300 igniters may be displayed for
  sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters that are
  displayed for sale must be kept behind a sales counter or locked up
  (for example, in a cabinet) unless they are in consumer packs that
  meet the requirements of section 313.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Only people authorized by the retailer may have
  access to the area behind a sales counter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separation of motors, kits and igniters

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} When displayed for sale, rocket motors and reloading
  kits that are not in consumer packs must be separated by a fire break,
  or kept at least 1 m , from igniters that are not in consumer packs.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer packs

**[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Division, a consumer pack must meet the following requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be of sufficient strength to withstand normal
  handling;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be designed so that it prevents a person who
  is handling it from being able to ignite the rocket motors, reloading
  kits or igniters it contains; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must be designed so that it prevents any shifting
  of the rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters during handling or
  transportation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

- **[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  more than 200 kg of rocket motors and reloading kits (combined
  quantity) and no more than 2 500 igniters may be stored at any one
  time, including those that are displayed for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters that are
  not displayed for sale must be stored in a dwelling or a storage unit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rocket with motor installed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A model rocket in which a motor has been installed
  must not be stored.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Heat or dampness

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters must not
  be exposed to heat or dampness that could cause them to deteriorate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400681){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400681.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-314-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 314"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters are stored in a dwelling,
  they must be stored away from flammable substances and sources of
  ignition, in a manner that protects them from theft and ensures that
  access to them is limited to people authorized by the retailer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters are
  stored in a storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located in a dry place,
    away from flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be
    constructed from a non-sparking material (for example, wood or
    painted metal);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} nothing other than rocket motors, reloading kits
    and igniters may be stored in the storage unit;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters
    are not in consumer packs, the motors and kits must be stored
    separately from the igniters (for example, on different shelves or
    separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be kept clean, dry, organized
    and free of grit;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} any spill, leakage or other contamination in the
    storage unit must be cleaned up immediately;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of fire in
    or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400686){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400686.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 315"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798488 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters to
  a licensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by their licence to
  store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed buyer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more rocket motors, reloading
  kits or igniters to an unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized
  by this Division to store.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

**[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317 .sectionLabel}** A retailer may sell
rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters only to a user.

#### [Rules for Users]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798496 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

- **[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who is at least 18 years old may acquire and store rocket motors,
  reloading kits and igniters, whether or not they hold a licence. A
  user who acquires rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters must
  comply with this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition --- at least 12 years old

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who is at least 12 years old may acquire and
  store single use rocket motors with an impulse that does not exceed 80
  newton-seconds and igniters for those motors without a licence. A user
  who acquires such motors or igniters must comply with this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

- **[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their rocket motors, reloading
  kits and igniters in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- unlicensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who does not hold a licence must store their
  rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters in a dwelling or a storage
  unit and must ensure that the requirements of sections 320 and 321 are
  met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400691){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400691.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-319-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 319"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

- **[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  more than 200 kg of rocket motors and reloading kits (combined
  quantity) and no more than 2 500 igniters may be stored at any one
  time. If high-power rocket motors or reloading kits for high-power
  rocket motors are stored with rocket motors or reloading kits, the
  combined quantity must not exceed 200 kg. If igniters for high-power
  rocket motors are stored with igniters, the combined quantity must not
  exceed 2 500.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- under 18 years old

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who is less than 18 years old may store no
  more than 6 single use rocket motors with an impulse that does not
  exceed 80 newton-seconds, and no more than 10 igniters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of storage

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters must be
  stored in a dwelling or a storage unit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rocket with motor installed

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A model rocket in which the motor has been installed
  must not be stored.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400696){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400696.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-320-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 320"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters are stored in a dwelling,
  they must be stored away from flammable substances and sources of
  ignition, in a manner that protects them from theft and ensures that
  access to them is limited to people authorized by the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters are
  stored in a storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located in a dry place,
    away from flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} nothing other than high-power rocket motors and
    reloading kits and igniters for high-power rocket motors may be
    stored with the rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters,
    and any high-power rocket motors or reloading kits and igniters for
    high-power rocket motors, are not in consumer packs, the motors and
    kits must be stored separately from the igniters (for example, on
    different shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be kept clean, dry, organized
    and free of grit;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} any spill, leakage or other contamination in the
    storage unit must be cleaned up immediately;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of fire in
    or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400701){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400701.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink

Version](section-321-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 321"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[High-power Rocket Motors]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798533 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Motor rockets, kits and igniters

**[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
unless otherwise indicated, a reference to a rocket motor, a reloading
kit or an igniter is a reference to a high-power rocket motor, a
reloading kit for a high-power rocket motor or an igniter for a
high-power rocket motor.

#### [Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798536 .Subheading}

##### [Acquisition for Sale and Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798537 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition for sale

**[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** A seller may acquire,
store and sell rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters if they hold a
licence. A seller who acquires rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters
must comply with this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

**[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324 .sectionLabel}** A seller must store
their rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters in the magazine
specified in their licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No display for sale

**[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not
display rocket motors or reloading kits for sale.

##### [Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798544 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters to
  a licensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by their licence to
  store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed buyer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more rocket motors, reloading
  kits or igniters to an unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized
  by this Division to store.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

**[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** A retailer may sell
rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters only to a user.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of every sale of a rocket motor, reloading kit or igniter for two
years after the date of the sale. The record must include the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licensed buyer, the licence number
  and expiry date;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the type, product name and power level of each rocket
  motor and each reloading kit sold and the name of the person who
  obtained its authorization;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the product name of each igniter sold and the name of
  the person who obtained its authorization;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the number of motors, kits and igniters sold under
  each product name; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400704){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400704.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-328-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 328"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Rules for Users]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798562 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire and
store rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters, whether or not they
hold a licence. A user who acquires rocket motors, reloading kits or
igniters must comply with this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

- **[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their rocket motors, reloading
  kits and igniters in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- unlicensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who does not hold a licence must store their
  rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters in a dwelling or a storage
  unit and ensure that the requirements of sections 331 to 333 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400709){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400709.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-330-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 330"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- dwelling

- **[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  the case of storage in a dwelling, no more than 10 kg of rocket motors
  and reloading kits (combined quantity) and no more than 40 igniters
  may be stored at any one time. If model rocket motors or reloading
  kits for model rocket motors are stored with rocket motors or
  reloading kits, the combined quantity must not exceed 10 kg. If
  igniters for model rocket motors are stored with igniters, the
  combined quantity must not exceed 40.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of storage in a storage unit, no more
  than 200 kg of rocket motors and reloading kits (combined quantity)
  and no more than 200 igniters may be stored at any one time. If model
  rocket motors or reloading kits for model rocket motors are stored
  with rocket motors or reloading kits, the combined quantity must not
  exceed 200 kg. If igniters for model rocket motors are stored with
  igniters, the combined quantity must not exceed 200.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rocket with motor installed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A high-power rocket in which a motor has been
  installed must not be stored.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400714){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400714.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-331-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 331"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters are stored in a dwelling,
  they must be stored away from flammable substances and sources of
  ignition, in a manner that protects them from theft and ensures that
  access to them is limited to people authorized by the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When rocket motors, reloading kits or igniters are
  stored in a storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located in a dry place,
    away from flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} nothing other than model rocket motors and
    reloading kits and igniters for model rocket motors may be stored
    with the rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the rocket motors, reloading kits and igniters,
    and any model rocket motors or reloading kits or igniters for model
    rocket motors, are not in consumer packs, the motors and kits must
    be stored separately from the igniters (for example, on different
    shelves or separated by a wooden barrier);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be kept clean, dry, organized
    and free of grit;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} any spill, leakage or other contamination in the
    storage unit must be cleaned up immediately;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of fire in
    or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400719){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400719.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink

Version](section-332-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 332"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attendance

**[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333 .sectionLabel}** Rocket motors,
reloading kits and igniters must be attended when they are not in
storage.

::: HistoricalNote

## [PART 16]{.HLabel1}[Consumer Fireworks and Novelty Devices]{.HTitleText1} {#h-798596 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of consumer fireworks (type F.1) and
novelty devices (type F.5) and regulates their use. Division 1 sets out
rules for sellers and Division 2 sets out rules for users.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 94](#1451643-1452820){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451643-1452820.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-334-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 334"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[335]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-335 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who sells
      consumer fireworks or novelty devices to other distributors or to
      retailers, whether or not they sell to
      users. ([distributeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes
      the storage of consumer fireworks or novelty
      devices. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[novelty device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[novelty device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that
      produces limited visible or audible effects, contains small
      amounts of pyrotechnic or explosive composition and is classified
      as a type F.5 explosive. ([dispositif de
      fantaisie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[retailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than a
      distributor, who sells consumer fireworks or novelty
      devices. ([détaillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributor or a
      retailer. ([vendeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires consumer
      fireworks or novelty devices for
      use. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, consumer fireworks are
  stored in a sales establishment if they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inside the sales establishment, whether or not they
    are in a storage unit or displayed for sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} outside the sales establishment in a storage unit
    that is used in operating the establishment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a licensed magazine that is either inside or
    outside the establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- novelty devices

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, novelty devices are
  stored in a sales establishment if they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inside the sales establishment, whether or not they
    are in a storage unit or displayed for sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} outside the sales establishment in a storage unit
    that is used in operating the establishment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a licensed magazine that is either inside or
    outside the establishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400727){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400727.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 95](#1451645-1452831){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451645-1452831.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-335-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 335"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer fireworks or novelty devices ---
quantity

**[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** A reference to a mass
of consumer fireworks or a mass of novelty devices in this Part is a
reference to their gross mass (the mass of the consumer fireworks or the
mass of the novelty devices plus the mass of any packaging or
container).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 96](#1451654-1452835){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451654-1452835.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-336-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 336"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on use

**[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337 .sectionLabel}** Except as authorized by
this Part, it is prohibited for a person to use

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} consumer fireworks; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} novelty devices.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 96](#1451654-1452841){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451654-1452841.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-337-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 337"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798616 .Subheading}

#### [Acquisition for Sale]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798617 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributor

- **[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  distributor may acquire, store and sell consumer fireworks or novelty
  devices if they hold a licence. A distributor who acquires consumer
  fireworks or novelty devices must comply with this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer may acquire, store and sell consumer
  fireworks or novelty devices, whether or not they hold a licence. A
  retailer who acquires consumer fireworks or novelty devices must
  comply with this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 97](#1451656-1452848){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451656-1452848.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-338-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 338"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Sales Establishment]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798623 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No sale from dwelling

**[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not sell

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} consumer fireworks from a dwelling; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} novelty devices from a dwelling.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 98](#1451658-1452854){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451658-1452854.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-339-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 339"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sales establishment -- requirements

**[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** A seller must ensure
that their sales establishment meets the following requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it has at least two unobstructed exits;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} all aisles containing consumer fireworks are at least
  1.2 m wide and not blocked at either end; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all aisles containing novelty devices are at least
  1.2 m wide and not blocked at either end.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 98](#1451658-1452861){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451658-1452861.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-340-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 340"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retail sales establishment

- **[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  sales establishment of a retailer who does not hold a licence may be
  permanent (located in a permanent structure) or temporary (located in
  a tent, trailer or other temporary shelter).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Whether the establishment is permanent or temporary,
  the retailer must ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sales establishment is protected from
    unauthorized access when it is not open for business; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all places where consumer fireworks or novelty
    devices are stored, whether inside or outside the establishment,
    must be at least 100 m from all above-ground storage tanks for
    flammable substances in bulk and at least 8 m from the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} fuel dispensers at a fuel dispensing station,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} retail propane-dispensing tanks and cylinders,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} above-ground storage tanks for flammable
      substances, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} dispensing facilities for compressed natural
      gas.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary sales establishment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the sales establishment is temporary, the retailer
  must also ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all places where consumer fireworks are stored,
    whether inside or outside the establishment, are at least 8 m from
    all flammable materials, sources of ignition, thoroughfares,
    buildings or other temporary sales establishments and at least 3 m
    from any vehicle parking area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the consumer fireworks are attended at all times;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the sales establishment selling consumer
    fireworks or novelty devices is a tent, the tent is made from
    flame-retardant material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 28(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400730){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400730.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 99](#1451660-1452865){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451660-1452865.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-341-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 341"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Storage]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798646 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence holder

- **[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller who holds a licence must store all their consumer fireworks in
  the magazine specified in their licence and ensure that the
  requirements respecting consumer fireworks in sections 343 to 345,
  345.2 and 346 are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlicensed retailer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retailer who does not hold a licence must store
  their consumer fireworks in a sales establishment other than a
  dwelling and ensure that the requirements respecting consumer
  fireworks in sections 343 to 345, 345.2, 346, 348 and 349 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 100](#1451667-1452872){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451667-1452872.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-342-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 342"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Novelty devices unlicensed retailer

**[[342.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342.1 .sectionLabel}** A retailer must
store their novelty devices in a sales establishment other than a
dwelling and ensure that the requirements respecting novelty devices in
sections 345.1, 345.2, 346.1, 348 and 349 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 100](#1451667-1452876){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451667-1452876.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Handling

**[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** Consumer fireworks may
be handled by a buyer only after they have been sold, unless they are in
consumer packs that meet the requirements of section 345 or in packaging
or containers that comply with the safety standards for means of
containment under the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods Act,
1992](/eng/acts/T-19.01).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-aerial consumer fireworks

- **[[344]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Non-aerial consumer fireworks
  (flares, fountains, snakes, ground spinners, strobe pots, wheels and
  ground whistles) may be displayed for sale only if they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} kept behind a sales counter;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} locked up (for example, in a cabinet);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in consumer packs that meet the requirements of
    section 345; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in packaging or containers that comply with the
    safety standards for means of containment under the [Transportation
    of Dangerous Goods Act, 1992](/eng/acts/T-19.01).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Aerial consumer fireworks

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Aerial consumer fireworks may be displayed for sale
  only if they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} kept behind a sales counter;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} locked up (for example, in a cabinet); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in packaging or containers that comply with the
    safety standards for means of containment under the [Transportation
    of Dangerous Goods Act, 1992](/eng/acts/T-19.01).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-aerial consumer and aerial consumer
  fireworks

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Non-aerial consumer and aerial consumer fireworks may
  be displayed for sale only if they are displayed in accordance with
  section 346.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-211, s. 506
- SOR/2016-75, s. 29
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 101(E)](#1451669-1452883){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451669-1452883.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-344-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 344"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adequate consumer pack --- fireworks

**[[345]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Part, a consumer pack of consumer fireworks must meet the following
requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be of sufficient strength to withstand normal
  handling;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be designed so that it prevents a person who
  is handling it from being able to ignite the consumer fireworks it
  contains;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must be designed so that it prevents any shifting
  of the consumer fireworks during handling or transportation; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the product name of all consumer fireworks in the
  pack must be printed on it, along with the words "Non-aerial
  Fireworks/[Pièces pyrotechniques non aériennes]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}", in a location that is clearly visible.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, s. 44(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400733){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400733.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 102](#1451676-1452887){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451676-1452887.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-345-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 345"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adequate consumer pack --- novelty
devices

**[[345.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Part, a consumer pack of novelty devices must meet the following
requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be of sufficient strength to withstand normal
  handling;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be designed so that it prevents a person who
  is handling it from being able to ignite the novelty devices it
  contains; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must be designed so that it prevents any shifting
  of the novelty devices during handling or transportation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 103](#1451681-1452894){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451681-1452894.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reuse of packaging

**[[345.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345.2 .sectionLabel}** A seller must
ensure that any packaging or container that has been used for consumer
fireworks or novelty devices is not reused unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transport classification of the consumer
  fireworks or novelty devices to be transported remains unchanged;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the consumer fireworks or novelty devices to be
  transported are arranged in the packaging or container so as to
  minimize their movement during transport;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the packaging or container is closed for transport in
  the same manner as it was for its first use;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the mass of the consumer fireworks or novelty devices
  is equal to or less than the mass of the consumer fireworks or novelty
  devices during the first use of the packaging or container; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the packaging or container shows no signs of damage,
  contamination or reduced strength.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 103](#1451681-1452903){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451681-1452903.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for display

**[[346]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346 .sectionLabel}** When consumer fireworks
are displayed for sale, the following requirements must be met:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} non-aerial fireworks in consumer packs that meet the
  requirements of section 345 or in packaging or containers that comply
  with the safety standards for means of containment under the
  [Transportation of Dangerous Goods Act, 1992](/eng/acts/T-19.01) must
  be separated into lots of 100 kg or less;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} aerial fireworks in packaging or containers that
  comply with the safety standards for means of containment under the
  [Transportation of Dangerous Goods Act, 1992](/eng/acts/T-19.01) must
  be separated into lots of 100 kg or less;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all other fireworks, whether aerial or non-aerial,
  must be separated into lots of 25 kg or less;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} each lot must be separated from the other lots by a
  fire break;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the fireworks must be kept away from flammable
  substances and sources of ignition;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the fireworks must not be exposed to heat or dampness
  that might cause them to deteriorate;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the fireworks must be separated from the ceiling and
  from any fire prevention system by at least 0.6 m;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} only people authorized by the retailer may have
  access to the area behind a sales counter;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} smoking must be prohibited within 8 m of the
  fireworks; and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} the fireworks must be attended when the sales
  establishment is unlocked.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400737){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400737.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-346-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 346"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Novelty devices --- displayed for sale

**[[346.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346.1 .sectionLabel}** When novelty
devices are displayed for sale, the following requirements must be met:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the novelty devices must be separated into lots of
  25 kg or less;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} each lot must be separated from the other lots by a
  fire break;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the novelty devices must be kept away from flammable
  substances and sources of ignition; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the novelty devices must not be exposed to heat or
  dampness that might cause them to deteriorate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 104](#1451683-1452911){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451683-1452911.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[347]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-347 .sectionLabel}** Sections 345.1 and
346.1 do not apply to sparklers or toy pistol caps.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 104](#1451683-1452915){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451683-1452915.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-347-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 347"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

- **[[348]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-348 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  more than 1 000 kg of consumer fireworks or novelty devices may be
  stored in a sales establishment at any one time, including consumer
  fireworks or novelty devices that are displayed for sale. If the sales
  establishment is located in a building that contains a dwelling, no
  more than 100 kg of consumer fireworks or novelty devices may be
  stored at any one time, including consumer fireworks or novelty
  devices that are displayed for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of storage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Consumer fireworks or novelty devices that are not
  displayed for sale must be stored in a storage unit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400742){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400742.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 104](#1451683-1452921){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451683-1452921.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-348-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 348"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

**[[349]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-349 .sectionLabel}** When consumer fireworks
or novelty devices are stored in a storage unit,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from flammable
  substances and sources of ignition;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
  and dry;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
  to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
  weather;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when unlocked;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
  impede exit in case of fire;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
  must be kept unobstructed;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
  ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
  Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at least
  10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a symbol, at
  least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a clearly
  visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400747){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400747.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 104](#1451683-1452932){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451683-1452932.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-349-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 349"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Sale]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798710 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[350]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-350 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more consumer fireworks to a licensed buyer than
  the buyer is authorized by their licence to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed buyer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more consumer fireworks or
  novelty devices to an unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by
  this Division to store.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 105](#1451685-1452936){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451685-1452936.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-350-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 350"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retailer

**[[351]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-351 .sectionLabel}** A retailer may sell
only to users.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of rules

- **[[352]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-352 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  distributor who sells consumer fireworks to a retailer must offer the
  retailer a copy of this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Table

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller who sells consumer fireworks to a user must
  offer the user either a copy of the table at the end of this Part or a
  document that includes the same information. The copy of the table or
  the document may be provided by electronic means, such as a QR code.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 106\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 106](#1451687-1452940){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451687-1452940.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-352-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 352"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[353]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-353 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of every sale of 150 kg or more of consumer fireworks or novelty
devices, or of consumer fireworks and novelty devices combined, for two
years after the date of the sale. The record must include the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licensed buyer, the licence number
  and expiry date;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the product name of each consumer firework or novelty
  device sold and the name of the person who obtained its authorization;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of consumer fireworks or novelty devices
  sold under each product name;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a sale by a distributor, an indication
  of whether the consumer fireworks or novelty devices were purchased
  for re-sale or for use; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400750){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400750.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 107](#1451689-1452946){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451689-1452946.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-353-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 353"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Rules for Users]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798735 .Subheading}

#### [Acquisition and Storage]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798736 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

- **[[354]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-354 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who is at least 18 years old may acquire, store and use consumer
  fireworks, whether or not they hold a licence. A user who acquires
  consumer fireworks must comply with this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition --- novelty devices

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A user who is at least 16 years old may acquire,
  store and use novelty devices, whether or not they hold a licence. A
  user who acquires novelty devices must comply with this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Toy pistol caps

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who is less than 16 years old may acquire and
  use toy pistol caps.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 108](#1451696-1452952){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451696-1452952.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-354-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 354"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

- **[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their consumer fireworks in the
  magazine specified in the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- unlicensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who does not hold a licence must store their
  consumer fireworks in a dwelling or a storage unit and ensure that the
  requirements of sections 356 and 357 are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage of novelty devices ---
  unlicensed user

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A user who does not hold a licence must store their
  novelty devices in a dwelling or a storage unit and ensure that the
  requirements of sections 356 and 357 respecting novelty devices are
  met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400755){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400755.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 109](#1451698-1452955){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451698-1452955.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-355-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 355"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- dwelling

- **[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  maximum quantity of consumer fireworks or novelty devices, or of
  consumer fireworks and novelty devices combined, that may be stored at
  any one time in a dwelling is 10 kg.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum quantity of consumer fireworks or novelty
  devices that may be stored at any one time in storage units, whether
  in a single unit or in several, is as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 1 000 kg of consumer fireworks;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 1 000 kg of novelty devices; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 1 000 kg of consumer fireworks and novelty devices
    combined.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400760){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400760.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 110](#1451700-1452964){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451700-1452964.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-356-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 356"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[357]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-357 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  consumer fireworks or novelty devices are stored in a dwelling, they
  must be stored in accordance with package instructions, located in a
  clean, dry place, away from flammable substances and sources of
  ignition and in a manner that ensures that access to them is limited
  to people authorized by the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When consumer fireworks or novelty devices are stored
  in a storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400765){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400765.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 110](#1451700-1452978){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451700-1452978.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-357-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 357"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Use]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798768 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions --- consumer fireworks

- **[[358]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  using consumer fireworks, a user, other than a certified display
  supervisor or a display assistant under the direct supervision of a
  certified display supervisor, must follow the instructions provided on
  the label of the consumer fireworks, and if there are no such
  instructions, the consumer fireworks must not be used except by a
  certified display supervisor or a display assistant under the direct
  supervision of a certified display supervisor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions --- novelty devices

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} When using novelty devices, a user must follow the
  instructions on the packaging, and if there are no such instructions,
  the novelty devices must not be used.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electric match

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user, other than a certified display supervisor or
  a display assistant under the direct supervision of a certified
  display supervisor, must not use an electric match to fire consumer
  fireworks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No smoking

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A user must not smoke, and must prohibit all others
  from smoking, within 8 m of the site of use of the fireworks.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 30
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 111](#1451702-1452985){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451702-1452985.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-358-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 358"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer fireworks --- electric match

- **[[358.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A certified display supervisor or a
  display assistant under the direct supervision of a certified display
  supervisor may use an electric match to fire consumer fireworks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer fireworks --- hybrid show

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Consumer fireworks may be used by a certified display
  supervisor or by a display assistant under the direct supervision of a
  certified display supervisor as a component of a hybrid show
  containing both consumer fireworks and display fireworks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fireworks accessories (type F.4) ---
  hybrid show

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Fireworks accessories (type F.4) may be used by a
  certified display supervisor or by a display assistant under the
  direct supervision of a certified display supervisor to fire consumer
  fireworks as a component of a hybrid show containing both consumer
  fireworks and display fireworks.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 112](#1451704-1452994){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451704-1452994.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}User under 18 years old

- **[[359]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who is under the age of 18 may use consumer fireworks if they are
  supervised by a person who is at least 18 years old.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}User under 16 years old

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A user who is under the age of 16 may use novelty
  devices if they are supervised by a person who is at least 18 years
  old.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supervision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who acquires consumer fireworks may give
  them to a user who is under the age of 18 if the person ensures that
  the user is supervised by a person who is at least 18 years old.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supervision --- novelty devices

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who acquires novelty devices may give them
  to a user who is under the age of 16 if the person ensures that the
  user is supervised by a person who is at least 18 years old.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 113](#1451706-1453000){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451706-1453000.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-359-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 359"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reuse of packaging

**[[359.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359.1 .sectionLabel}** A user must ensure
that any packaging or container that has been used for consumer
fireworks or novelty devices is not reused unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transport classification of the consumer
  fireworks or novelty devices to be transported remains unchanged;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the consumer fireworks or novelty devices to be
  transported are arranged in the packaging or container so as to
  minimize their movement during transport;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the packaging or container is closed for transport in
  the same manner as it was for its first use;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the mass of the consumer fireworks or novelty devices
  is equal to or less than the mass of the consumer fireworks or novelty
  devices during the first use of the packaging or container; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the packaging or container shows no signs of damage,
  contamination or reduced strength.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 114](#1451711-1453009){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451711-1453009.html"
  role="button"}
:::
