<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">CHOOSE a wide, clear site
away from all obstacles. Refer to the safety instructions on the
fireworks label for minimum distances from spectators.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">CHOISIR un
emplacement spacieux, bien dégagé et loin de tout obstacle. Consulter
les consignes de sécurité sur l’étiquette des pièces pyrotechniques pour
connaître les distances minimales entre les pièces et les
spectateurs.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef003.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">DO NOT FIRE IN WINDY
CONDITIONS.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">NE PAS
METTRE À FEU LES PIÈCES PYROTECHNIQUES PAR TEMPS VENTEUX.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef004.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">READ all instructions on the
fireworks. PLAN the order of firing before you begin.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">LIRE toutes
les instructions sur les pièces pyrotechniques. DÉTERMINER l’ordre de
mise à feu avant de débuter.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef005.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">USE A GOOD FIRING BASE such as
a pail filled with earth or sand.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">UTILISER
UNE BONNE BASE DE MISE À FEU, tel un seau, remplie de terre ou de
sable.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef006.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">BURY fireworks that do not have
a base HALFWAY in a container of earth or sand (such as a pail, box or
wheelbarrow) unless the label on the firework indicates otherwise. Set
them at a 10-degree angle, pointing away from people.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">ENFOUIR À
MOITIÉ les pièces pyrotechniques qui ne possèdent pas de base dans un
contenant (par exemple, un seau, une boîte ou une brouette) renfermant
du sable ou de la terre, sauf indication contraire sur l’étiquette. Les
installer à un angle de 10 degrés et les pointer en direction opposée
des spectateurs.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef007.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">NEVER try to light a firework
or hold a lit firework in your hand unless the instructions of the
person who obtained its authorization indicate that they are designed to
be hand-held.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">NE JAMAIS
tenir dans la main des pièces pyrotechniques qui sont allumées ou que
vous tentez d’allumer, sauf si les instructions de la personne qui a
obtenu l’autorisation des pièces indiquent qu’elles sont conçues pour
être tenues dans la main.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef008.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">LIGHT CAREFULLY: Always light
the fuse at its tip.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">ALLUMER
PRUDEMMENT : toujours allumer la mèche à l’extrémité.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef009.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">KEEP WATER NEARBY: Dispose of
used fireworks (including debris) in a pail of water.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">GARDER DE
L’EAU À PORTÉE DE LA MAIN : mettre les pièces pyrotechniques utilisées
et les débris dans un seau d’eau.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef010.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">WAIT at least 30 minutes before
approaching a firework that did not go off. NEVER try to RELIGHT a
firework that did not go off. NEVER try to fix a firework that is
defective.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">ATTENDRE au
moins 30 minutes avant de s’approcher d’une pièce pyrotechnique dont la
mise à feu n’a pas fonctionné. NE JAMAIS tenter de RALLUMER une pièce
pyrotechnique dont la mise à feu n’a pas fonctionné. NE JAMAIS tenter de
réparer une pièce pyrotechnique qui est défectueuse.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef011.jpg"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">KEEP fireworks in a cool, dry,
ventilated place, out of the reach of children.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">CONSERVER
les pièces pyrotechniques dans un endroit frais, sec, aéré et hors de la
portée des enfants.</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupleft">
<img src="/images/SOR-2013-211img/SOR-2013-211_ef012.JPG"
alt="This image illustrates the rule that follows." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderRight">IT IS RECOMMENDED that safety glasses be
worn.</td>
<td><span class="otherLang" lang="fr">IL EST RECOMMANDÉ de porter des
lunettes de sécurité.</span></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## [PART 17]{.HLabel1}[Special Effect Pyrotechnics]{.HTitleText1} {#h-798794 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[360]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-360 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of special effect pyrotechnics and
regulates their use. Division 1 sets out rules for sellers. Division 2
sets out rules for users and other acquirers and indicates how to obtain
a fireworks operator certificate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[361]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[black powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[black powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an explosive classified
    as type P.1. ([poudre noire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes the
    storage of the type of pyrotechnics to be sold or
    acquired. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[propellant powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[propellant powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means black powder and
    smokeless powder. ([poudre propulsive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pyrotechnic event]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pyrotechnic event]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an event at which
    special effect pyrotechnics are used and includes a film or
    television production in which special effect pyrotechnics are
    used. ([activité pyrotechnique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[special effect pyrotechnics]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special effect pyrotechnics]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in
    addition to any explosive classified as type F.3, the following
    types of explosive if it will be used to produce a special effect in
    a film or television production or a performance before a live
    audience:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fireworks accessories (type F.4);

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} black powder and hazard category PE 1 black
      powder substitutes (type P.1);

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} smokeless powder and hazard category PE 3 black
      powder substitutes (type P.2);

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} initiation systems (type I) (for example,
      blasting accessories);

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} detonating cord (type E.1); and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} low-hazard special purpose explosives (Type S.1)
      and high-hazard special purpose explosives (Type S.2). ([pièce
      pyrotechnique à effets spéciaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[smokeless powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[smokeless powder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an explosive
    classified as type P.2. ([poudre sans fumée]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[special purpose pyrotechnics]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special purpose pyrotechnics]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means special
    effect pyrotechnics that are combined with a flammable liquid, solid
    or gas to produce custom-made special effects. ([pièce pyrotechnique
    à usage particulier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires special
    effect pyrotechnics for use, which includes setting them up and
    firing them. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 33
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400768){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400768.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-361-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 361"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pyrotechnics quantity

**[[362]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-362 .sectionLabel}** A reference to the mass
of a special effect pyrotechnic in this Part is a reference to its gross
mass (the mass of the pyrotechnic plus the mass of any packing or
container) except in the case of propellant powder, where it is a
reference to its net quantity (the mass of the powder excluding the mass
of any packaging or container and, in the case of an explosive article,
also excluding any component that is not an explosive substance).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4(E)](#1354349-1401074){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1401074.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-362-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 362"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on use

**[[363]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-363 .sectionLabel}** Except as authorized by
this Part, it is prohibited for a person to use special effect
pyrotechnics.

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798819 .Subheading}

#### [Acquisition for Sale and Storage]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798820 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[364]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-364 .sectionLabel}** A seller may acquire,
store and sell special effect pyrotechnics if they hold a licence. A
seller who acquires special effect pyrotechnics must comply with this
Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

- **[[365]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-365 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must store their special effect pyrotechnics in the magazine
  specified in their licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2018-231, s. 34\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 34
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-365-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 365"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No display for sale

**[[366]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-366 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not
display special effect pyrotechnics for sale.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer of powder

**[[367]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-367 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not
transfer propellant powder from one container to another for the purpose
of sale unless their licence authorizes them to do so.

#### [Sale]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798833 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate required

- **[[368]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-368 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller may sell special effect pyrotechnics only to a buyer who holds
  the fireworks operator certificate that is required for use of the
  pyrotechnics that are to be bought.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence and certificate required

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller may sell initiation systems or detonating
  cord only to a buyer who holds a licence and a fireworks operator
  certificate (special effects pyrotechnician --- detonating cord).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 1, a seller may sell flash cotton,
  flash paper, flash string, sparkle string or propellant powder to a
  buyer who holds neither a licence nor a fireworks operator
  certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence required

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A seller may sell special effect pyrotechnics to a
  buyer who is not a user only if the buyer holds a licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[369]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-369 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more special effect pyrotechnics to a licensed
  buyer than the buyer is authorized by their licence to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed buyer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more special effect
  pyrotechnics to an unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by
  this Part to store.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification

- **[[370]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-370
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before selling special effect
  pyrotechnics, the seller must require the buyer to establish their
  identity by showing either

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a piece of identification, issued by the Government
    of Canada or a provincial, municipal or foreign government, that
    bears a photograph of the user; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} two pieces of identification, each of which sets
    out the buyer's name, at least one of which is issued by the
    Government of Canada or a provincial, municipal or foreign
    government and at least one of which sets out the buyer's address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comparison

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the buyer provides a piece of identification that
  bears a photograph, the seller must, before selling the special effect
  pyrotechnics, ensure that the photograph is that of the buyer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[371]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-371 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of every sale of special effect pyrotechnics for two years after
the date of the sale. The record must include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the number and expiry date of the
  buyer's licence and, if applicable, the number and expiry date of the
  buyer's fireworks operator certificate;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the type and product name of each special effect
  pyrotechnic sold and the name of the person who obtained its
  authorization;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of special effect pyrotechnics sold
  under each product name;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a short description of the effects of any explosive
  article sold;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the size of the container in which any propellant
  powder was sold; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400776){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-371-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 371"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Rules for Users and Other Acquirers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-798866 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION a]{.HLabel3}[Users without a Licence or Certificate]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798867 .Subheading}

##### [Flash Cotton, Flash Paper, Flash String and Sparkle String]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798868 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[372]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-372 .sectionLabel}** A user who holds
neither a fireworks operator certificate nor a licence may acquire,
store and use flash cotton, flash paper, flash string and sparkle
string.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

**[[373]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-373 .sectionLabel}** A user who acquires
flash cotton, flash paper, flash string or sparkle string must store it
in a dwelling or a storage unit and ensure that the requirements of
sections 374, 382 and 383 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400780){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400780.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-373-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 373"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

**[[374]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-374 .sectionLabel}** No more than 200 g of
flash cotton, 1 kg of flash paper, 200 g of flash string and 200 g of
sparkle string may be stored at any one time.

##### [Percussion Caps and Propellant Powder Used in Historical Re-enactments]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798875 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

- **[[375]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-375 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds neither a fireworks operator certificate nor a licence
  may acquire, store and use percussion caps and propellant powder, if
  the caps and powder are acquired for use in original or reproduction
  firearms in an historical re-enactment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for use

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who acquires percussion caps and propellant
  powder for an historical re-enactment

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must have the written approval of the local
    authority to hold the re-enactment or must be under the supervision
    of a person who has that approval; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must have experience in the safe use of explosives
    in historical re-enactments, have completed a course on this use
    certified by the Minister or be under the supervision of a person
    who has that experience or has completed such a course.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400783){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400783.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-375-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 375"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

**[[376]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-376 .sectionLabel}** A user must store their
percussion caps and propellant powder in a dwelling or in a storage unit
and ensure that the requirements of sections 377 to 380, 382 and 383 are
met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400787){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400787.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-376-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 376"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Percussion caps

- **[[377]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-377
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Percussion caps must be stored in
  their original packaging.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Smokeless powder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Smokeless powder must be stored in its original
  container or in small arms cartridges.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Black powder

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Black powder must be stored in its original container
  with no more than 500 g of black powder per container, in small arms
  cartridges or in black powder cartouches.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400792){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400792.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 115](#1451713-1453012){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451713-1453012.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-377-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 377"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detached dwellings or site of use

**[[378]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-378 .sectionLabel}** The maximum quantity of
propellant powder that may be stored at any one time in a detached
dwelling, in a storage unit attached to a detached dwelling or in a
storage unit at the site of use is 25 kg, of which no more than 10 kg
may be black powder.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400796){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400796.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-378-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 378"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other dwellings --- smokeless powder

- **[[379]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-379 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  maximum quantity of smokeless powder that may be stored at any one
  time in a dwelling other than a detached dwelling, or in a storage
  unit attached to a dwelling other than a detached dwelling, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 20 kg, if all the smokeless powder is in containers
    that hold no more than 1 kg; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 5 kg, if any of the smokeless powder is in a
    container that holds more than 1 kg.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other dwellings --- black powder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum quantity of black powder that may be
  stored at any one time in a dwelling other than a detached dwelling,
  or in a storage unit attached to a dwelling other than a detached
  dwelling, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 1 kg, with no more than 500 g of black powder per
    container, if the black powder is in original containers; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 3 kg less any quantity that is in containers, if
    the black powder is in small arms cartridges or black powder
    cartouches.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400800){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400800.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 116](#1451715-1453014){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451715-1453014.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-379-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 379"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detached storage unit

**[[380]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-380 .sectionLabel}** The maximum quantity of
black powder and smokeless powder that may be stored at any one time in
storage units that are not attached to a dwelling and are not at the
site of use, whether in a single unit or in several, is 25 kg and 75 kg,
respectively.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400804){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400804.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 117](#1451717-1453017){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451717-1453017.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-380-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 380"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Pyrotechnics Used in Student Training]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798907 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Student in training

**[[381]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-381 .sectionLabel}** A user who holds
neither a fireworks operating certificate nor a licence and who is
taking a college or university course on special effect pyrotechnics
that is certified by the Minister may, during their training and while
under the supervision of a holder of a fireworks operator certificate
(senior pyrotechnician) or a fireworks operator certificate (special
effects pyrotechnician), use any special effect pyrotechnics that their
supervisor is authorized to use.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-381-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 381"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798912 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[382]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-382 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  special effect pyrotechnics are stored in a dwelling, they must be
  stored in accordance with package instructions, located in a clean,
  dry place, away from flammable substances and sources of ignition and
  in a manner that ensures that access to them is limited to people
  authorized by the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When special effect pyrotechnics are stored in a
  storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} nothing other than special effect pyrotechnics may
    be stored in the storage unit;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} propellant powder, firework accessories and other
    special effect pyrotechnics must be stored separately from one
    another (for example, on different shelves or separated by a wooden
    barrier);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 118\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400809){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400809.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 118](#1451719-1453030){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451719-1453030.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-382-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 382"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- site of use

- **[[383]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-383 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  special effect pyrotechnics are stored in a storage unit at the site
  of use, the unit must be made from, or lined with, a non-sparking
  material, marked with the words "Pyrotechnics/[Pièces
  pyrotechniques]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" and kept locked. Nothing other
  than special effect pyrotechnics may be stored in the storage unit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location of storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The storage unit must be kept away from other
  flammable substances and sources of ignition and in an area that is
  not accessible to the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400813){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400813.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-383-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 383"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Use]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798935 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions

- **[[384]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-384 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user must, when using special effect pyrotechnics, follow the
  instructions of the pyrotechnician in charge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited use

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user must not use special effect pyrotechnics if
  they show any signs of deterioration (for example, discoloration or a
  vinegary smell).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electric match

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A user must not use an electric match to ignite flash
  cotton, flash paper, flash string or sparkle string.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 32
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 119](#1451724-1453033){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451724-1453033.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-384-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 384"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION b]{.HLabel3}[Other Acquirers Without a Certificate]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798945 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}License holder

**[[385]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385 .sectionLabel}** A person who does not
hold a fireworks operator certificate may acquire and store special
effect pyrotechnics if they hold a licence. They must store their
special effect pyrotechnics in the magazine specified in their licence.

#### [SUBDIVISION c]{.HLabel3}[Users with a Certificate]{.HTitleText3} {#h-798948 .Subheading}

##### [Fireworks Operator Certificates]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798949 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of certificate

**[[386]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-386 .sectionLabel}** The fireworks operator
certificates issued by the Minister that are required for the use of
special effect pyrotechnics are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (pyrotechnician);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (senior
  pyrotechnician);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (special effects
  pyrotechnician);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (special effects
  pyrotechnician --- detonating cord); and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (visitor
  pyrotechnician).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-386-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 386"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Qualifications to Obtain a Certificate]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798959 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pyrotechnician

- **[[387]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-387 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} To
  obtain a fireworks operator certificate (pyrotechnician), a person
  must successfully complete the special effects pyrotechnics safety and
  legal awareness course offered by the Explosives Regulatory Division,
  Department of Natural Resources or a course certified as equivalent by
  the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Senior pyrotechnician

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} To obtain a fireworks operator certificate (senior
  pyrotechnician), a person must have acted as a pyrotechnician for two
  years and must be able to safely use explosives that are classified as
  type F.3 and propellant powder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special effects pyrotechnician

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} To obtain a fireworks operator certificate (special
  effects pyrotechnician), a person must have acted as a senior
  pyrotechnician for two years and must be able to safely use explosives
  that are classified as type F.3, propellant powder and special purpose
  pyrotechnics.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special effects pyrotechnician ---
  detonating cord

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} To obtain a fireworks operators certificate (special
  effects pyrotechnician --- detonating cord), a person must have a
  fireworks operator certificate (special effects pyrotechnician) and
  must be able to safely use initiation systems and detonating cords.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Visitor pyrotechnician

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} To obtain a fireworks operator certificate (visitor
  pyrotechnician), a person must reside outside Canada and have the
  necessary experience using special effect pyrotechnics to safely carry
  out the activities of a holder of a fireworks operator certificate
  (pyrotechnician).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-387-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 387"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Application]{.HTitleText4} {#h-798973 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for certificate ---
pyrotechnician

- **[[388]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-388 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a fireworks operator certificate (pyrotechnician) must
  complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector of Explosives the
  application form provided by the Department of Natural Resources. The
  application must include the following information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, date of birth, address,
    telephone number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 120\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 120\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} proof that the applicant has successfully completed
    a pyrotechnics safety and legal awareness course certified by the
    Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of course completion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who has not completed the special effect
  pyrotechnics safety and legal awareness course or a certified
  equivalent on the date their application is submitted may, within six
  months after that date, submit to the Chief Inspector of Explosives
  proof of their successful completion of the course or equivalent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- senior pyrotechnician
  and special effects pyrotechnician

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a fireworks operator certificate
  (senior pyrotechnician) or a fireworks operator certificate (special
  effects pyrotechnician) must complete, sign and send to the Chief
  Inspector of Explosives the application form provided by the
  Department of Natural Resources. The application must include the
  following information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, date of birth, address,
    telephone number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 120\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number and expiry date of the applicant's
    fireworks operator certificate;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 120\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the applicant's work journal that sets
    out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date and place of each pyrotechnic event at
      which the applicant has worked and the types of explosives used,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the capacity in which the applicant acted at
      each pyrotechnic event, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the applicant's supervisor at each
      pyrotechnic event.

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2018-231, s. 35\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other certificates

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for one of the following certificates
  must provide the information and documents referred to in
  subsection (3) and, in addition, must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a fireworks operator certificate (senior
    pyrotechnician), establish that they have acted as a pyrotechnician
    for one year and submit a letter from a supervisor which attests
    that the applicant is able to safely use explosives classified as
    type F.3 and propellant powder;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a fireworks operator certificate (special
    effects pyrotechnician), establish that they have acted as a senior
    pyrotechnician for one year and submit a letter from a supervisor
    which attests that the applicant is able to safely use explosives
    classified as type F.3, propellent powder and special purpose
    pyrotechnics; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a fireworks operator certificate (special
    effects pyrotechnician --- detonating cord), establish that they
    have acted as a special effects pyrotechnician for one year and
    submit a letter from a supervisor which attests that the applicant
    is able to safely use initiation systems and detonating cords.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- visitor pyrotechnician

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a fireworks operator certificate
  (visitor pyrotechnician) must complete, sign and send to the Chief
  Inspector of Explosives the application form provided by the
  Department of Natural Resources. The application must include the
  following information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, date of birth, address,
    telephone number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 120\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 120\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the applicant's resumé which sets out the
    pyrotechnic events at which they have used special effect
    pyrotechnics and the people and organizations for which they have
    worked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a list of the pyrotechnic events in which they plan
    to participate in Canada and the dates of the events; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and number of the
    fireworks operator certificate of the pyrotechnician in charge at
    each event in which they plan to participate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a fireworks operator certificate or
  a modification to or change of a certificate must pay the applicable
  fees set out in Part 19.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
- SOR/2018-231, s. 35
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400816){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400816.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 120](#1451726-1453042){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451726-1453042.html"
  role="button"}
:::
