[Previous
Version](section-388-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 388"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Acquisition and Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799011 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[389]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-389 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire and
store special effect pyrotechnics, whether or not they hold a licence,
if they hold a fireworks operator certificate with the endorsements
required for the use of the pyrotechnics to be acquired. However, a user
who does not hold a licence must not acquire initiation systems or
detonating cords. A user who acquires special effect pyrotechnics must
comply with this subdivision.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 121](#1451738-1453050){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451738-1453050.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-389-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 389"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

- **[[390]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-390 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their special effect pyrotechnics
  in the magazine specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a user who holds a licence
  may store up to 500 electric matches and up to 25 kg of other special
  effect pyrotechnics in a dwelling or a storage unit. A user who does
  so must ensure that the requirements of sections 393 to 397 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400821){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400821.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-390-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 390"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- unlicensed user

**[[391]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-391 .sectionLabel}** A user who does not
hold a licence must store their special effect pyrotechnics in a
dwelling or a storage unit and ensure that the requirements of sections
392 to 397 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400825){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400825.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-391-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 391"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

**[[392]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-392 .sectionLabel}** No more than 500
electric matches and 25 kg of other special effect pyrotechnics may be
stored at any one time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Smokeless powder

- **[[393]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-393
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Smokeless powder must be stored in
  its original container or in small arms cartridges.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Black powder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Black powder must be stored in its original
  container, in small arms cartridges or in black powder cartouches.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detached dwelling

**[[394]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-394 .sectionLabel}** The maximum quantity of
propellant powder that may be stored at any one time in a detached
dwelling, or in a storage unit attached to a detached dwelling, is 25 kg
of which no more than 10 kg may be black powder.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400829){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400829.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-394-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 394"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other dwellings --- smokeless powder

- **[[395]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-395 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  maximum quantity of smokeless powder that may be stored at any one
  time in a dwelling other than a detached dwelling, or in a storage
  unit that is attached to a dwelling other than a detached dwelling, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 20 kg, if all the smokeless powder is in containers
    that hold no more than 1 kg; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 5 kg, if any of the smokeless powder is in a
    container that holds more than 1 kg.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other dwellings --- black powder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum quantity of black powder that may be
  stored at any one time in a dwelling other than a detached dwelling,
  or in a storage unit that is attached to a dwelling other than a
  detached dwelling, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 1 kg, if the black powder is in containers; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 3 kg less any quantity that is in containers, if
    the black powder is in small arms cartridges or black powder
    cartouches.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400833){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400833.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-395-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 395"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detached storage unit

**[[396]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-396 .sectionLabel}** The maximum quantity of
propellant powder that a user may store at any one time in a storage
unit that is not attached to a dwelling, whether in a single unit or in
several, is 75 kg.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400837){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400837.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-396-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 396"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[397]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-397 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  special effect pyrotechnics are stored in a dwelling, they must be
  stored in accordance with package instructions, located in a clean,
  dry place, away from flammable substances and sources of ignition and
  in a manner that ensures that access to them is limited to people
  authorized by the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When special effect pyrotechnics are stored in a
  storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from
    flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
    and dry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} nothing other than special effect pyrotechnics may
    be stored in the storage unit;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} propellant powder, firework accessories and other
    special effect pyrotechnics must be stored separately from one
    another (for example, on different shelves or separated by a wooden
    barrier);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
    ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 122\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400842){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400842.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 122](#1451740-1453063){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451740-1453063.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-397-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 397"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[398]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-398 .sectionLabel}** [(1) and
  (2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2018-231, s. 36\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- site of use

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any storage unit at the site of use must be located
  in an area that is not accessible to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- in magazine

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Special effect pyrotechnics that are not stored in a
  locked storage unit must be stored in a magazine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pyrotechnics to be attended

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Special effect pyrotechnics must be attended when
  they are not in a storage unit or a magazine.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 36
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400846){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400846.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-398-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 398"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Use]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799068 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pyrotechnician and visitor pyrotechnician

**[[399]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-399 .sectionLabel}** A user who holds a
fireworks operator certificate (pyrotechnician) or a fireworks operator
certificate (visitor pyrotechnician) may use the following explosives:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} explosives that are classified as type F.3, type S.1
  or type S.2 and whose use by pyrotechnicians or visitor
  pyrotechnicians has been authorized by the Chief Inspector of
  Explosives under section 32 or 33 as well as all fireworks
  accessories;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} smokeless powder;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} explosives classified as type F.3, other than those
  referred to in paragraph (a), and black powder, but only under the
  direct supervision of a senior pyrotechnician or special effects
  pyrotechnician;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} special purpose pyrotechnics, but only under the
  direct supervision of a special effects pyrotechnician; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} initiation systems and detonating cord, but only
  under the direct supervision of a special effects pyrotechnician who
  holds a fireworks operating certificate (special effects
  pyrotechnician --- detonating cord).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 123](#1451745-1453067){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451745-1453067.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-399-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 399"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Senior pyrotechnician

**[[400]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-400 .sectionLabel}** A user who holds a
fireworks operator certificate (senior pyrotechnician) may use the
following explosives:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} explosives classified as type F.3, type S.1 or type
  S.2, fireworks accessories, black powder and smokeless powder;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} special purpose pyrotechnics, but only under the
  direct supervision of a special effects pyrotechnician; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} initiation systems and detonating cords, but only
  under the direct supervision of a special effects pyrotechnician who
  holds a fireworks operating certificate (special effects
  pyrotechnician --- detonating cord).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 124](#1451747-1453070){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451747-1453070.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-400-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 400"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special effects pyrotechnician

- **[[401]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-401 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a fireworks operator certificate (special effects
  pyrotechnician) may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} assemble at the site of use and use special purpose
    pyrotechnics, explosives classified as type F.3, type S.1 or type
    S.2, fireworks accessories, black powder and smokeless powder; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use initiation systems and detonating cords, but
    only under the direct supervision of a special effects
    pyrotechnician who holds a fireworks operating certificate (special
    effects pyrotechnician --- detonating cord).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special effects pyrotechnician ---
  detonating cord

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who holds a fireworks operator certificate
  (special effects pyrotechnician --- detonating cord) may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} assemble at the site of use and use special purpose
    pyrotechnics, explosives classified as type F.3, type S.1 or type
    S.2, fireworks accessories, black powder and smokeless powder; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use initiation systems and detonating cords.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 125](#1451749-1453074){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451749-1453074.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-401-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 401"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Supervision of a Pyrotechnic Event]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799090 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pyrotechnician in charge

- **[[402]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-402
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every organizer of a pyrotechnic
  event must ensure that the event is supervised by a pyrotechnician in
  charge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The pyrotechnician in charge must ensure that the
  event is carried out safely and that the requirements of sections 403
  to 408 are complied with.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plan

- **[[403]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-403 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  pyrotechnic event plan must be prepared in writing and kept for two
  years after the date of the pyrotechnic event. The plan must include
  the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the pyrotechnician in charge and the
    number and expiry date of their fireworks operator certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the site of the event, including
    the placement of the special effect pyrotechnics, the proximity of
    the audience and the location of every exit, every storage area for
    the pyrotechnics and every smoke detector that may be triggered by
    the pyrotechnics used in the event;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the type and product name of each special effect
    pyrotechnic that will be used and name of the person who obtained
    its authorization;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of each special effect pyrotechnic;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the anticipated height, duration and fallout effect
    of the effects of each special effect pyrotechnic;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of the anticipated effects of each
    special purpose pyrotechnic;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the method and sequence of firing the special
    effect pyrotechnics; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an assessment of the likelihood of harm to people
    or property resulting from the use of the special effect
    pyrotechnics.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The plan must be submitted to the local authority.
  The written approval of the local authority to hold the pyrotechnic
  event must be obtained before the event takes place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety meetings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Meetings must be held with the people who will
  participate in presenting the pyrotechnic event (for example, security
  guards, artists and technicians) to inform them of the special effect
  pyrotechnics that will be used and the safety precautions to be taken
  during the event. Subsequent meetings must be held if the event is
  changed in a way that increases the likelihood of harm to people or
  property resulting from the use of the pyrotechnics.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, s. 44(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400851){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400851.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-403-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 403"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fallout zone

- **[[404]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-404 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fallout zone must be established, taking into account the properties
  of the special effect pyrotechnics to be used, how they will be
  positioned, the instructions of the person who obtained their
  authorization, the weather conditions if the pyrotechnic event is to
  be held outdoors and the likelihood of harm to people or property
  resulting from the use of the pyrotechnics.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flammables

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fallout zone must not contain any flammables or
  other items that are likely to catch fire.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Only people authorized by the pyrotechnician in
  charge may enter or be in the fallout zone from the time any special
  effect pyrotechnics are brought into the zone until the pyrotechnician
  in charge declares the zone to be free of explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No smoking

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Smoking must be prohibited in the fallout zone.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 33
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 126](#1451754-1453084){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451754-1453084.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-404-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 404"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fire prevention and first aid

**[[405]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-405 .sectionLabel}** During the pyrotechnic
event, fire prevention measures that minimize the possibility of harm to
people or property must be put in place and facilities, equipment and
personnel for fire fighting and administering first aid that minimize
the possibility of harm must be present at the site.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions

- **[[406]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-406 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  setting up and firing the special effect pyrotechnics, the
  instructions of the pyrotechnician in charge must be followed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Firing unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Only the pyrotechnician in charge, or a person
  designated by the pyrotechnician in charge, may have access to the
  firing unit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Physical keying device

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The pyrotechnician in charge, or a person designated
  by the pyrotechnician in charge, must have control of any physical
  keying device at all times.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety interlock

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The firing unit must be equipped with a safety
  interlock that has at least two steps.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extraneous electricity

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Precautions that minimize the likelihood of
  extraneous electricity igniting an electric match must be taken.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Device attached to body

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Any device that is used to contain special effect
  pyrotechnics and is hand-held or attached to a person's body must be
  equipped with a firing system that has two switches.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Firing system --- requirement

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The pyrotechnician in charge must ensure that the
  firing system is secured at all times to prevent accidental ignition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Devices

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Any device that is used to contain special effect
  pyrotechnics must be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} designed and manufactured to prevent fragmentation
    or distortion of the device;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} designed and manufactured to prevent or contain
    fragmentation of the pyrotechnics;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} mounted so as to prevent any change in position or
    direction when used;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} positioned and secured in a manner that minimizes
    the likelihood of harm to people and property; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} maintained in good condition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Damaged pyrotechnics

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Special effect pyrotechnics that are damaged,
  leaking, damp or contaminated must not be used.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No firing

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A special effect pyrotechnic must not be fired if a
  circumstance occurs that could increase the likelihood of harm to
  people or property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Postponing or stopping event

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} A pyrotechnic event must be postponed or stopped if
  unfavourable weather conditions develop, a special effect pyrotechnic
  malfunctions or any other circumstance occurs that could increase the
  likelihood of harm to people or property.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 34
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 127](#1451756-1453088){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451756-1453088.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-406-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 406"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[407]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-407
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s.
  128\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Misfired pyrotechnics

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Misfired special effect pyrotechnics must not be
  approached until at least

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one minute after firing, if the firing was
    initiated by an electric match; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 30 minutes after firing, if the firing was
    initiated by other means.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Precautions that minimize the likelihood of harm to
  people and property from misfired special effect pyrotechnics must be
  taken.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Search

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} As soon as the circumstances permit after the periods
  referred to in subsection (2), the site of the pyrotechnic event must
  be searched and all explosives must be removed from the site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} After the event, only people designated to do a
  search by the pyrotechnician in charge may enter or be in the fallout
  zone until the pyrotechnician in charge declares the zone to be free
  of explosives.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 128](#1451761-1453094){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451761-1453094.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-407-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 407"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Logbook of events

**[[408]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-408 .sectionLabel}** A record of the
pyrotechnic event must be made in a logbook that sets out the name of
the pyrotechnician in charge and the number and expiry date of their
fireworks operator certificate. The logbook must be kept for two years
after the date of the last recorded event. The record must include the
following information and documents:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the pyrotechnic event plan prepared for the
  event;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the local authority's approval to hold the
  event;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of every person who worked at
  the event under the supervision of the pyrotechnician in charge; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of any unusual circumstance, a
  statement of the number of misfires and a description of how each
  misfire was dealt with.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400855){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400855.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-408-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 408"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of licence holder

**[[409]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-409 .sectionLabel}** When a pyrotechnic
event is held on behalf of a licence holder, the holder must keep a
record of the event for two years after the date of the event. The
record must include the following information and documents:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder's name and address and the number
  and expiry date of their licence;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the pyrotechnician in charge and the
  number and expiry date of their fireworks operator certificate;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the local authority's approval to hold the
  event;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the type and product name of each special effect
  pyrotechnic used and the name of the person who obtained its
  authorization;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the quantity used under each product name; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date and site of the event.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400859){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400859.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-409-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 409"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 18]{.HLabel1}[Display Fireworks]{.HTitleText1} {#h-799185 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[410]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-410 .sectionLabel}** This Part authorizes
the acquisition, storage and sale of display fireworks (type F.2) and
their accessories and regulates their use. Division 1 sets out rules for
sellers and users of display fireworks and fireworks accessories,
including how to obtain a fireworks operator certificate. Division 2
sets out additional rules for display fireworks that are firecrackers.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[411]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-411 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that authorizes the
    storage of display fireworks and their
    accessories. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who acquires display
    fireworks or their accessories for use, which includes setting them
    up and firing them. ([utilisateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of display fireworks

**[[412]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-412 .sectionLabel}** A reference to a mass
of display fireworks or their accessories in this Part is a reference to
their net quantity (the mass of the fireworks excluding the mass of any
packaging or container).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-412-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 412"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use prohibited

**[[413]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-413 .sectionLabel}** Except as authorized by
this Part, it is prohibited for a person to use display fireworks or
their accessories.

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Display Fireworks]{.HTitleText2} {#h-799198 .Subheading}

Definition of [[fireworks]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[414]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-414 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[fireworks]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means display fireworks and fireworks
accessories that are used with display fireworks.

#### [SUBDIVISION a]{.HLabel3}[Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-799201 .Subheading}

##### [Acquisition for Sale and Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799202 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[415]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-415 .sectionLabel}** A seller may acquire,
store and sell fireworks if they hold a licence. A seller who acquires
fireworks must comply with this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

- **[[416]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-416 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must store their fireworks in the magazine specified in their
  licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electric matches

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not store electric matches in a
  magazine in which fireworks are stored.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 129](#1451767-1453098){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451767-1453098.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-416-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 416"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No display for sale

**[[417]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-417 .sectionLabel}** A seller must not
display fireworks for sale.

##### [Sale]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799212 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized buyers

**[[418]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-418 .sectionLabel}** A seller may sell
fireworks only to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who holds a licence; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a user who holds a fireworks operator certificate
  with the endorsements required for the use of the fireworks to be
  acquired and who provides the seller with a copy of the certificate
  and a copy of a local authority's approval to hold the fireworks
  display in which the fireworks will be used.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400863){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400863.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 130](#1451769-1453100){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451769-1453100.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-418-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 418"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- licensed buyer

- **[[419]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-419 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  seller must not sell more fireworks to a licensed buyer than the buyer
  is authorized by their licence to store.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- unlicensed buyer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seller must not sell more fireworks to an
  unlicensed buyer than the buyer is authorized by the local authority
  to store or the quantity set out in section 426, whichever is less.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400868){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400868.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-419-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 419"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reuse of packaging --- seller

**[[419.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-419.1 .sectionLabel}** A seller must
ensure that any packaging or container that has been used for fireworks
is not reused unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transport classification of the fireworks to be
  transported remains unchanged;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fireworks to be transported are arranged in the
  packaging or container so as to minimize their movement during
  transport;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the packaging or container is closed for transport in
  the same manner as it was for its first use;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the mass of the fireworks is equal to or less than
  the mass of the fireworks during the first use of the packaging or
  container; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the packaging or container shows no signs of damage,
  contamination or reduced strength.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 131](#1451771-1453109){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451771-1453109.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[420]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-420 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of every sale of fireworks for two years after the date of the
sale. The record must include the following information and documents:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a sale to a licensed buyer, the number
  and expiry date of their licence;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a sale to the holder of a fireworks
  operator certificate, the number and expiry date of the certificate
  and a copy of a local authority's approval to hold the fireworks
  display in which the fireworks will be used;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the type and product name of each firework sold and
  the name of the person who obtained its authorization;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of fireworks sold under each product
  name; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 38, 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400872){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400872.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-420-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 420"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION b]{.HLabel3}[Rules for Users]{.HTitleText3} {#h-799232 .Subheading}

##### [Fireworks Operator Certificates]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799233 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of certificates

**[[421]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-421 .sectionLabel}** The certificates issued
by the Minister that are required for the use of fireworks are the
following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (display assistant);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor
  with endorsement); and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} fireworks operator certificate (display visitor).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-421-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 421"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Qualifications to Obtain a Certificate]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799242 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display assistant

- **[[422]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-422 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} To
  obtain a fireworks operator certificate (display assistant), a person
  must successfully complete the display fireworks safety and legal
  awareness course offered by the Explosives Regulatory Division,
  Department of Natural Resources or a course certified as equivalent by
  the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display supervisor

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} To obtain a fireworks operator certificate (display
  supervisor), a person must have acted as a display assistant in at
  least three fireworks displays within five years after the date on
  which the applicant completed the display fireworks safety and legal
  awareness course or its equivalent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display supervisor with endorsement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} To obtain a fireworks operator certificate (display
  supervisor with endorsement), a person must hold a fireworks operator
  certificate (display supervisor) and must either

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} successfully complete an advanced safety course,
    certified by the Minister, on the fireworks or display sites covered
    by the endorsement; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} demonstrate to the Minister that, working under the
    direct supervision of a display supervisor in charge, they have
    obtained the necessary experience to safely carry out the activities
    covered by the endorsement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display visitor

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} To obtain a fireworks operator certificate (display
  visitor), a person must reside outside Canada and must have the
  experience necessary to safely carry out the activities of a holder of
  a fireworks operator certificate (display assistant).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 38, 44(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-422-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 422"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Application]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799256 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applying for certificate

- **[[423]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-423 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a fireworks operator certificate (display assistant)
  must complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector of Explosives the
  application form provided by the Department of Natural Resources. The
  application must include the following information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, date of birth, address,
    telephone number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of any organization of fireworks operators
    to which the applicant belongs;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a photograph of the applicant taken within the
    previous 12 months; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} proof that the applicant has successfully completed
    the display fireworks safety and legal awareness course offered by
    the Explosives Regulatory Division, Department of Natural Resources
    or a course certified as equivalent by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late submission of proof

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who has not completed the display
  fireworks safety and legal awareness course or a certified equivalent
  on the date their application is submitted may, within six months
  after that date, submit to the Chief Inspector of Explosives proof of
  their successful completion.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display supervisor

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a fireworks operator certificate
  (display supervisor) must complete, sign and send to the Chief
  Inspector of Explosives the application form provided by the
  Department of Natural Resources. The application must include the
  following information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, date of birth, address,
    telephone number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 132\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number and expiry date of the applicant's
    fireworks operator certificate;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 132\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the applicant's work journal that sets
    out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date and place of each fireworks display at
      which the applicant has worked and a description of the fireworks
      used,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the capacity in which the applicant acted at
      each fireworks display, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the display supervisor in charge at
      each fireworks display; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a letter of recommendation that is signed by the
    display supervisor in charge of a fireworks display in which the
    applicant participated and that attests that the applicant is
    competent to act as a display supervisor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsements

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In an application for a fireworks operator
  certificate (display supervisor with endorsement), in addition to
  providing the information and documents referred to in subsection (3),
  the applicant must establish that they have acted as a display
  supervisor in charge in at least three fireworks displays within the
  previous five years and,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a large shell endorsement, establish that they
    hold a fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor) and
    submit a letter that is signed by the display supervisor in charge
    of a display at which the applicant was trained in the use of large
    shells and which attests that the applicant is able to safely use
    the shells;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a nautical effects endorsement, establish that
    they hold a fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor) and
    submit a letter that is signed by the display supervisor in charge
    of a display at which the applicant was trained in the use of
    nautical effects and which attests that the applicant is able to
    safely use the effects;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a flying saucer endorsement, establish that
    they hold a fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor) and
    submit a letter that is signed by the display supervisor in charge
    of a display at which the applicant was trained in the use of flying
    saucers and which attests that the applicant is able to safely use
    flying saucers;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for a rooftop site endorsement, establish that they
    hold a fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor) and
    submit a letter that is signed by the display supervisor in charge
    of a display at which the applicant was trained to fire fireworks
    from a rooftop and which attests that the applicant is able to
    safely fire from a rooftop site;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for a bridge site endorsement, establish that they
    hold a fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor) and
    submit a letter that is signed by the display supervisor in charge
    of a display at which the applicant was trained to fire fireworks
    from a bridge and which attests that the applicant can safely fire
    from a bridge site;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for a flatbed site endorsement, establish that they
    hold a fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor) and
    submit a letter that is signed by the display supervisor in charge
    of a display at which the applicant was trained to fire fireworks
    from a flatbed and which attests that the applicant can safely fire
    from a flatbed site; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for a floating platform site endorsement, establish
    that they hold a fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor)
    and submit a letter that is signed by the display supervisor in
    charge of a display in which the applicant was trained to fire
    fireworks from a floating platform and which attests that the
    applicant can safely fire from a floating platform site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display visitor

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a fireworks operator certificate
  (display visitor) must complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector
  of Explosives the application form provided by the Department of
  Natural Resources. The application must include the following
  information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, date of birth, address,
    telephone number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 132\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 132\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the applicant's resumé that sets out the
    displays in which they have used display fireworks and the people
    and organizations for which they have worked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a list of the fireworks displays in which they plan
    to participate while in Canada and the dates of the events; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and number of the
    fireworks operator certificate of the supervisor in charge at each
    fireworks display in which they plan to participate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a fireworks operator certificate or
  for a modification to, or change of, a certificate must pay the
  applicable fees set out in Part 19.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 132](#1451773-1453116){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451773-1453116.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-423-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 423"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Acquisition and Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799298 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[424]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-424 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire
fireworks, whether or not they hold a licence, if they hold a fireworks
operator certificate with the endorsements required for the use of the
fireworks to be acquired. A user who acquires fireworks must comply with
this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 133](#1451783-1453122){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451783-1453122.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-424-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 424"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licence holder

- **[[425]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-425 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their fireworks in the magazine
  specified in their licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electric matches

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user must not store electric matches in a magazine
  in which other fireworks are stored.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- display supervisor in charge

**[[426]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-426 .sectionLabel}** A user who is the
display supervisor in charge of a display, whether or not they hold a
licence, may store the fireworks to be used in a display ---- to a
maximum of 500 electric matches and 100 kg of other fireworks --- in a
storage unit if they obtain the written approval of the local authority
to do so. The user must ensure that the requirements in section 427 are
met.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-231, s. 39
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400875){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400875.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-426-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 426"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

**[[427]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-427 .sectionLabel}** When fireworks are
stored in a storage unit,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located away from flammable
  substances and sources of ignition;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the interior of the storage unit must be kept clean
  and dry;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
  to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
  weather;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
  unlocked;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
  impede exit in case of fire;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
  must be kept unobstructed;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from a
  non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} only type F explosives, other than electric matches,
  may be stored with display fireworks;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} electric matches must be stored separately from other
  explosives (for example, on different shelves or separated by a wooden
  barrier);

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of an
  ignition or fire in or near the storage unit must be taken; and

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
  Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at least
  10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a symbol, at
  least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a clearly
  visible location.

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-77, s. 134\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400881){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400881.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 134](#1451785-1453134){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451785-1453134.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-427-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 427"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Use]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799324 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display assistant and display visitor

**[[428]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-428 .sectionLabel}** A user may use
fireworks if they hold a fireworks operator certificate (display
assistant) or a fireworks operator certificate (display visitor) and use
them under the direct supervision of the display supervisor in charge.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display supervisor

- **[[429]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-429 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user may use fireworks other than aerial shells with a diameter of
  more than 155 mm, nautical shells and flying saucers if they hold a
  fireworks operator certificate (display supervisor) and fire them from
  a site other than a rooftop, bridge, flatbed or floating platform.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsement required

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} use aerial shells with a diameter of more than 155
    mm, nautical shells and flying saucers if they hold a fireworks
    operator certificate (display supervisor) that includes the required
    endorsement or they are under the direct supervision of a display
    supervisor in charge whose certificate includes the required
    endorsement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fire fireworks from a rooftop site, bridge site,
    flatbed site or floating platform site if they hold a fireworks
    operator certificate (display supervisor) that includes the required
    endorsement or they are under the direct supervision of a display
    supervisor in charge whose certificate includes the required
    endorsement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reuse of packaging --- user

**[[429.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-429.1 .sectionLabel}** A user must ensure
that any packaging or container that has been used for fireworks is not
reused unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transport classification of the fireworks to be
  transported remains unchanged;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fireworks to be transported are arranged in the
  packaging or container so as to minimize their movement during
  transport;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the packaging or container is closed for transport in
  the same manner as it was for its first use;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the mass of the fireworks is equal to or less than
  the mass of the fireworks during the first use of the packaging or
  container; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the packaging or container shows no signs of damage,
  contamination or reduced strength.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 135](#1451787-1453144){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451787-1453144.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION c]{.HLabel3}[Supervision of a Fireworks Display]{.HTitleText3} {#h-799334 .Subheading}

Definition of [[firing site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[430]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-430 .sectionLabel}** In this subdivision,
[[firing site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the area within a display site
where the fireworks are set up and fired.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display supervisor in charge

- **[[431]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-431
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who organizes a
  fireworks display must ensure that it is supervised by a display
  supervisor in charge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The display supervisor in charge must ensure that the
  display is carried out safely and that the requirements in sections
  432 to 438 are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plan

- **[[432]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-432 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fireworks display plan must be prepared in writing and kept for two
  years after the date of the display. The plan must include the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the display supervisor in charge and
    the number and expiry date of their fireworks operator certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the location of any storage units in which the
    fireworks to be displayed will be stored before or after the
    display;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the display site, including the
    distance in metres from the firing site to the nearest spectators,
    buildings, structures and vulnerable sites;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the type of fireworks to be used;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} the diameter in millimetres of the largest aerial
    shell to be used or, if no aerial shells are to be used, the maximum
    height the fireworks will reach during the display;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of fireworks to be used;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of how the fireworks will be
    positioned within the firing site and how they will be fired;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the crowd-control measures that
    will be taken; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an assessment of the likelihood of harm to people
    and property resulting from the use of the fireworks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The plan must be submitted to the local authority.
  The written approval of the local authority to hold the fireworks
  display must be obtained before the display takes place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety meetings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Meetings must be held with the people who will
  participate in presenting the fireworks display (for example, security
  guards and technicians) to inform them of the fireworks that will be
  used and the safety precautions to be taken during the display.
  Subsequent meetings must be held if the display is changed in a way
  that increases the likelihood of harm to people or property resulting
  from the use of the fireworks.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, s. 40
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400887){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400887.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 136(E)](#1451789-1453147){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451789-1453147.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-432-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 432"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fireworks to be attended

**[[433]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-433 .sectionLabel}** The fireworks must be
attended when they are not in a storage unit or a magazine.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400891){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400891.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-433-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 433"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loading zone

- **[[434]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-434 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  the fireworks are brought to the firing site, a loading zone must be
  established whose outer boundary is at least 30 m from the perimeter
  of the firing site. A smaller loading zone may be established only
  with the written approval of the local authority.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flammables

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The loading zone must not contain any flammables or
  other items that are likely to catch fire.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fallout zone

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before the continuity of the circuits is tested or,
  in the case of manually fired fireworks, before the first firework is
  fired, a fallout zone must be established that encompasses the area in
  which fireworks debris is likely to fall, taking into account the
  properties of the fireworks to be used, the angle from which they will
  be fired and the anticipated weather conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Only people authorized by the display supervisor in
  charge may enter or be in the loading zone or the fallout zone from
  the time any fireworks are brought into the zone until the supervisor
  in charge declares the zone to be free of explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Handling

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Only a person who holds a fireworks operator
  certificate (display assistant), fireworks operator certificate
  (display supervisor), fireworks operator certificate (display
  supervisor with endorsement) or fireworks operator certificate
  (display visitor) and is authorized by the display supervisor in
  charge may handle fireworks in the loading zone or fallout zone.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No smoking

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Smoking must be prohibited in the loading zone.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 35
- SOR/2018-231, s. 41
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400896){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400896.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 137](#1451791-1453158){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451791-1453158.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-434-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 434"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fire prevention and first aid

**[[435]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-435 .sectionLabel}** During the fireworks
display, fire prevention measures that minimize the possibility of harm
to people or property must be put in place and facilities, equipment and
personnel for fire fighting and administering first aid that minimize
the possibility of harm must be present at the site.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Firing procedures

- **[[436]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-436
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Fireworks must be positioned and
  aimed so that after firing they will not cross over or burst directly
  above the spectators and any debris from the fireworks will fall
  within the fallout zone.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Aerial shells

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When aerial shells are fired,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the mortars and mortar racks that are used to fire
    the shells must be robust, in sound condition and otherwise safe and
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mortars and mortar racks must be assembled,
    arranged and secured in a manner that minimizes the likelihood of
    harm to people and property if there is a premature explosion of a
    shell;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any support structures must be secured so that they
    do not fall over when a shell is fired; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} multi-break shells in racks, report shells and
    shells with a diameter of more than 155 mm must be fired with
    electric matches.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to firing unit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Only the display supervisor in charge, or a person
  designated by the display supervisor in charge, may have access to a
  firing unit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Physical keying device

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The display supervisor in charge, or a person
  designated by the display supervisor in charge, must have control of
  any physical keying device at all times.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety interlock

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} All firing units must be equipped with a safety
  interlock that has at least two steps.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extraneous electricity

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Precautions must be taken that minimize the
  likelihood of extraneous electricity igniting an electric match.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Firing system --- requirement

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If fireworks are electrically fired, the display
  supervisor in charge must ensure that the firing system is secured at
  all times to prevent accidental ignition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Damaged fireworks

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Fireworks that are damaged, leaking, damp or
  contaminated must not be used.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No firing

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Fireworks must not be fired if a circumstance occurs
  that could increase the likelihood of harm to people or property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Postponing or stopping display

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A fireworks display must be postponed or stopped if
  unfavourable weather conditions develop, a firework malfunctions or
  any other circumstance occurs that could increase the likelihood of
  harm to people or property.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 138](#1451796-1453162){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451796-1453162.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-436-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 436"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Firing unit disconnected

- **[[437]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-437 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  firing unit must be disconnected immediately after the fireworks
  display as well as during a pause in the display if keeping the unit
  connected could increase the likelihood of harm to people or property.
  When a unit is disconnected, any physical keying device must be
  removed and kept in the possession of the display supervisor in charge
  or a person designated by the display supervisor in charge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electrical firing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The firing site must not be approached until 30
  minutes after the display has ended if any fireworks used in the
  display were fired with an electric match.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manual firing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Misfired fireworks must not be approached until 30
  minutes after the display has ended if the fireworks used in the
  display were manually fired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Precautions must be taken that minimize the
  likelihood of harm to people and property from misfired fireworks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Search

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} As soon as the circumstances permit after the periods
  referred to in subsections (2) and (3), the fallout zone must be
  searched and all explosives must be removed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} After the display, only people designated to do a
  search by the display supervisor in charge may enter or be in the
  fallout zone until the display supervisor in charge declares the zone
  to be free of explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Second search

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The fallout zone must be searched a second time as
  soon as light and weather conditions permit if, at the time of the
  first search conducted under subsection (5) light and weather
  conditions were insufficient to ensure that all explosives were
  removed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 36(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400900){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400900.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 139](#1451798-1453165){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451798-1453165.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-437-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 437"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of use

**[[438]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-438 .sectionLabel}** A record of the display
must be made in a logbook that sets out the name of the display
supervisor in charge and the number and expiry date of their fireworks
operator certificate. The logbook must be kept for two years after the
date of the last recorded display. The record must include the following
information and documents:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the fireworks display plan prepared for the
  display;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the local authority's approval to hold the
  display;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of every person who worked at
  the display under the supervision of the display supervisor in charge;
  and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of any unusual circumstance, a
  statement of the number of misfires and a description of how each
  misfire was dealt with.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400904){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400904.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-438-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 438"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of licence holder

**[[439]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-439 .sectionLabel}** When a fireworks
display is held on behalf of a licence holder, the licence holder must
keep a record of the display for two years after the date of the
display. The record must include the following information and
documents:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder's name and address and the number
  and expiry date of the licence;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the local authority's approval to hold the
  display;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the display supervisor in charge and the
  number and expiry date of their fireworks operators certificate;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the product name and diameter of each firework used
  and the name of the person who obtained its authorization;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of fireworks used under each product
  name; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date and site of the display.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, ss. 44(F), 45(E)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400908){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400908.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-439-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 439"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Firecrackers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-799437 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION a]{.HLabel3}[Rules for Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-799438 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

**[[440]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-440 .sectionLabel}** A seller may sell
firecrackers to a buyer who provides the seller with a copy of their
firecracker use certificate. However, the quantity of firecrackers sold
must not exceed the quantity that the buyer is authorized by their
certificate to use.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

**[[441]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-441 .sectionLabel}** A seller must keep a
record of every sale of firecrackers for two years after the date of the
sale. The record must include the following information and documents:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name and address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the buyer's firecracker use certificate;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of firecrackers sold; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unused or misfired firecrackers

**[[442]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-442 .sectionLabel}** A seller must accept
any unused or misfired firecrackers that are returned.

#### [SUBDIVISION b]{.HLabel3}[Rules for Users]{.HTitleText3} {#h-799449 .Subheading}

##### [Firecracker Use Certificate]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799450 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

**[[443]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-443 .sectionLabel}** To obtain a firecracker
use certificate, a person must demonstrate to the Minister that they are
able to safely use firecrackers and that precautions will be taken that
minimize the likelihood of harm to people and property resulting from
their use.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 38
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-443-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 443"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for certificate

**[[444]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-444 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
firecracker use certificate must complete, sign and send to the Chief
Inspector of Explosives the application form provided by the Department
of Natural Resources. The application must include the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, date of birth, address and
  telephone number;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of their past experience using
  firecrackers;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of firecrackers that the applicant
  intends to use and the time, date and site of use;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the local authority's written approval to
  use the firecrackers; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety precautions that will be
  taken when using the firecrackers.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400912){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400912.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-444-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 444"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Acquisition and Storage]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799462 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[445]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-445 .sectionLabel}** A user may acquire,
store and use firecrackers, whether or not they hold a licence, if they
hold a firecracker use certificate. A user who acquires firecrackers
must comply with this subdivision.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- licensed user

- **[[446]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-446 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  user who holds a licence must store their firecrackers in the magazine
  specified in the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage --- unlicensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A user who does not hold a licence must store their
  firecrackers in a dwelling or a storage unit and ensure that the
  requirements of sections 447 and 448 are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400917){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400917.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-446-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 446"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity

**[[447]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-447 .sectionLabel}** No more than five cases
of firecrackers --- not exceeding 16 000 firecrackers per case --- may
be stored at any one time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- dwelling

- **[[448]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-448 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  firecrackers are stored in a dwelling, they must be stored away from
  flammable substances and sources of ignition, in a manner that
  protects the firecrackers against theft and ensures that access to
  them is limited to people authorized by the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements --- storage unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When firecrackers are stored in a storage unit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be located in a dry place,
    away from flammable substances and sources of ignition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be constructed and maintained
    to prevent unauthorized access and to protect the contents from
    weather;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is a container, it must not
    impede exit in case of fire;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the storage unit is not a container, all exits
    must be kept unobstructed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any shelving in the storage unit must be made from
    a non-sparking material (for example, wood or painted metal);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} nothing other than firecrackers may be stored in
    the storage unit;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be attended when it is
    unlocked;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the storage unit must be kept clean, dry, organized
    and free of grit;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any spill, leakage or other contamination in the
    storage unit must be cleaned up immediately;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} precautions that minimize the likelihood of fire in
    or near the storage unit must be taken; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a sign that displays the words "Danger --- Fire
    Hazard/[Risque d'incendie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in letters at
    least 10 cm high and that prohibits smoking using letters, or a
    symbol, at least 10 cm high must be posted on the storage unit in a
    clearly visible location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400922){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400922.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-448-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 448"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Use]{.HTitleText4} {#h-799488 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval required

- **[[449]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-449
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before using firecrackers, a user
  must obtain written approval from the local authority.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When using firecrackers, the user must take the
  following precautions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} non-flammable clothing and protective equipment
    that minimizes the likelihood of harm to the user must be worn; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least one fire extinguisher with a capacity of
    at least 3--A :60--B:C must be easily accessible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Misfired firecrackers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A user must remove any misfired firecrackers from the
  site of use as soon as the circumstances permit after the display.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Firecrackers to be returned

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A user must return all misfired and unused
  firecrackers to the seller as soon as the circumstances permit after
  the date of use specified in the firecracker certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400926){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400926.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-449-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 449"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 19]{.HLabel1}[Fees]{.HTitleText1} {#h-799500 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[450]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-450 .sectionLabel}** This Part sets out the
fees payable for obtaining authorizations, permits, licences and
certificates.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[451]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-451 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[distribution establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[distribution establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
    meaning as in section 144. ([établissement de
    distribution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[division 1 factory licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[division 1 factory licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
    meaning as in section 55. ([licence de fabrique de la section
    1]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[manufacturing certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manufacturing certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
    meaning as in section 106. ([certificat de
    fabrication]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[mobile process unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mobile process unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in section 56. ([unité de fabrication mobile]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[process unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[process unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    section 56. ([unité de fabrication]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[retail establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[retail establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in section 144. ([établissement de vente au détail]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[user magazine licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[user magazine licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
    as in section 144. ([licence de poudrière
    (utilisateur)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[user magazine zone licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[user magazine zone licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
    meaning as in section 144. ([licence de poudrière
    (utilisateur-zone)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vendor magazine licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vendor magazine licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
    as in section 144. ([licence de poudrière
    (vendeur)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}NEQ

**[[452]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[NEQ]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means net explosive quantity (the mass of
the explosive excluding the mass of any packaging or container).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 4](#1354349-1400930){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354349-1400930.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-452-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 452"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

- **[[453]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-453 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  fees to be paid for obtaining the authorizations, licences, permits
  and certificates set out in column 1 of the table to this section are
  set out in column 2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment deadline

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fees are payable at the time the application is
  submitted. However, the fees referred to in items 1 and 3 of the table
  are payable within 30 days after the date of the invoice from the
  Department of Natural Resources.
