## [PART 20]{.HLabel1}[Restricted Components]{.HTitleText1} {#h-799529 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overview

**[[454]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-454 .sectionLabel}** This Part prescribes
components of explosives for the purpose of the definition [[restricted
component]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the [Explosives
Act](/eng/acts/E-17), restricts the sale of those components and sets
out the requirements for their sale and storage.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399372){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399372.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-454-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 454"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[455]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-455 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[component seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[component seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who sells a
    restricted component. ([vendeur de composant]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[product seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[product seller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    manufactures a product, other than an explosive, for sale using a
    restricted component. ([vendeur de produit]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[sell]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sell]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes offer for
    sale. ([vendre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399379){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399379.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-455-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 455"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Chief Inspector's delegate

**[[456]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-456 .sectionLabel}** The duties and
functions of the Chief Inspector of Explosives that are set out in
subsections 467(1) and 468(1), section 488, subsections 489(1), 500(1)
and 501(1) and sections 514 and 515 may be performed by an inspector
designated by the Chief Inspector.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399384){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399384.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-456-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 456"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Components]{.HTitleText2} {#h-799558 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed components

**[[457]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-457 .sectionLabel}** The components set out
in Tables 1 to 3 to this Part are prescribed for the purpose of the
definition [[restricted component]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2
of the [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399389){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399389.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-457-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 457"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Tier 1 Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1399390 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition

**[[458]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-458 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[Tier 1 component]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a restricted component
set out in column 1 of Table 1 to this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399394){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399394.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-458-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 458"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Authorized Sale and Acquisition]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399395 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

**[[459]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459 .sectionLabel}** Only a person who is
authorized by this Division may sell a Tier 1 component.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399399){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399399.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-459-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 459"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[460]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-460 .sectionLabel}** A Tier 1 component may
be acquired for the purpose of manufacturing products, other than
explosives, for sale only by a person who is authorized by this Division
to acquire a component for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399404){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399404.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-460-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 460"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- use in laboratories

- **[[461]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-461 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may sell a Tier 1 component for use in a laboratory that is
  part of or affiliated with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a post-secondary educational institution recognized
    by a province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a hospital or health clinic; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a government or law enforcement agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- component seller

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A component seller may sell a Tier 1 component. The
  component seller must comply with this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, s. 44(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399413){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399413.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-461-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 461"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition --- product seller

**[[462]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-462 .sectionLabel}** A product seller may
acquire a Tier 1 component for the purpose of manufacturing products for
sale. The product seller must comply with this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399417){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399417.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-462-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 462"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Component Sellers and Product Sellers List]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399418 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Component sellers list

**[[463]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-463 .sectionLabel}** Only a component seller
who is on the component sellers list referred to in subsection 467(1) is
authorized to sell a Tier 1 component.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399422){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399422.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-463-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 463"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- component seller

- **[[464]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-464 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  component seller must complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector
  of Explosives the application form provided by the Department of
  Natural Resources. The application must include the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the component seller's name, address, telephone
    number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Tier 1 components to be sold;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the address of each location where a Tier 1
    component will be stored or sold and the storage capacity or
    anticipated annual sales volume, as the case may be, for each
    component at each location; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and email
    address of a contact person for each location where a Tier 1
    component will be stored or sold.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must also include a declaration that
  a security plan has been prepared for each location where the Tier 1
  component will be stored or sold. The plan must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the emergency procedures to be
    followed in responding to all risk events, including
    security-related events, and the title of the person who will be
    responsible for ensuring that each procedure is followed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures to be taken to
    control access to the component, including control of keys;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures to be taken to
    control access to sales records;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the stock management system to be
    implemented and the title of the person who will be responsible for
    carrying out weekly inspections of the stock; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures to be taken to ensure
    that the sale of the component will be refused if the quantity
    requested is not proportional to the buyer's needs or if the
    component seller or their employee has reasonable grounds to suspect
    that the component will be used for a criminal purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399438){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399438.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 140](#1451800-1453168){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451800-1453168.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-464-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 464"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Product sellers list

**[[465]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-465 .sectionLabel}** Only a product seller
who is on the product sellers list referred to in subsection 468(1) is
authorized to manufacture a product, other than an explosive, for sale
using a Tier 1 component.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399444){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399444.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-465-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 465"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- product seller

- **[[466]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-466 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  product seller must complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector of
  Explosives the application form provided by the Department of Natural
  Resources. The application must include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the product seller's name, address, telephone
    number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the product name of products that will be
    manufactured using a Tier 1 component and that will be sold;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a list of the Tier 1 components that will be used
    in manufacturing the products;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of each location where a Tier 1
    component will be stored and the storage capacity for each component
    at each location; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and email
    address of a contact person for each location where a Tier 1
    component will be stored.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must also include a declaration that
  a security plan has been prepared for each location where the Tier 1
  component will be stored. The plan must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the emergency procedures to be
    followed in responding to all risk events, including
    security-related events, and the title of the person who will be
    responsible for ensuring that each procedure is followed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures to be taken to
    control access to the component, including control of keys; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the stock management system to be
    implemented and the title of the person who will be responsible for
    carrying out weekly inspections of the stock.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399459){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399459.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 141](#1451805-1453171){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451805-1453171.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-466-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 466"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Listing of component seller

- **[[467]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-467 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  component seller provides the information required by section 464, the
  Chief Inspector of Explosives must include their name on the component
  sellers list, assign them a number and provide them with a document
  that certifies the number and effective date of listing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of listing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A listing is effective for five years after the date
  that is set out in the document.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399466){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399466.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-467-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 467"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Listing of product seller

- **[[468]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-468 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  product seller provides the information required by section 466, the
  Chief Inspector of Explosives must include their name on the product
  sellers list, assign them a number and provide them with a document
  that sets out the number and effective date of listing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of listing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A listing is effective for five years after the date
  that is set out in the document.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399473){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399473.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-468-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 468"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change

**[[469]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-469 .sectionLabel}** Every component seller
and product seller must provide the Chief Inspector of Explosives with a
written notice of any change to the information provided in an
application within 10 days after the date of the change.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399477){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399477.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 142(F)](#1451810-1453172){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451810-1453172.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-469-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 469"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Rules for Component Sellers and Product Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399478 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities of component sellers and
product sellers

**[[470]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-470 .sectionLabel}** Every component seller
must ensure that the requirements set out in sections 471 to 487 are met
at each location where they store or sell a Tier 1 component. Every
product seller must ensure that the requirements set out in sections 471
to 480 are met at each location where they store a Tier 1 component.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399482){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399482.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-470-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 470"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized location

**[[471]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-471 .sectionLabel}** A Tier 1 component can
only be stored at or sold from a location that has been disclosed in an
application or in the notice of change referred to in section 469.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399486){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399486.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-471-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 471"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

**[[472]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-472 .sectionLabel}** The local police force
must be informed in writing of all locations where a Tier 1 component is
to be stored or sold.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399490){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399490.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-472-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 472"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Locked structures

- **[[473]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-473 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  structure that contains a Tier 1 component and every door, window or
  other point of access to a building in which the component is stored
  must be locked when it is not attended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Key control plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A key control plan must be prepared in writing and
  implemented.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lighting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} All main entrances to a building in which a Tier 1
  component is stored must be lit at all times outside business hours.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399499){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399499.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-473-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 473"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security plan

**[[474]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-474 .sectionLabel}** The security plan of a
component seller or product seller must be implemented and must be
updated every 12 months.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399503){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399503.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-474-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 474"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sign

- **[[475]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-475 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  sign that warns against unauthorized access must be posted on the
  outside at each entrance to each location where a Tier 1 component is
  stored.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Access to a Tier 1 component must be limited to
  people authorized by the component seller or product seller.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, s. 44(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399509){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399509.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-475-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 475"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employee list

**[[476]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-476 .sectionLabel}** A list of the employees
who work at each location where a Tier 1 component is stored or sold
must be kept at that location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399513){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399513.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-476-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 476"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification

**[[477]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-477 .sectionLabel}** When a Tier 1 component
is received,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the component that is received must
  be compared with the quantity that is recorded on the bill of lading;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any signs of tampering with the vehicle or rail car
  in which the component is shipped and any signs of attempted theft of
  that component must be recorded, and the record must be kept for two
  years after the date on which it is made;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person from whom the component was bought must be
  informed of any signs of tampering or attempted theft and any loss
  that is not attributable to normal operations; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the cause of any loss of the component that is not
  attributable to normal operations must be recorded, and the record
  must be kept for two years after the date on which it is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399522){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399522.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-477-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 477"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stock management

- **[[478]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-478 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  stock management system must be put in place to account for a Tier 1
  component that is under the control of the component seller or product
  seller.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Audit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An annual inventory audit of the component must be
  performed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weekly inspections

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Weekly inspections of the component must be carried
  out. A record of the results of each inspection, including any loss or
  tampering and the cause of any loss that is not attributable to normal
  operations, must be kept for two years after the record is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399531){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399531.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-478-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 478"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual inventory

**[[479]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-479 .sectionLabel}** For each calendar year,
an inventory must be made and a record of the following information kept
for two years:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the listing number of the component seller or product
  seller;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a record of the Tier 1 component that sets out, for
  each location where that component is stored or sold,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the starting inventory,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the component that was
    manufactured,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the component that was acquired
    and the manner of acquisition,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the component that was used, sold,
    exported, destroyed, stolen or lost, as the case may be,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the year-end inventory, and

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the historical normal range of loss that is due to
    loss of water or mechanical abrasion; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and email address
  of the person who made the inventory.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399544){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399544.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 143](#1451812-1453175){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451812-1453175.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-479-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 479"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Theft or tampering

**[[480]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-480 .sectionLabel}** If any theft or
attempted theft of, or tampering with, a Tier 1 component is discovered,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the local police force must be informed immediately;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a written report of the incident must be submitted to
  the Chief Inspector of Explosives within 24 hours after the discovery.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399550){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399550.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-480-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 480"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No sale

- **[[481]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-481 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  sale of a Tier 1 component must be refused if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity requested is not proportional to the
    buyer's needs; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the component seller or their employee has
    reasonable grounds to suspect that the component will be used for a
    criminal purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every refusal to sell the component under
  subsection (1) or section 487 must be reported to the Chief Inspector
  of Explosives and to the local police force within 24 hours after the
  refusal.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399559){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399559.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-481-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 481"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification

**[[482]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-482 .sectionLabel}** Before a Tier 1
component is sold, the buyer must be required to establish their
identity by providing

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the buyer intends to use the component to
  manufacture an explosive and a licence or certificate is required for
  that purpose, the number of the buyer's licence or certificate;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the buyer intends to sell the component, proof
  that the buyer is included on the component sellers list; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in all other cases,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a piece of identification, issued by the Government
    of Canada or a provincial, municipal or foreign government, that
    bears a photograph of the buyer,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} two pieces of identification, each of which sets
    out the buyer's name, at least one of which is issued by the
    Government of Canada or a provincial, municipal or foreign
    government and at least one of which sets out the buyer's address,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's provincial pesticide licence,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} proof that registration of an agricultural
    operation with Québec's Ministère de l'Agriculture, des Pêcheries et
    de l'Alimentation is assigned to the buyer,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} proof of the buyer's Ontario Federation of
    Agriculture number, or

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} proof of the buyer's registration under the
    [Controlled Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-32).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399572){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399572.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-482-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 482"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Intermediary

**[[483]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-483 .sectionLabel}** A Tier 1 component may
be sold to a buyer who is unable to establish their identity in
accordance with section 482 if another component seller confirms in
writing that they have been provided with the identification required
for that buyer. The confirmation must set out the type of document
provided to the other component seller and its reference number.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399576){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399576.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-483-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 483"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

- **[[484]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-484 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  record of each sale of a Tier 1 component must be kept for two years
  after the date of the sale. The record must include the following
  information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name, address and telephone number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the bill of lading, sales receipt or similar
    document;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the type of document provided under section 482 and
    the document's reference number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the product name of the component sold;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the component sold under each
    product name;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the component was sold in
    bulk or in packages;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the component was sold in packages, the weight
    or volume of each package;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of how the component will be used;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} if the component was shipped, the driver's licence
    number, the estimated and actual date of delivery, the address to
    which it is delivered and the quantity received; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} if delivery was made at the time of purchase, a
    receipt signed by the buyer including the information required under
    paragraphs (a), (b) and (d) to (i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual sales contract

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a component seller who has entered
  into an annual sales contract with a buyer, the information required
  under paragraphs (1)(a)(d) and (i) need only be recorded once in each
  calendar year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The record of sale must be kept locked up when it is
  not being used and must be made available only to a person who needs
  access to it in the course of their employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (3) only apply if the quantity of
  a Tier 1 component sold is more than 1 kg.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399598){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399598.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-484-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 484"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shipping --- vehicle

- **[[485]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-485 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  more than 1 kg of a Tier 1 component is shipped by vehicle,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each access point on the portion of the vehicle
    containing the component must be locked or sealed with a security
    cable immediately after the shipment is loaded; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the driver of the vehicle must be provided with a
    written notice stating that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the component is to be attended unless the
      vehicle is either parked in a secure location or the vehicle and
      load are locked up,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the driver is to inspect all locks and, if seals
      are present, inspect all seals at each stop and at the final
      destination, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the driver must immediately report to the
      component seller any signs of theft, attempted theft or tampering
      and any loss that is not attributable to normal operations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shipping --- rail

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a Tier 1 component is shipped by rail,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each access point on the rail car containing the
    component must be locked or sealed with a security cable immediately
    after the shipment is loaded; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a means must be in place to track the shipment on a
    daily basis until delivery occurs and to investigate if the shipment
    does not arrive at its destination.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399612){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399612.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-485-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 485"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

**[[486]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-486 .sectionLabel}** When a Tier 1 component
is sold to a buyer who is not a component seller or product seller, the
buyer must be provided with a written notice stating that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} security measures are to be taken to prevent the
  theft of the component;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any sign of theft, attempted theft or tampering and
  any loss that is not attributable to normal operations must be
  immediately reported to the local police force; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the resale of the component is prohibited.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399619){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399619.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-486-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 486"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility of employee

**[[487]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-487 .sectionLabel}** An employee of a
component seller must refuse to sell a Tier 1 component if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity requested is not proportional to the
  buyer's needs; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employee has reasonable grounds to suspect that
  the component will be used for a criminal purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399625){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399625.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-487-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 487"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Suspension and Removal]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399626 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

- **[[488]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-488 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  component seller or product seller fails to comply with the
  [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17) or these Regulations, the Chief
  Inspector of Explosives may suspend them from the component sellers
  list or product sellers list. The suspension continues until the
  component seller or product seller remedies the failure to comply.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a component seller or product seller fails to
  comply with the [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17) or these Regulations
  on more than one occasion, the Chief Inspector of Explosives may
  remove them from the component sellers list or product sellers list.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399633){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399633.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-488-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 488"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to be heard

- **[[489]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-489
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before suspending or removing a
  component seller or product seller from the component sellers list or
  product sellers list, the Chief Inspector of Explosives must provide
  them with written notice of the reasons for the suspension or removal
  and its effective date, and give them an opportunity to provide
  reasons why the listing should not be suspended or removed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A component seller or a product seller is suspended
  automatically and without notice if they fail to submit the annual
  inventory required under section 479.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399640){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399640.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-489-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 489"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

- **[[490]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-490
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within 15 days after the date of a
  component seller's or product seller's suspension or removal from the
  component sellers list or product sellers list, they may send the
  Minister a written request for a review of the decision of the Chief
  Inspector of Explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must confirm, revoke or amend the
  decision under review.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399647){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399647.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-490-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 490"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Tier 2 Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1399648 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition

**[[491]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-491 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[Tier 2 component]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a restricted component
set out in column 1 of Table 2 to this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399652){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399652.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-491-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 491"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Authorized Sale and Acquisition]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399653 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

**[[492]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-492 .sectionLabel}** Only a person who is
authorized by this Division may sell a Tier 2 component.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, s. 39
- SOR/2018-231, s. 44(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399656){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399656.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-492-20181102.html "Link to previous version of section 492"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

**[[493]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-493 .sectionLabel}** A Tier 2 component may
be acquired for the purpose of manufacturing products, other than
explosives, for sale only by a person who is authorized by this Division
to acquire a component for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399660){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399660.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-493-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 493"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- use in laboratories

- **[[494]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-494 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may sell a Tier 2 component for use in a laboratory that is
  part of or affiliated with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a post-secondary educational institution recognized
    by a province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a hospital or health clinic; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a government or law enforcement agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- component seller

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A component seller may sell a Tier 2 component. The
  component seller must comply with this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399670){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399670.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-494-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 494"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition --- product seller

- **[[495]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-495 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  product seller may acquire a Tier 2 component for the purpose of
  manufacturing products for sale. The product seller must comply with
  this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The requirements set out in this Division do not
  apply to a product seller who acquires or manufactures a restricted
  component set out at item 10 or 11 of Table 2 to this Part for the
  purpose of manufacturing a product for sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399677){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399677.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-495-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 495"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Component Sellers and Product Sellers List]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399678 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Component sellers list

**[[496]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-496 .sectionLabel}** Only a component seller
who is on the component sellers list referred to in subsection 500(1) is
authorized to sell a Tier 2 component.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399682){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399682.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-496-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 496"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- component seller

**[[497]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-497 .sectionLabel}** The component seller
must complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector of Explosives the
application form provided by the Department of Natural Resources. The
application must include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the component seller's name, address, telephone
  number, fax number and email address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the components to be sold;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the address of each location where a component will
  be stored or sold and the storage capacity or anticipated annual sales
  volume, as the case may be, for each component at each location; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number, fax number and
  email address of a contact person for each location where a component
  will be stored or sold.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399690){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399690.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-497-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 497"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Product sellers list

**[[498]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-498 .sectionLabel}** Only a product seller
who is on the product sellers list referred to in subsection 501(1) is
authorized to manufacture a product, other than an explosive, for sale
using a Tier 2 component.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-75, ss. 38, 44(F)
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399693){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399693.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-498-20160415.html "Link to previous version of section 498"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- product seller

**[[499]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-499 .sectionLabel}** The product seller must
complete, sign and send to the Chief Inspector of Explosives the
application form provided by the Department of Natural Resources. The
application must include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the product seller's name, address, telephone number
  and email address;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the product name of products that will be
  manufactured using a Tier 2 component and that will be sold;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a list of the Tier 2 components that will be used in
  manufacturing the products;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of each location where a Tier 2 component
  will be stored and the storage capacity for each component at each
  location; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and email address
  of a contact person for each location where a Tier 2 component will be
  stored.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399704){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399704.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-77, s. 144](#1451817-1453178){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1451817-1453178.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-499-20230603.html "Link to previous version of section 499"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Listing of component seller

- **[[500]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-500 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  component seller provides the information required by section 497, the
  Chief Inspector of Explosives must include their name on the component
  sellers list, assign them a number and provide them with a document
  that certifies the number and effective date of listing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of listing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A listing is effective for five years after the date
  that is set out in the document.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399711){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399711.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-500-20150201.html "Link to previous version of section 500"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Listing of product seller

- **[[501]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-501 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  product seller provides the information required by section 499, the
  Chief Inspector of Explosives must include their name on the product
  sellers list, assign them a number and provide them with a document
  that sets out the number and effective date of listing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of listing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A listing is effective for five years after the date
  that is set out in the document.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399718){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399718.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-501-20150201.html "Link to previous version of section 501"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change

**[[502]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-502 .sectionLabel}** Every component seller
and product seller must provide the Chief Inspector of Explosives with a
written notice of any change to the information provided in an
application within 10 days after the date of the change.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399722){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399722.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-502-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 502"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Rules for Component Sellers and Product Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399723 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities of component sellers and
product sellers

**[[503]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-503 .sectionLabel}** Every component seller
must ensure that the requirements set out in sections 504 to 513 are met
at each location where they store or sell a Tier 2 component. Every
product seller must ensure that the requirements set out in sections 504
to 508 are met at each location where they store a Tier 2 component.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399727){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399727.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-503-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 503"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized location

**[[504]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504 .sectionLabel}** A Tier 2 component can
only be stored at or sold from a location that has been disclosed in an
application or in the notice of change referred to in section 502.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399731){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399731.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-504-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 504"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Locked component

- **[[505]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-505 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  Tier 2 component must be locked up when it is not attended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sign

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A sign that warns against unauthorized access must be
  posted on the outside at each entrance to each location where a Tier 2
  component is stored.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Access to a Tier 2 component must be limited to
  people authorized by the component seller or product seller.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399740){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399740.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-505-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 505"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employee list

**[[506]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-506 .sectionLabel}** A list of the employees
who work at each location where a Tier 2 component is stored or sold
must be kept at that location.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399744){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399744.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-506-20150201.html "Link to previous version of section 506"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stock management

- **[[507]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-507 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  stock management system must be put in place to account for a Tier 2
  component that is under the control of the component seller or product
  seller.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weekly inspections

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Weekly inspections of the component must be carried
  out. A record of the results of each inspection, including any loss or
  tampering and the cause of any loss that is not attributable to normal
  operations, must be kept for two years after the date on which the
  record is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399751){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399751.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-507-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 507"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Theft or tampering

**[[508]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-508 .sectionLabel}** If any theft or
attempted theft of, or any tampering with, a Tier 2 component is
discovered,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the local police force must be informed immediately;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Chief Inspector of Explosives must be informed
  within 24 hours after the discovery; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a written report of the incident must be submitted to
  the Chief Inspector of Explosives as soon as the circumstances permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399758){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399758.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-508-20160201.html "Link to previous version of section 508"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No sale

- **[[509]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-509 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  sale of a Tier 2 component must be refused if the component seller has
  reasonable grounds to suspect that the component will be used for a
  criminal purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every refusal to sell the component under
  subsection (1) or section 513 must be reported to the Chief Inspector
  of Explosives and to the local police force within 24 hours after the
  refusal.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399767){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399767.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-509-20140201.html "Link to previous version of section 509"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification

**[[510]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-510 .sectionLabel}** Before a Tier 2
component is sold, the buyer must be required to establish their
identity by providing

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the buyer intends to use the component to
  manufacture an explosive and a licence or certificate is required for
  this purpose, the number of the buyer's licence or certificate;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the buyer intends to sell the component, proof
  that the buyer is included on the component sellers list; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in all other cases,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a piece of identification, issued by the Government
    of Canada or a provincial, municipal or foreign government, that
    bears a photograph of the buyer,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} two pieces of identification, each of which sets
    out the buyer's name, at least one of which is issued by the
    Government of Canada or a provincial, municipal or foreign
    government and at least one of which sets out the buyer's address,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's provincial pesticide licence,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} proof that registration of an agricultural
    operation with Québec's Ministère de l'Agriculture, des Pêcheries et
    de l'Alimentation is assigned to the buyer,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} proof of the buyer's Ontario Federation of
    Agriculture number,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's business licence or proof of the
    buyer's corporate registration, or

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} proof of the buyer's registration under the
    [Controlled Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-32).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399781){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399781.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-510-20131127.html "Link to previous version of section 510"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Intermediary

**[[511]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-511 .sectionLabel}** A Tier 2 component may
be sold to a buyer who is unable to establish their identity in
accordance with section 510 if another component seller confirms in
writing that they have been provided with the identification required
for that buyer. The confirmation must set out the type of document
provided to the other component seller and its reference number.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399785){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399785.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sale

- **[[512]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-512 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  record of each sale of a Tier 2 component must be kept for two years
  after the date of the sale. The record must include the following
  information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buyer's name, address and telephone number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of the sale;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the bill of lading, sales receipt or similar
    document;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the type of document provided under section 510 and
    the document's reference number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the product name of the component sold;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the component sold under each
    product name;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the component was sold in
    bulk or in packages;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the component was sold in packages, the weight
    or volume of each package;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of how the component will be used;
    and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} if the component was shipped, the date of reception
    and the quantity received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual sales contract

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a component seller who has entered
  into an annual sales contract with a buyer, the information required
  under paragraphs (1)(a)(d) and (i) need only be recorded once in each
  calendar year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The record of sale must be kept locked up when it is
  not being used and must be made available only to a person who needs
  access to it in the course of their employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (3) do not apply to the sale of a
  Tier 2 component set out in column 1 of Table 2 to this Part if the
  quantity sold is no more than the quantity set out for that component
  in column 2 of Table 2 to this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399806){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399806.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility of employee

**[[513]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-513 .sectionLabel}** An employee of a
component seller must not sell a Tier 2 component if they have
reasonable grounds to suspect that the component will be used for a
criminal purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399810){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399810.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Suspension and Removal]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399811 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

- **[[514]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-514 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  component seller or product seller fails to comply with the
  [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17) or these Regulations, the Chief
  Inspector of Explosives may suspend them from the component sellers
  list or product sellers list. The suspension continues until the
  component seller or product seller remedies the failure to comply.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a component seller or product seller fails to
  comply with the [Explosives Act](/eng/acts/E-17) or these Regulations
  on more than one occasion, the Chief Inspector of Explosives may
  remove them from the component sellers list or product sellers list.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399836){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399836.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to be heard

**[[515]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-515 .sectionLabel}** Before suspending or
removing a component seller or product seller from the component sellers
list or product sellers list, the Chief Inspector of Explosives must
provide them with written notice of the reasons for the suspension or
removal and its effective date, and give them an opportunity to provide
reasons why the listing should not be suspended or removed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399840){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399840.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

- **[[516]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-516
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within 15 days after the date of a
  component seller's or product seller's suspension or removal from the
  component sellers list or product sellers list, a component seller or
  product seller may send the Minister a written request for a review of
  the decision of the Chief Inspector of Explosives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must confirm, revoke or amend the
  decision under review.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399847){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399847.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Tier 3 Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1399848 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition

**[[517]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-517 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[Tier 3 component]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a restricted component
set out in column 1 of Table 3 to this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399861){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399861.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Authorized Sale]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399862 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

**[[518]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-518 .sectionLabel}** A component seller may
sell a Tier 3 component. The component seller must comply with this
Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399866){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399866.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Rules for Component Sellers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1399867 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Theft or tampering

**[[519]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-519 .sectionLabel}** If any theft or
attempted theft of, or any tampering with, a Tier 3 component is
discovered,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the local police force must be informed immediately;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Chief Inspector of Explosives must be informed
  within 24 hours after the discovery; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a written report of the incident must be submitted to
  the Chief Inspector of Explosives as soon as the circumstances permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399874){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399874.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No sale

- **[[520]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-520 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  sale of a Tier 3 component must be refused if the component seller has
  reasonable grounds to suspect that the component will be used for a
  criminal purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every refusal to sell the component under
  subsection (1) or section 521 must be reported to the Chief Inspector
  of Explosives and to the local police force within 24 hours after the
  refusal.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399881){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399881.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility of employee

**[[521]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-521 .sectionLabel}** An employee of a
component seller must not sell a Tier 3 component if they have
reasonable grounds to suspect that the component will be used for a
criminal purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-121, s. 3](#1354347-1399885){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1354347-1399885.html"
  role="button"}
:::
